Method: 使用五个开权重Transformer模型，构建匹配的有效-无效前提-结论对，覆盖多种推理族、语义域、模板和难度水平。通过线性探针（probing）从隐藏状态解码逻辑有效性，进行跨模板/跨域/跨推理族的泛化测试，在行为错误样本上进行正确性条件化评估，并通过穷举留一法（leave-one-out）测试和基于探针方向表征的干预实验检验表征的边界与因果作用。

Result: （1）尽管模型行为表现接近随机水平，逻辑有效性几乎可以近乎完美地从隐藏状态中解码；（2）在未见模板、领域和推理族上，有效性解码依然很强；（3）在行为错误的样本上（条件化评估可行的情况下），有效性仍然高度可解码；（4）穷举留一法测试揭示了该泛化的明确极限；（5）沿探针导出的有效性方向进行干预的效果相较于随机对照是微弱且非特异的。

Conclusion: 模型的隐藏状态中强有力地表征了逻辑有效性信息，但这种表征既未可靠地转化为行为输出，也未在因果层面被有效利用。这表明"表征有效性"、"行为表达有效性"和"因果使用有效性"是三个相互独立的维度，语言模型可以"知道"逻辑有效性但无法可靠地"运用"它。

Abstract: Large language models can look capable of logical reasoning, but correct or incorrect answers alone tell us little about what the model represents internally. We study logical verification in five open-weight transformer models using matched valid--invalid premise--claim pairs that vary across inference families, semantic domains, templates, and difficulty levels. Despite near-chance behavioral performance, logical validity is often almost perfectly decodable from hidden states and remains strongly decodable under held-out templates, domains, and inference families. Validity also remains highly decodable on behaviorally incorrect examples in the conditions where correctness-conditioned evaluation is well defined. At the same time, exhaustive leave-one-out tests reveal clear limits to this generalization, and interventions along probe-derived validity directions have only weak, nonspecific effects compared with random controls. Our results suggest that representing validity, expressing it in behavior, and using it causally are distinct. Validity related information can be strongly decodable from a model's hidden states without being reliably expressed in its output.

</details>


### [159] [Learning to Fuse LLMs with Ontology Rankers for Rare-Disease Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02473)
*Zhaoyang Jiang,Zhizhong Fu,Yunsoo Kim,Zicheng Li,Xuanqi Peng,Fei Teng,Jiacong Mi,Honghan Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于行为的融合模型，将本体论排序器（如Phenomizer）与大语言模型的差分诊断能力结合，无需放弃本体论排序器的结构化证据。该方法先消除基准测试集与本体注释共同来源导致的泄漏路径，再通过分析两个排序列表、一致性和本体支持度学习逐例的最优信任分配。融合模型在八个开源LLM上与Phenomizer结合，使Phenomizer Recall@1在Phenopacket Store上提升7.86个百分点、在RAMEDIS上提升20.18个百分点；与DeepSeek-V4-Flash通过API配合时，仅用其他LLM训练的融合模型仍能获得5.19个百分点的提升且无需重训练。90.8%的正确融合诊断保留可检查的候选级本体证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 罕见病诊断中，本体论排序器因每条候选诊断可追溯到匹配的患者表型而有优势，但LLM虽能生成差分诊断却缺乏清晰的证据链。作者不主张系统间替代，而是探究LLM能否在不放弃既有结构化证据的前提下改进排序器的诊断性能。

Method: 提出基于行为的融合模型：输入两个排序列表（本体排序器和LLM输出），建模二者的协议度与每位候选的本体支持度，学习针对个体病例对每个系统的依赖权重。研究特别先移除了基准案例与本体注释同源于同一出版物所导致的测试集泄漏路径。采用八个开源LLM进行评估，并对DeepSeek-V4-Flash进行API跨模型泛化测试。

Result: 融合模型显著提升Recall@1：Phenopacket Store上提升7.86个百分点，RAMEDIS上提升20.18个百分点；使用DeepSeek-V4-Flash API时，无需重训练即从0.1657提升至0.2176（+5.19点）。90.8%的正确融合诊断保留了可检查的候选级本体证据。

Conclusion: LLM能够强化既有的罕见病诊断工具，而无需摒弃使其有用的结构化证据链，证明了两种诊断范式之间互补融合的可行性与价值。

Abstract: Ontology rankers remain useful for rare-disease diagnosis because each candidate can be traced to matched patient phenotypes. Large language models (LLMs) can generate differential diagnoses from the same patient description, but their predictions lack an equally clear evidence trail. Rather than asking which system should replace the other, we ask whether an LLM can improve the ranker without giving up its evidence. Our behavior-based fusion model examines the two ranked lists, their agreement, and the ontology support behind each candidate, and learns how much to rely on each system for the individual case. Before comparison, we remove a documented test-set leakage pathway caused by benchmark cases and ontology annotations being derived from the same publications. Across eight open LLMs, fusion improves Phenomizer Recall@1 by 7.86 percentage points on Phenopacket Store and 20.18 points on RAMEDIS. When paired with DeepSeek-V4-Flash through an API, a fusion model trained only on the other LLMs improves Recall@1 from 0.1657 to 0.2176, a 5.19-point gain, without retraining. For 90.8% of correct fused diagnoses, the disease retains candidate-level ontology evidence that can be inspected. These results show that LLMs can strengthen an established diagnostic tool without discarding the structured evidence that makes it useful.

</details>


### [160] [PragAlign: Feedback-Guided Pragmatic Alignment for Controlled Synthetic Dialogue Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02480)
*Smitha Muthya Sudheendra,Jaideep Srivastava*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PragAlign是一个反馈引导的受控合成对话生成框架,采用生成-评估-修订循环,在800个匹配的对话规格上达到99.5%的评估者定义接受率,显著优于一次性生成的72.25%;但人工评估表明情感适度性仍是开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在隐私受限的服务场景中,合成对话生成可支持相关研究,但生成的对话必须保持交际意图、情感意义和自然的对话流程。现有方法难以在多重约束下同时保证这些质量维度,需要一种可控的框架来提升对话生成的约束满足度。

Method: PragAlign采用基于LLM的生成-评估-修订循环:以服务上下文、目标意图和目标情感为条件生成对话,附加辅助特征风格控制;LLM评估器对意图对齐、情感对齐、连贯性、流畅性和整体质量进行评分,并提供针对各标准的具体反馈,支持最多三轮细化修订。

Result: 在800个匹配对话规格上,PragAlign达到99.5%的评估者定义接受率,对比一次性生成的72.25%和无结构化反馈的重复生成的95.88%。细化增益集中在情感对齐上,这也成为消融实验中主要的失败模式。人类评估1200个对话显示,意图表达和对话流程高度可辨识,而情感适切性较不稳定且更具主观性。

Conclusion: PragAlign可作为提升评估者定义交际约束满足度的质量控制框架,但研究同时表明:重复尝试贡献了大部分效果提升,结构化反馈主要改善最终阶段的多样性约束满足;情感实现和独立人类感知质量仍是未来需要解决的开放挑战。

Abstract: Synthetic dialogue generation can support research in privacy-restricted service settings, but generated conversations must preserve communicative intent, affective meaning, and natural dialogue flow. We introduce PragAlign, a feedback-guided framework for controlled synthetic dialogue generation conditioned on service context, target intent, and target emotion, with auxiliary trait-style controls. PragAlign uses a generate--evaluate--revise loop in which an LLM-based evaluator scores intent alignment, emotion alignment, coherence, fluency, and aggregate quality, then provides criterion-specific feedback for up to three refinement rounds. On 800 matched dialogue specifications, PragAlign achieves 99.50\% evaluator-defined acceptance, compared with 72.25\% for one-shot generation and 95.88\% for repeated generation without structured feedback. This indicates that repeated attempts account for much of the gain over one-shot generation, while structured feedback primarily improves last-mile multi-constraint satisfaction rather than broad average quality. Refinement gains are concentrated in emotion alignment, which is also the dominant failure mode in ablations. A separate human evaluation of 1,200 generated dialogues shows that intent expression and dialogue flow are highly recognizable to annotators, while emotion appropriateness is less stable and more subjective. These results support PragAlign as a quality-control framework for improving evaluator-defined communicative constraint satisfaction, while showing that affective realization and independent human-perceived quality remain open challenges.

</details>


### [161] [How LLMs Build Fictional Worlds: Setting and Narrative Space in AI-Generated Creative Storytelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02482)
*Katrin Rohrbacher,Björn Nieth,Emmanuelle Salin,Bjoern Eskofier,Michaela Mahlberg*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文分析了大型语言模型（LLMs）在虚构故事中使用的世界构建策略，重点研究"设定"（setting）维度。通过对比GPT 4.1、LlaMA 3.3、Mistral 3.2和Gemma 3生成的1000篇英德双语故事与Project Gutenberg中的人类作者小说，利用微调的BERT分类器识别五种叙事空间类型（动作空间、感知空间、视觉空间、描述空间和无空间），发现人类文本主要使用"动作空间"，而LLMs系统性地过度生成"感知空间"，这种差异在叙事时间上保持稳定，且具有模型特异性和语言敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解LLMs如何构建虚构世界（worldbuilding），特别是通过可量化的"设定"维度来揭示AI生成叙事与人类叙事之间的系统性差异，从而深化对LLM叙事能力与局限性的认识。

Method: 使用微调的BERT分类器（针对英语和德语）识别五种叙事空间类型；让四种LLM（GPT 4.1、LlaMA 3.3、Mistral 3.2、Gemma 3）在英语和德语中分别生成1000篇故事；将生成文本的空间分布与Project Gutenberg中人类作者小说构成的基线进行对比分析。

Result: 人类作者文本主要采用"动作空间"，通过具身化的角色-环境互动来锚定叙事；而LLMs系统性地过度生成"感知空间"，强调氛围和情感。这种差异在叙事时间进程中保持稳定，且不同模型和不同语言之间表现出模式差异。

Conclusion: LLMs展现出与人类作者小说一致不同的世界构建模式，这些差异既是模型特异性的，也是语言敏感性的，揭示了AI生成叙事在"设定"构建上的系统性偏差。

Abstract: In this paper, we analyze how Large Language Models (LLMs) employ worldbuilding strategies, focusing on setting as one measurable dimension of storyworld construction. We compare 1,000 AI-generated stories per model in English and German with human-authored fiction from Project Gutenberg. Building on prior work, we operationalize setting through five types of narrative space: "action", "perceived," "visual," "descriptive" and "no space", identified using fine-tuned BERT classifiers for German and English. We generate narratives using GPT 4.1, LlaMA 3.3, Mistral 3.2, and Gemma 3 and compare their spatial distributions to a human-authored baseline. We find that human-authored texts predominantly employ "action space," grounding narratives in embodied character-environment interaction, whereas LLMs systematically overproduce "perceived space," emphasizing atmosphere and affect. This divergence remains stable across narrative time. Overall, our findings show that LLMs exhibit worldbuilding patterns that differ consistently from human-authored fiction in ways that are both model-specific and language-sensitive.

</details>


### [162] [Debias-SparseGPT: Bias-Aware Pruning for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02496)
*Irina Proskurina,Guillaume Metzler,Antoine Gourru,Julien Velcin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Debias-SparseGPT，一种在后训练剪枝过程中引入表示级去偏的剪枝方法，通过在人口统计学对比输入上定义二阶项来缓解SparseGPT等权重重稀疏化方法在LLM中放大偏见的问题，在多种生成式LLM及稀疏率下同时降低剪枝诱导偏见并保持困惑度和零样本准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然剪枝和量化等模型压缩技术能促进LLM的高效部署与加速，但现有研究（如SparseGPT）表明权重重稀疏化方法会放大模型中已有的偏见，导致输出在不同人物线索（persona cues）下产生显著差异。这种压缩引入的偏见问题缺乏有效的后训练解决方案。

Method: 提出Debias-SparseGPT，在后训练剪枝过程中通过包含人口统计学对比输入上的二阶项进行表示级去偏（representational debiasing）。该方法将去偏目标直接集成到剪枝优化框架中，可结合长上下文、内容丰富示例增强校准集以进一步提升效果。

Result: 在多种生成式LLM上验证，覆盖25%、50%稀疏率以及结构化2:4稀疏模式。相比SparseGPT，Debias-SparseGPT持续降低剪枝诱导偏见，同时保持模型困惑度和零样本准确率；在最严苛的2:4结构化稀疏下，扩展校准集可同时改善下游性能和公平性。

Conclusion: Debias-SparseGPT在偏见-性能权衡上取得进步，同时保留了稀疏模型的计算效率，为压缩场景下的公平性保障提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Model compression techniques such as pruning and quantization facilitate the efficient deployment and acceleration of Large Language Models (LLMs). However, recent studies show that weight sparsification methods, such as SparseGPT, can amplify existing biases in models, with outputs varying significantly depending on persona cues in the prompt. In this paper, we introduce Debias-SparseGPT, a post-training pruning method incorporating representational debiasing using a second-order term defined over demographically contrasting inputs. We perform empirical validation of our method over a wide range of generative LLMs. Across models and sparsity regimes (25%, 50%, and structured 2:4 sparsity), Debias-SparseGPT consistently reduces pruning-induced bias compared to SparseGPT while preserving model perplexity and zero-shot accuracy. Under the most restrictive 2:4 structured sparsity pattern, which most aggressively degrades model quality, augmenting the calibration set with long-context, content-rich examples further improves both downstream performance and fairness. Overall, Debias-SparseGPT advances the bias-performance trade-off while preserving the computational efficiency of sparse models.

</details>


### [163] [When Persona Attributes Improve Population Alignment in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02526)
*Leon Fröhling,Jens Rupprecht,Markus Strohmaier,Claudia Wagner*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了大型语言模型（LLMs）中使用"人格提示"（persona prompting）预测调查参与者响应的问题。作者提出调查问题中观测到的人类响应变异是此前人格提示效果混合表现的可能解释，并系统比较了不同人格属性选择方法的性能，在4个社会调查、2个国家、6个LLM和每个调查20个预测任务上进行了评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人格提示在LLM调查预测中的应用日益增多，但现有研究结果混合且部分矛盾，缺乏清晰的成败模式。虽然已知道人格属性的选择很重要，且使用更多属性不一定更好，但不同属性选择方法的性能及其选择标准仍不清楚。作者希望通过引入人类响应变异这一解释变量，为理解人格提示的适用条件提供新视角。

Method: 作者提出人类对调查问题的响应变异可能是此前人格提示表现不一致的解释因素。在此基础上，系统比较了不同人格属性选择方法（如随机选择、基于特定特征的选择等）在人格提示中的应用效果。实验覆盖4个不同的社会调查、2个国家、6个不同的LLM，每个调查包含20个预测任务，形成大规模系统化评估。

Result: 论文提供了人格提示在调查预测任务中何时有用的识别标准，并对不同属性选择方法在基于LLM的调查预测中的有效性提供了新见解。具体实验结果表明不同属性选择方法之间存在性能差异，且人类响应变异能部分解释此前实践中的混合结果。

Conclusion: 本文有助于阐明人格提示在调查预测中的适用场景，并为如何选择人格属性以提升LLM调查预测性能提供了实践指导。研究指出调查问题的人类响应变异程度是决定人格提示效能的关键因素之一。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used to predict the responses of human participants in survey panels. Towards that goal, persona prompting has recently emerged as a technique to inform and align large pretrained language models. Persona prompting refers to the practice of using short textual descriptions of 'personas' in prompts to steer the LLM's generations. Personas describe individuals through different attributes such as their socio-demographics, attitudes, or behaviors, with the aim of aligning LLMs to produce responses that correlate with the corresponding human responses. Yet, recent work has produced mixed and partly conflicting results of persona prompting without clear patterns of success and failure. Among the few consistent findings is that the selection of persona attributes matters, and that using more attributes does not necessarily lead to better performance. It remains unclear how different attribute selection methods perform and how to choose among them. In this paper, we propose that observed human response variation of a survey question is a potential explanation for the mixed performance observed so far. In addition, we compare the performance of persona prompting associated with different methods for selecting persona attributes. We evaluate these methods on four different (general) social surveys across two countries, six LLMs, and twenty prediction tasks per survey. Our work helps to identify when persona prompting can be expected to be useful in survey prediction tasks, and provides new insights on the effectiveness of different attribute selection methods for LLM-based survey prediction using persona prompting.

</details>


### [164] [Predictors of Loneliness in Older Adults Using Multimodal Analysis of Speech and Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02606)
*Vinmay Khandode,Sai Karthik Kosuri,Neil K. R. Sehgal,Adam Greene,Elif Alpoge,Elana Duffy,Matthew Lee Smith,Thomas K. M. Cudjoe,Sharath Chandra Guntuku*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究分析了310名老年人的半结构化电话访谈中的言语与语言特征，发现孤独感通过语言线索（否定词、负面语气、与冲突相关的语言）和声学线索（音调、语气、响度）共同表现。多模态模型（r=0.298）优于纯文本和纯音频模型，但孤独感检测建议作为现有心理评估的辅助工具，而非独立诊断手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 孤独感是老年人群体中严重的公共健康问题，与抑郁、认知衰退和死亡率升高相关，但目前缺乏在自然对话情境下可扩展且客观的孤独感检测方法，因此需要探索基于语音分析的情感孤独感识别途径。

Method: 采用多模态框架，对310名老年人的半结构化电话访谈进行分析，结合语言特征（心理语言学词典、n-gram、主题模型）与声学特征（音调、语气、响度），考察这些特征与自我报告的孤独感评分之间的关联，并将多模态模型与纯文本、纯音频模型进行对比。

Result: 较高孤独感与否定词（r=0.11）、负面语气（r=0.12）及冲突相关语言相关；较低孤独感与社会参照（r=-0.18）、动机驱力（r=-0.11）及言语情感丰富度（r=-0.12）相关。多模态模型（r=0.298）优于单一模态模型。

Conclusion: 孤独感同时通过语言和声学线索显现，语音分析方法在心理评估中具有潜力，可作为情感孤独感的早期指标，但应作为现有评估工具的补充而非独立的诊断工具。

Abstract: Loneliness is a critical public health issue among older adults, linked to higher risks of depression, cognitive decline, and mortality. Scalable, objective methods for its detection remain limited, particularly in natural conversational contexts. We analyzed speech and language markers of loneliness in 310 older adults using semi-structured telephone interviews to help understand how they process feeling lonely and how their language differs at different levels of feeling loneliness. Our multimodal framework combined linguistic features (psycholinguistic dictionaries, n-grams, and topic models) with acoustic features (pitch, tone, loudness) to examine associations with self-reported loneliness scores. Both predefined and data-driven methods captured patterns in verbal content and vocal delivery. Higher loneliness was associated with negations(r = 0.11), negative tone(r = 0.12), and conflict-related language. Lower loneliness was linked to social references(r = -0.18), motivational drives(r = -0.11), and emotional richness in speech(r = -0.12). We also found that the multimodal model (r = 0.298) outperforms the text-only and audio-only models. Findings suggest that loneliness manifests through both linguistic and acoustic cues, supporting the potential of speech-based analysis in psychological assessments and as an early indicator of emotional loneliness when used alongside existing assessments, rather than as standalone diagnostic tools.

</details>


### [165] [TaRA: Training-Aware Low-Rank Adaptation Initialization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02639)
*Taehyeon Kim,Eunhyeok Park*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出TaRA（Training-aware Low-Rank Adaptation Initialization），一种训练感知的LoRA初始化方法，通过数学推导使低秩因子诱导的梯度尽可能逼近全秩权重矩阵的梯度，提升初始训练阶段的梯度保真度，且计算开销可忽略不计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA虽已成为参数高效微调的标准方法，但其性能对初始化高度敏感，原因在于低秩分解带来的信息瓶颈。现有方法利用预训练权重、激活或梯度的主成分来构造高质量初始化，但未直接考虑全秩模型的训练动态，导致初始化与真实微调过程脱节。

Method: TaRA通过形式化数学推导，构造LoRA初始化使得低秩因子在训练起始时产生的梯度与对应全秩权重矩阵的梯度高度近似，从而提升梯度保真度，同时仅引入可忽略的计算开销。

Result: 在多个多样且具有挑战性的微调任务中，TaRA一致性地优于先前的现有最优方法，展现了简单、稳健且可扩展的LoRA初始化方案的有效性。

Conclusion: TaRA为LoRA初始化提供了一种训练感知的新视角，通过梯度对齐而非仅依赖静态主成分信息，实现了更优的微调性能，并可作为通用解决方案在多种任务中稳定应用。

Abstract: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) has become a de facto standard for parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT), yet its performance is highly sensitive to initialization due to the information bottleneck imposed by low-rank decomposition. Existing approaches attempt to construct high-quality LoRA initializations by exploiting principal components of pretrained weights, activations, or gradients. However, these methods do not directly account for the training dynamics of the full-rank model. In this paper, we propose Training-aware Low-Rank Adaptation Initialization (TaRA), a method that initializes LoRA such that the gradients induced by the low-rank factors closely approximate the gradient of the corresponding full-rank weight matrix. Derived from a mathematical formulation, TaRA improves gradient fidelity at the start of training while introducing negligible computational overhead. Across diverse and challenging fine-tuning tasks, TaRA consistently outperforms prior state-of-the-art methods, establishing a simple, robust, and scalable solution for effective LoRA initialization.

</details>


### [166] [WinoQueer-NL: Assessing Bias in Dutch Language Models toward LGBTQ+ Identities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02651)
*Jiska Beuk,Gerasimos Spanakis*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对荷兰语语言模型中针对LGBTQ+群体的偏见缺乏研究的问题，基于英语WinoQueer基准开发了文化及语言适配的荷兰语数据集，通过43名荷兰酷儿参与者的线上调查验证并扩充了偏见内容，最终发布了包含42,906个句子的数据集，并在多种荷兰语专用及多语言模型中评估了偏见水平。研究发现模型平均偏见得分看似中性（~50%），但细分分析显示跨性别身份的句子被模型偏爱高达97%，而同性恋相关仅6%，跨性别和非二元身份群体持续获得最高偏见分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 英语语言模型中的反酷儿偏见已被广泛研究，但荷兰语模型在该领域的研究严重不足。作者旨在弥补这一空白，开发一个文化适切、可验证的荷兰语偏见评估数据集。

Method: 作者基于英语WinoQueer基准，通过文化和语言适配构建了荷兰语数据集，包含成对的刻板与非刻板句子。随后进行了包含43名荷兰酷儿参与者的在线调查，确认了171项刻板印象中145项的文化相关性，并通过自由文本反馈识别了22个新偏见。最终数据集包含42,906个句子，利用掩码语言模型（MLMs）和自回归语言模型（ARLMs）评估，偏见通过比较刻板与非刻板句子的对数似然得分来衡量。

Result: 模型平均偏见得分看似中性（约50%），但细粒度分析揭示了显著差异：部分模型对跨性别身份的刻板句子偏爱高达97%，而对同性恋相关句子仅偏爱6%。跨性别和非二元身份群体始终获得最高的偏见得分，表明在荷兰语模型中这些边缘群体承受的系统性偏见最为严重。

Conclusion: 研究强调了对文化根基扎实的数据集的需求，用以评估和缓解对荷兰语语言模型中边缘化群体造成不成比例影响的偏见；单纯的均值指标会掩盖群体间显著的不均衡偏见分布。

Abstract: While English language models have been widely examined for anti-queer bias, Dutch models remain understudied. To address this gap, we developed a culturally and linguistically adapted Dutch dataset based on the English WinoQueer benchmark, containing pairs of stereotypical and counter-stereotypical sentences. To validate and expand it, we conducted an online survey with 43 Dutch queer participants, confirming 145 of 171 stereotypes as culturally relevant and identifying 22 new biases through free-text responses. The final released dataset, comprising 42,906 sentences, was evaluated using a range of Dutch-specific and multilingual models, including both masked language models (MLMs) and autoregressive language models (ARLMs), with bias measured via a score comparing log-likelihoods of stereotypical versus counter-stereotypical sentences. While the mean bias score across models appeared neutral (~50%), closer analysis revealed significant disparities: some models favored stereotypical sentences up to 97% of the time for transgender identities, but only 6% of the time for gay-related pairs, with transgender and non-binary identities consistently receiving the highest bias scores. Our findings highlight the importance of culturally grounded datasets for evaluating and mitigating biases that disproportionately impact marginalized groups in Dutch language models.

</details>


### [167] [oHC: Orthogonal Hyper-Connections on SO(4) via Quaternions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02672)
*Haoqiang Guo,Xuyi Chen,Bo Ke,Yishu Lei,Ziyang Xu,Shikun Feng,Ximen,Wenhan Luo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出正交超连接（oHC），将超连接（HC）中的残差混合矩阵约束在旋转群SO(n)上，既防止范数放大又避免范数衰减，从而在不牺牲流间多样性的前提下稳定训练。基于四元数对实现了无参数、无需迭代投影的闭式构造，实验表明oHC在多种下游任务上优于单流残差基线、mHC和iHC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超连接（HC）用n条并行残差流取代单一残差流，通过可学习的n×n残差矩阵逐层混合。若矩阵不加约束，混合步骤对残差流的缩放因子不受限且随层数累积放大，导致训练不稳定。现有的流形约束HC（mHC）将矩阵限制在双随机矩阵上，解决了范数放大问题，但下限无约束——混合会迫使流间差异随时间收缩，导致流趋于趋同、多样性随深度耗尽。

Method: 提出正交超连接（oHC），将残差矩阵约束在旋转群SO(n)上，使混合步骤在任何方向上都既不放大也不衰减残差流。针对近期HC模型使用的四流设置（n=4），利用单位四元数对在闭式形式下参数化SO(4)，不引入额外参数，用固定模式的符号加法取代迭代投影，构造速度比mHC更快。

Result: 在综合的下游任务集上评估，oHC优于单流残差基线、双随机约束的mHC以及将矩阵固定为单位阵（实际上不做混合）的iHC。

Conclusion: oHC在保持训练稳定性的同时避免了流间差异强制收缩的问题，既不像无约束HC那样放大范数，也不像mHC那样衰减差异，通过等距变换保留了多流多样性，且计算开销更低，是多流残差架构中更具优势的混合方式。

Abstract: Hyper-Connections (HC) replace the single residual stream of a Transformer with $n$ parallel ones, mixing them at every layer with a learned $n \times n$ residual matrix. Leaving that matrix unconstrained places no limit on the factor by which the mixing step rescales the residual streams, and that factor compounds across layers, which destabilizes training. Manifold-constrained Hyper-Connections (mHC) address this by restricting the matrix to the doubly stochastic matrices. That caps the factor at one, so the mixing can no longer amplify any direction, but nothing bounds it from below. We prove that inside this set the mixing step can reduce the norm of the residual streams only by shrinking the differences between the streams, while their mean is left unchanged; and since the reduction accumulates over layers, the streams grow more alike and their diversity is spent with depth. We therefore propose Orthogonal Hyper-Connections (oHC), restricting the residual matrix to the rotation group $SO(n)$, so that the mixing step can neither amplify nor attenuate the residual streams in any direction, which keeps training stable and no longer forces the differences between the streams to contract. Specifically, at the four streams used by recent HC models we parameterize the group in closed form by a pair of unit quaternions, which adds no parameters, replaces the iterative projection with a fixed pattern of signed additions, and can be constructed faster than mHC. We evaluate oHC across a comprehensive set of downstream tasks, where it outperforms the single-stream residual baseline, mHC and iHC, which fixes the residual matrix to the identity.

</details>


### [168] [From Tokens to Semantics: Leveraging Complementary Signals for Hallucination Detection in Black-Box LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02679)
*Urja Pawar,Rajitha Ramanayake,Owen O'Neill,Nabeel Kemal,Abhishek Mandal,Houssem Chatbri,Christopher Martin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了在大语言模型（LLM）支持高风险或面向公众的工作流时，如何在无可信上下文或参考文档的条件下检测模型生成内容中的"虚构/幻觉"（fabrication）。作者比较了语义熵和基于token对数概率的不确定性两类信号的互补性，提出了TopK（聚合采样响应中的token级信号）、Gated和Stacked（监督学习方法）等方法，并在七个基准上使用四种LLM评估，发现Stacked在近半数情况下表现最佳，但没有一个方法在所有场景下普遍最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当LLMs用于面向公众或高风险的流程时，模型可能生成虚构内容，这会造成严重危害。现有检测方法存在盲区：语义熵在响应形成单一语义簇时失效（无法区分多个相同含义的采样），而token级不确定性可能遗漏"一致但错误"的输出（模型始终自信地给出错误答案）。两种信号各自的失败模式具有互补性，因此需要研究如何结合它们来提升幻觉检测的鲁棒性。

Method: 作者提出了四种方法：(1) TopK——扩展token级不确定性检测，将token级信号跨采样响应聚合；(2) CoCoA（混合方法）——结合目标响应的不确定性与语义不相似度；(3) Gated（监督）——将单语义簇的情况路由到基于聚合token特征的分类器；(4) Stacked（监督）——联合从语义不确定性和更广泛的token特征中学习。评估涵盖七个基准（五个公开基准含文本数据集和多模态手写支票提取，两个自建基准：Financial Summaries和Long-Text QA），使用四种LLM，并在1%到15%的假阳性率预算下评估性能，同时考察对生成和校准选择的敏感性。

Result: 在跨模型和数据集的评估中，Stacked在近一半的情形下取得最佳性能；TopK和CoCoA在无需监督训练标签的情况下仍具竞争力，但其阈值需要仔细校准。没有任何方法在所有场景下普遍最强，结果随假阳性率预算（1%-15%）、生成和校准参数以及数据集特征而变化。

Conclusion: 语义熵与token不确定性在幻觉检测中具有互补性，需要结合使用。没有单一方法适用于所有场景，实际部署应根据具体应用在假阳性率预算等约束下选择方法，并注意阈值校准的重要性。监督方法（尤其Stacked）在多数情况下性能领先，但无监督方法（TopK、CoCoA）在无标注数据时仍是可行选择。

Abstract: When LLMs support public-facing or high-stakes workflows, missed fabrications can harm users and institutions, while false alarms consume limited human-review capacity. When no trusted context or reference document is available, we study two signals accessible through black-box model APIs: semantic entropy, which measures disagreement among sampled response meanings, and uncertainty derived from token log-probabilities. Their failure modes can be complementary: semantic entropy becomes uninformative when responses form one semantic cluster, while token uncertainty can miss consistently confident errors. We extend token-based uncertainty detection by aggregating token-level signals across sampled responses through our TopK method, evaluate the hybrid CoCoA method, which combines target-response uncertainty with semantic dissimilarity, and propose and study two supervised methods: Gated, which routes single-cluster cases to an aggregated-token-feature classifier, and Stacked, which learns jointly from semantic uncertainty and broader token features. We evaluate seven benchmarks, including five public benchmarks (four text datasets and multimodal handwritten-cheque extraction) and two constructed benchmarks (Financial Summaries and Long-Text QA), using four language models. In our evaluation across models and datasets, Stacked gave the best performance in nearly half of the cases, while TopK and CoCoA remain competitive without supervised training labels, although their thresholds require careful calibration. No method is universally strongest. We therefore evaluate performance at false-positive-rate budgets from 1% to 15%, assess their sensitivity to generation and calibration choices, and examine variation across dataset characteristics.

</details>


### [169] [DKL: Decoupled Knowledge Learning for Instruction-Tuned Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02685)
*Kushagra Bhushan,Meghanadh Pulivarthi,Sai Krishna Reddy Sathi,Gaurav Pandey,Sonam Gupta,Vineet Kumar,Jaydeep Sen,Yatin Nandwani,Sachindra Joshi,Dinesh Raghu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出DKL（解耦知识学习）方法，通过在对应的基础LLM上做扩展预训练注入新知识，再通过模型合并将知识注入到指令微调LLM中，避免昂贵的指令微调，同时不破坏模型的指令跟随能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG虽能增强事实依据但检索失败时会幻觉；RAFT和PA-RAG等微调方法需要生成覆盖整个语料库的海量合成QA；扩展预训练（EPT）虽避免合成数据但仍需昂贵的指令微调（IFT），且当指令微调语料不可用时无法实施。

Method: DKL方法：不在Instruct LLM上直接做EPT，而是先在对应的基础LLM（Base LLM）上进行扩展预训练注入新知识，然后将知识注入后的权重与Instruct LLM的权重进行模型合并，从而将新知识传递给Instruct LLM而不影响其指令跟随能力。

Result: 在检索失败案例上，将RAG准确率从54.17提升到79.26，且使用更少的训练数据超越了先前的各类方法。

Conclusion: DKL是一种轻量级方法，通过模型合并实现知识与指令跟随能力的解耦注入，避免了昂贵的指令微调，有效解决了检索失败和指令微调语料不可用的问题。

Abstract: RAG has become the de facto method for incorporating new, corpus-specific knowledge into an instruction following LLM (Instruct LLM). Although RAG-based prompting improves factual grounding, it fails when retrieval is incorrect or incomplete, leading to hallucinations. Finetuning methods such as RAFT and PA-RAG enhance RAG by injecting new knowledge into the model's parameters, but require generating a massive amount of synthetic QA that covers the entire corpus. Extended Pre-Training (EPT) on the text corpus avoids the need for comprehensive synthetic data generation but compromises an Instruct LLM's instruction-following capabilities, necessitating instruction fine-tuning (IFT) after pre-training. However, IFT is costly and may be infeasible due to the unavailability of an instruction-tuning corpus. In this work, we propose DKL-Decoupled Knowledge Learning for Instruction-Tuned Language Models. Instead of doing EPT on the Instruct LLM, DKL performs EPT on its corresponding base LLM to infuse new knowledge. These knowledge infused weights are then merged with the Instruct LLM, imparting new knowledge without affecting their instruction-following capabilities. DKL is a lightweight method that avoids expensive instruction fine-tuning and relies on model merging to infuse the new knowledge into the Instruct LLM without destroying its instruction following capabilities. Empirical results show that DKL improves RAG accuracy from 54.17 to 79.26 on retrieval failure cases, while outperforming prior approaches with substantially less training data.

</details>


### [170] [Trace as State: Reasoning Traces as Conditional States for Long-Context Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02702)
*Xu Zou,Jie Tang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对Transformer因果推理与长上下文任务状态发现顺序之间的不匹配问题，提出"Trace as State"方法：将收集的推理轨迹作为任务状态的文本代理，置于长上下文块之前，让先前的推理结果指导重读。在三个模型和三个长上下文数据集上的实验表明，该方法在27种组合中26种优于反向放置的对照组（Trace Append），在某些任务上准确率从29.2%提升至81.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer以因果方式处理信息，但长上下文推理可能依赖于在后续位置才被发现的任务状态。研究者在正式化这种不匹配时发现，对于因果状态更新处理器，条件先于上下文提供时在最坏情况下所需的记忆量可能指数级少于条件最后提供的情形。这一原理启发了将推理轨迹前置的想法。

Method: 作者提出Trace as State方法：先收集推理轨迹，将其作为任务状态的文本代理，在全新的pass中将该轨迹置于长上下文块之前，使之前推导出的信息能够指导模型的重读。同时设置Trace Append作为匹配的对照实验，使用相同的任务状态代理但将其置于上下文之后。

Result: 在三个模型和三个长上下文数据集上，Trace as State在27个模型-任务-指标组合中有26个优于Trace Append。在GraphWalks Parents任务上，DeepSeek V4 Pro Preview的精确匹配从初始pass的29.2%和Trace Append的43.0%提升至81.8%；GLM-5.2则从66.4%和83.2%提升至100.0%。

Conclusion: 将推理轨迹置于上下文之前可以有效提升长上下文推理性能，同时保留因果Transformer的结构不变。这为在因果架构约束下解决需要后续状态发现的长上下文任务提供了一条可行的路径。

Abstract: Transformers process information causally, but long-context reasoning may depend on task state discovered only later. We formalize this mismatch through conditional state update tasks. For causal state update processors, providing the condition first can require exponentially less memory in the worst case than providing it last.
  Motivated by this principle, we introduce Trace as State. We use collected reasoning traces as a textual proxy for task state and place it before the long-context block on a fresh pass, allowing information derived previously to guide rereading.
  We conduct extensive experiments on Trace as State and Trace Append, a matched control that uses the same task state proxy but put it after the context. Across three models and three long-context datasets, Trace as State outperforms Trace Append in 26 of 27 reported combinations of model, task, and metric. On GraphWalks Parents, exact match lifts DeepSeek V4 Pro Preview from 29.2% on the initial pass and 43.0% with Trace Appendto 81.8% with Trace as State, and from 66.4% and 83.2% to 100.0% for GLM-5.2. These results show that placing traces before the context can improve long-context reasoning while retaining the causal transformer structure.

</details>


### [171] [CORAL: An LLM-Native Harness for Production Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02730)
*Muhammad Rafay Azhar,Yuhang Zhou,Gilbert Jiang,Yuchen Wang,Rahul Sharma,Matthew DeSousa,Jiayi Liu,Xin Guo,Lizhu Zhang,Xiangjun Fan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CORAL是一个LLM驱动的闭环代理系统，通过观察运行信号、记忆历史决策、调用工具（包括数值优化器）循环地重新配置生产推荐系统。在两大社交平台上经A/B实验验证，CORAL在一平台上以零额外服务成本提升参与度，在另一平台上以不损害参与度的方式降低成本，横跨参与度-效率前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产推荐系统需要持续优化，但传统方式依赖人类工程师进行在线实验，这是一个缓慢、被动的过程，受工程人力限制，导致系统部分环节在环境变化后未能及时修订。虽然LLM已被用于排序、用户建模等，但很少有系统让代理处于持续闭环中主动作用于在线推荐器并从决策结果中学习。

Method: 将问题形式化为部分可观测、非平稳、带约束的优化问题。每个循环中，LLM代理：1) 观察运行信号；2) 基于过去决策与结果的记忆进行推理；3) 调用工具（包括数值优化器）以在固定运行预算内重配置推荐系统；4) 实测结果反馈到下一循环。策略在上下文中改进，无需参数更新。

Result: 在两个大规模社交平台上通过A/B实验验证：同一套系统在一个平台上以零额外服务成本提升参与度，在另一个平台上在不降低参与度的前提下减少服务成本。随着循环迭代，性能持续提升，覆盖了参与度-效率前沿。

Conclusion: 单一的代理闭环能够自动化传统上由人类算法工程师在显式防护栏下执行的持续优化工作，为生产推荐系统的自动化运维提供了可行范式。

Abstract: Production recommender systems shape what billions of people see, and sustaining their performance requires continual optimization: as content, user behavior, and upstream models shift, the choices governing retrieval, ranking, and serving must be revisited. Traditionally, human engineers test such changes through online experiments--a slow, reactive process limited by engineering effort, leaving parts of the system unrevised as conditions change. Although large language models have been applied to ranking, user modeling, and offline model development, few systems place an agent in a continual closed loop that acts on a live recommender and learns from the measured effects of its decisions. We present CORAL (Constraint-Optimized Recommender via an Agentic Loop), an LLM-native harness that closes this loop: each cycle, the agent observes operating signals, reasons over a memory of past decisions and outcomes, and invokes tools--including a numerical optimizer that keeps changes within a fixed operating budget--to reconfigure the recommender, with measured outcomes informing the next cycle. We formulate this as a partially observed, non-stationary, constrained optimization problem in which the policy improves in context, without parameter updates, from its prior actions. Across two large-scale social platforms, evaluated with A/B experiments, the same harness improves engagement at no additional serving cost on one and reduces serving cost without degrading engagement on the other, spanning the engagement-efficiency frontier. Performance improves as the loop iterates, suggesting that a single agentic loop can automate continual optimization work traditionally performed by human algorithm engineers under explicit guardrails.

</details>


### [172] [Choosing a PEFT Variant for Per-Patient Dysarthric ASR: A Single-Speaker Case Study on Two ASR Bases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02735)
*Bernard Muller,László Tóth,LaVonne Roberts*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究针对一名罹患严重构音障碍的中风后匈牙利男性说话者（S1，409条话语），在两种生产级基座模型（Whisper-large-v3匈牙利语微调版及多语言Qwen3-ASR-1.7B）上系统比较了七种LoRA家族参数高效微调方法。结果显示注意力投影适配器在两种基座上均显著改善CER；LoRA与DoRA无显著差异，故采用更简单的LoRA；真实4-bit QLoRA在所有种子和基座上均更差且无内存节省；LoHA、VeRA、VB-LoRA和AdaLoRA未达到LoRA家族水平。全量微调更精确（11.43% CER），但同时适配前馈模块的115 MB LoRA（约3.7%存储成本）可达到接近全量微调的效果（差距0.66个百分点）。6点入组网格显示约5分钟患者音频即可捕获零样本到30分钟CER降幅的45.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 每患者适配器是构音障碍语音识别（ASR）生产环境的首选架构，但参数高效微调（PEFT）变体尚未在说话者依赖的每患者场景下进行系统比较。研究者旨在填补这一空白，为该场景选择最优的LoRA家族方法。

Method: 以单说话者案例研究形式，在同一中风后匈牙利男性构音障碍患者上比较七种LoRA家族方法（LoRA、QLoRA、AdaLoRA、DoRA、LoHA、VeRA、VB-LoRA），使用两个生产基座模型（Whisper-large-v3匈牙利语微调版和Qwen3-ASR-1.7B），采用配对bootstrap进行显著性检验，并测试全量微调、前馈模块适配及不同入组时长等变体。

Result: 注意力投影适配器大幅改善两种基座的CER；LoRA与DoRA无显著差异（p>0.5；13.86/13.90%及28.10/28.33% CER）；真实4-bit QLoRA在所有种子和基座上均较差（14.56/30.09% CER）且无内存优势；LoHA仍带来18.6%相对CER降低。全量微调为11.43% CER，而适配前馈块的115 MB LoRA以约3.7%存储成本逼近（差距0.66个百分点）。5分钟音频可获45.6%的CER降幅收益。

Conclusion: 在严重构音障碍的每患者ASR场景中，标准LoRA是性价比最优的选择；全量微调虽有更高精度但存储开销大；仅需约5分钟的患者音频即可获得显著的适配收益，为临床入组流程提供了实证依据。

Abstract: Per-patient adapters are the preferred production architecture for dysarthric automatic speech recognition (ASR), yet parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) variants have not been compared in the speaker-dependent, per-patient regime. We present a single-speaker case study comparing seven LoRA-family methods (LoRA, QLoRA, AdaLoRA, DoRA, LoHA, VeRA, VB-LoRA) on two production bases (Whisper-large-v3 with Hungarian fine-tuning, and a multilingual Qwen3-ASR-1.7B checkpoint) for one post-stroke Hungarian male speaker (S1, 409 utterances; severe dysarthria on auditory-perceptual clinical assessment). Attention-projection adapters substantially improve CER on both bases. Across three seeds, a paired bootstrap detects no significant LoRA-DoRA difference (p>0.5; 13.86/13.90 % CER on Whisper, 28.10/28.33 % on Qwen3-ASR), so we adopt the simpler, cheaper LoRA. Real 4-bit (NF4) QLoRA is worse on every seed and both bases (14.56/30.09 % CER) with no memory saving at this scale, and LoHA, VeRA, VB-LoRA and AdaLoRA do not reach the LoRA family, though LoHA still gives an 18.6 % relative CER reduction on Whisper. On the same base, full fine-tuning is more accurate (11.43 % CER), but a 115 MB LoRA that also adapts the feed-forward blocks reaches within 0.66 pp of it at approximately 3.7 % of the per-patient storage. A 6-point enrollment grid shows about 5 min of patient audio captures 45.6 % of the zero-shot-to-30-min CER reduction, with further gains at 10 and 30 min (caveat: one speaker, one language, severe post-stroke dysarthria). Training scripts and recipes will be released, source-available under a research-use licence, on publication.

</details>


### [173] [Language Models Can Control Their Own Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02737)
*Namgyu Ho,Huzama Ahmad,Woosung Koh,Se-Young Yun,Tal Schuster,Cicero Nogueira dos Santos*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了声明式注意力（DA）协议，让模型在思维链中主动声明需要关注的上下文区域，从而跳过大部分KV缓存读取，在15个长上下文任务上实现显著的计算节省。




<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稀疏注意力方法通常通过轻量级代理分数在外部预先选择相关token，但这种方法每步仍需O(N)的额外开销。作者提出了一个关键问题：模型本身是否已经知道上下文中哪些部分相关？这构成了内在式注意力路由的核心动机。



Method: 提出了Declarative Attention (DA)协议，通过在chain-of-thought中让模型主动声明注意力需求，将生成过程划分为三种模式：<global>（全上下文）、<focus>（特定区域）、<local>（仅最近输出）。推理引擎像解析工具调用一样解析这些声明，从而跳过大部分KV缓存读取。

Result: 在15个长上下文任务的零样本评估中，DA在现成模型（Gemma-4-31B、Qwen-3.6-27B）上使解码期间的总注意力token数分别减少52.0%和31.1%，精度损失仅为1.27pp和2.75pp，且精度损失随模型规模增大而缩小。



Conclusion: DA为稀疏注意力开辟了一个新的维度，展示了通过让模型自我声明注意力区域来实现高效推理的可行性，并且未来在基于训练的方法下还有进一步优化潜力。



Abstract: Language models spend most of their attention on a small fraction of context, yet they read the entire KV cache to find the few tokens that matter. If the user asks about a previous detail in a 1M-token conversation, global attention layers must scan the full context to generate each token of the reply. A prominent approach mitigates this cost by pre-selecting relevant tokens via lightweight proxy scores, but this extrinsic scoring still incurs O(N) per step. We take an intrinsic approach motivated by the simple question: wouldn't the model already know which parts of the context are relevant? To this end, we introduce Declarative Attention (DA), a protocol that elicits the model to declare where it needs to attend within its chain-of-thought, partitioning generation into three modes: <global> (full context), <focus> (a specific region), and <local> (recent output only). The inference engine parses these declarations like tool calls and skips most of the KV cache read. Under zero-shot evaluation across 15 long-context tasks, DA on off-the-shelf models (Gemma-4-31B, Qwen-3.6-27B) significantly reduces total attended tokens during decoding (52.0%, 31.1%) with modest accuracy drops (1.27pp, 2.75pp) that shrink with model scale. DA unlocks a new axis of sparse attention, with further potential under training-based methods that future work can explore.

</details>


### [174] [Untangling the Mechanisms of Misleading Context in Medical Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02754)
*Robin Linzmayer,Noémie Elhadad*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究误导性上下文如何损害大语言模型在医学问答中的判断力。基于MedMisBench基准的8,627道医学推理问题，作者注入"伪造证据"与"裸断言"两种误导线索，测试三个推理模型。结果发现模型对断言比对证据更易受影响（更频繁采纳错误答案）；误导线索在推理轨迹中被披露率（81-98%）远高于响应文本（7-90%）；两类线索对推理的破坏机制不同（证据早期累积进入，断言在结尾处重定向结论）；LLM监控器在读取开放推理轨迹时能捕获78%的受污染决策（5%假阳性），而仅看响应最多只有32%。结论指出模型最易受影响的误导上下文披露最少，且只有开放推理轨迹才能可靠监测，但前沿模型提供商往往不提供该轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型虽能以专家级水平回答医学问题，但它们依赖的上下文可能具有误导性，而这种误导性上下文会破坏模型在医学关键领域中的判断力。理解误导上下文如何损害模型判断——包括其易感性、披露情况、推理破坏机制与可监测性——对构建安全可靠的医学AI系统至关重要，尤其是在高风险医疗决策场景下。

Method: 使用MedMisBench医学推理子集（8,627道经临床医生审阅的问答题目），注入两类误导性上下线索——伪造证据与裸断言。测试三个推理模型：两个暴露完整推理轨迹，一个前沿模型仅暴露其响应。通过不确定性重采样分析推理轨迹以区分两类线索对推理的不同污染机制，并使用LLM监控器评估在不同监管条件下（是否读取推理轨迹、是否有引导提示）检测受污染决策的能力。

Result: 三模型对断言比伪造证据更易受影响（采纳被断言答案的频率高出10-27个百分点）。误导线索在推理轨迹中的披露率（81-98%）显著高于响应（7-90%），且断言类线索比基于证据的线索披露更少。重采样显示两类线索破坏推理的方式不同：证据在早期进入并逐步累积影响，断言则在推理接近结尾时重定向结论。LLM监控器在读取引导条件下的开放推理轨迹时可捕获78%的受污染决策（5%假阳性），而仅基于响应检测最多仅捕获32%。

Conclusion: 研究表明模型最易受影响的误导线索（裸断言）披露率恰恰最低，且该类型污染只有在开放推理轨迹中才可被可靠监测，而前沿模型提供商通常不对外提供推理轨迹。这构成一个安全悖论：最需要透明监测的模型类型恰恰最不可监测。此发现对医学AI的可靠性保障与监管策略设计具有重要启示，呼吁更广泛地开放模型推理过程以支持下游安全监控。

Abstract: Large language models now answer medical questions with expert-level performance. However, the context these systems act on can be misleading, and misleading context can corrupt a model's medical judgment. To understand how misleading context corrupts this judgment, we examine the model's susceptibility to the context, disclosure of it, mechanism of corrupted reasoning, and monitorability of the decision. On the medical reasoning subset of MedMisBench, a clinician-reviewed question-answering benchmark of 8,627 questions, we inject two types of misleading context cues, fabricated evidence and a bare assertion. We test three reasoning models, two that expose their full reasoning trace and one frontier model that exposes only its response. All three are more susceptible to the assertion than to the fabricated evidence, adopting the asserted answer 10 to 27 points more often. The misleading cues are disclosed in 81 to 98% of traces but only 7 to 90% of responses, and the assertion is disclosed less often than evidence based cues. Resampling from reasoning traces without disclosure shows the two cues corrupt reasoning differently, evidence entering early and accumulating while the assertion redirects the conclusion near its end. An LLM monitor catches 78% of corrupted decisions at 5% false positives when reading an open model's trace with guidance, against at most 32% from any response. The misleading context that models are most susceptible to is disclosed least, and was caught reliably only from an open reasoning trace, which frontier providers withhold.

</details>


### [175] [From Reweighting to Rewriting: Unlocking the Intervention Effects of Influential Samples in Training Data Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02771)
*Yuzhang Luo,Chenpeng Wang,Jianhui Chen,Liangming Pan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"影响引导的响应改写"方法，利用影响函数选择干预目标，通过改写示例的响应而非重新加权来干预模型行为。实验表明，响应改写比重新加权能产生更强、更持久且双向的行为转变，揭示了影响函数识别的示例本身具有比其局部重加权估计更大的干预潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练数据归因（TDA）的干预价值取决于两部分：选择哪些示例以及如何修改它们。影响函数（IF）估计无穷小重新加权下的行为变化，但IF选择的示例在基于权重的干预下相比随机选择优势有限。这引发了关键疑问：是影响示例本身缺乏干预价值，还是重新加权未能实现其行为杠杆。作者旨在区分影响估计所捕捉的局部重加权效应与所识别示例的更广泛干预潜力。

Method: 提出影响引导的响应改写（influence-guided response rewriting）：使用影响函数识别干预目标示例，保持指令不变，将示例的响应替换为行为对齐或行为对立的监督信号。在四个开源大语言模型上，以认知弃权（epistemic abstention）为主要实验场景，对同一组影响函数选择的示例分别执行改写与重新加权两类干预，并进行系统比较。

Result: 响应改写产生更强、更持久且双向（增强或抑制目标行为）的行为转变；而相同示例的重新加权效果微弱且不稳定。进一步分析表明，影响函数选择的示例相比其他选择器具有更高的改写杠杆，且行为变化集中在目标相关行为上。该定性对比在安全拒绝（safety refusal）场景中同样成立。

Conclusion: 影响估计捕捉的局部重加权效应与所识别示例的更广泛干预杠杆之间存在本质区别。这提示TDA方法的评估应考虑干预方式（intervention-aware），因为影响函数选择的高影响示例可能具有远超其局部权重估计所指示的干预潜力。

Abstract: Training data attribution (TDA) aims to identify training examples that shape model behavior, but its intervention value depends on both which examples are selected and how they are modified. Influence functions (IF) estimate behavioral changes under infinitesimal reweighting, yet IF-selected examples often show limited advantages over random selection under conventional weight-based interventions. This raises the question of whether influential examples lack intervention value or whether reweighting fails to realize their behavioral leverage.We introduce influence-guided response rewriting, which uses IF to identify intervention targets and replaces their responses with behavior-aligned or behavior-opposed supervision while keeping instructions fixed. Across four open-weight LLMs, we compare rewriting and reweighting on the same influence-selected examples using epistemic abstention as our primary testbed. Response rewriting produces stronger, more persistent, and bidirectional behavioral shifts, while reweighting the same examples yields weak and inconsistent effects. Further analyses show that influence-selected examples provide greater rewriting leverage than alternative selectors, with changes remaining concentrated on target-relevant behaviors. The same qualitative contrast extends to safety refusal. These results distinguish the local reweighting effects captured by influence estimates from the broader intervention leverage of the examples they identify, motivating intervention-aware evaluation of TDA methods.

</details>


### [176] [HyperStyler: Low-resource Authorship Style Transfer via Context-aware Style Navigation and Hypernetworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02772)
*Jongkyung Shin,Minguk Jeon,Chanwoo Park,Chiehyeon Lim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出HyperStyler，将低资源作者风格迁移解耦为风格选择与风格实现两个阶段，通过动态参数调制替代隐藏状态注入，在保持语义的同时实现高保真风格迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LAST方法将多样化的参考样本压缩为单一静态作者嵌入，导致上下文相关风格变化被平均化；同时依赖隐藏表示进行风格控制，使风格与内容纠缠不清，难以兼顾风格保真度和语义保持。

Method: 提出HyperStyler架构，包含两个模块：Stylo-navigator（风格导航器）通过联合建模源文本上下文和目标作者参考样本，预测风格坐标；Stylo-hypernet（风格超网络）通过动态参数调制（而非隐藏状态注入）实现风格的具体表达，从而实现风格选择与风格实现的解耦。

Result: 在Reddit、Blog和News三个数据集上的实验表明，HyperStyler在各项指标上一致优于包括基于LLM方法在内的现有方法，且具有良好的跨域泛化能力。仅需在T5-large基础上增加2.4%的参数，推理速度比LLM快1.8倍以上。

Conclusion: 通过将风格迁移显式分解为选择与实现两个子任务，并用参数调制替代隐藏状态注入，HyperStyler有效解决了静态作者嵌入表征不足和风格内容纠缠的问题，实现了参数高效、推理高效且性能优异的小样本作者风格迁移。

Abstract: Low-resource authorship style transfer (LAST) aims to rewrite text into the style of an arbitrary target author using only a few reference examples while preserving the original meaning. Existing methods often struggle to achieve both high style fidelity and semantic preservation because they compress diverse references into a single static author embedding, which averages out context-dependent stylistic variation, and rely on hidden representations for style control, which entangle style with content. We propose HyperStyler, a novel architecture that decouples LAST into style selection and style realization. Stylo-navigator predicts style coordinates by jointly modeling the source context and target-author references, and Stylo-hypernet realizes them via dynamic parameter modulation instead of hidden-state injection. Our experiments on Reddit, Blog, and News datasets demonstrate that HyperStyler consistently outperforms prior methods including LLM-based approaches and generalizes robustly across domains. Notably, HyperStyler achieves superior performance with as few as 2.4% additional parameters over T5-large, while being over 1.8x faster than LLMs at inference.

</details>


### [177] [Two Centuries of Sexism in British Parliament: A Computational Analysis of Women's Representation in the Hansard Corpus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30485)
*Mohammad Omar Khursheed,Mandira Sawkar,Ashiqur R. KhudaBukhsh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文利用大语言模型分析了英国议会200年间（1803-2005）6,531篇关于女性权利的辩论演讲，系统性地揭示了性别歧视语言在两百年间的持续存在模式。研究还发布了包含670万篇演讲、120万场辩论、89%性别匹配率的元数据增强版Hansard数据集，发现反对女性代表权的演讲中54%含有性别歧视内容（支持方仅21%），且两方使用不同类型的性别歧视（敌意性与善意性），与Ambivalent Sexism理论一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在揭示立法机关在辩论女性权利时（即便是支持女性权利）所使用的语言中编码了跨越两个世纪持续存在的系统性性别歧视模式，填补利用大规模语言模型和历史语料库对议会性别话语进行量化分析的空白，同时提供优化后的公开数据集以促进计算社会科学研究。

Method: 使用大语言模型对5,531篇英国议会辩论演讲（Hansard, 1803-2005）进行分类，包括：1) 分类演讲者对女性选举权和政治代表权的立场；2) 基于Ambivalent Sexism Inventory（矛盾性别歧视量表）分析议会中的性别歧视言论。同时构建并发布了元数据增强版Hansard语料库，含670万篇演讲、120万场辩论，House of Commons演讲者的性别匹配率达89%。

Result: 1) 反对女性代表权的演讲中54%含性别歧视内容，支持阵营仅为21%；2) 反对阵营同时使用敌意性和善意性性别歧视，而支持阵营的性别歧视绝大多数是善意性的；3) 女性议员支持女性政治权利的比例为93%，远高于男性议员的70%，这一差距在获得选举权后才缩小。

Conclusion: 研究发现敌意性（hostile）和善意性（benevolent）性别歧视在不同修辞语境中的使用模式与Ambivalent Sexism理论一致，这一发现不仅量化了历史议会中的性别偏见，也为理解现代议会性别话语提供了历史纵深视角。

Abstract: The language a legislature uses to debate women's rights, even in favour of them, encodes systematic patterns of sexism that persist across two centuries. In this work, we analyse 6,531 speeches over 200 years of UK parliamentary debate (Hansard, 1803-2005) by using large language models to classify a speaker's perspective towards women's suffrage and political representation, as well as analyse sexist speech in parliament from the lens of the Ambivalent Sexism Inventory. We also release this parliamentary dataset, an organized and metadata-enriched version of the publicly available Hansard Corpus optimized for computational social science research, with 6.7 million speeches across 1.2 million debates, with 89% gender-matching for speeches by MPs from the House of Commons. We find that 54% of speeches opposing women's representation contain sexist content, compared to 21% of speeches that are for the cause, and that the two sides use fundamentally different types of sexism: anti-suffrage rhetoric combines hostile and benevolent framing, while pro-suffrage sexism is overwhelmingly benevolent. Female MPs support women's political rights at 93% compared to 70% for male MPs, a gap that closes only after enfranchisement. Our findings are evidence that benevolent and hostile sexism are used in different rhetorical contexts in a manner consistent with the theory of Ambivalent Sexism.

</details>


### [178] [EarlyEval: Cheaper Agent Evaluation via Early Outcome Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02783)
*Yuling Shi,Zhensu Sun,Junsen Dong,Chengcheng Wan,David Lo,Xiaodong Gu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出EarlyEval框架，通过训练LightGBM成功/失败分类器，利用智能体的中间行为特征提前预测任务结果并提前终止运行，从而在单个任务内部降低评估成本，实现13%-26%的步骤削减和最高44.1%输入token、29.4%输出token的节省，同时保持89%-97%的预测准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体的评估成本急剧上升，一次前沿模型的agentic benchmark评估可能花费数百至数千美元，且在迭代开发中需反复支付。已有的benchmark蒸馏方法仅减少评估任务数量，但未降低每个保留任务的执行成本，亟需在任务粒度内开辟新的效率优化维度。

Method: 提出早期结果预测（early outcome prediction）方法并实例化为EarlyEval框架。关键洞察是智能体的最终结果往往在完成前就通过中间行为显露端倪。具体实现：训练一对LightGBM成功分类器和失败分类器，特征涵盖行为特征、文本特征和参考答案特征；当任一分类器越过校准后的置信度阈值时即提前终止智能体运行，引入的每步额外开销可忽略不计。

Result: 在SWE-bench Verified、TerminalBench和Toolathlon三个基准上，EarlyEval可消除13%-26%的智能体运行步骤，最多削减44.1%的输入token和29.4%的输出token，预测准确率在89%-97%之间，同时每个智能体的resolve rate平均仅受1-2个百分点的影响。

Conclusion: 早期结果预测为LLM智能体评估效率提供了与benchmark蒸馏互补的新维度——在任务内部降低执行成本。实验结果证明该方法在保持评估准确性的同时能显著节省计算资源，为低成本、可扩展的智能体评估开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Evaluating LLM agents is essential for guiding their development, yet it has grown prohibitively expensive: a single pass of a frontier model over an agentic benchmark can cost hundreds to thousands of dollars, a price paid repeatedly across iterative development cycles. Prior efforts, centered on benchmark distillation, reduce the number of evaluation tasks but leave the cost of executing each retained task untouched. In this work, we introduce early outcome prediction, a complementary axis of efficiency that instead cuts cost within each task. Our key insight is that an agent's final outcome is often evident from its intermediate behavior well before execution completes. We instantiate this idea in EarlyEval, a lightweight framework that trains a pair of LightGBM success and failure classifiers over behavioral, textual, and reference-solution features, and halts an agent run the moment either classifier crosses a calibrated confidence threshold, adding negligible per-step overhead. Across three benchmarks, SWE-bench Verified, TerminalBench, and Toolathlon, EarlyEval can eliminate 13%-26% of agent steps and up to 44.1% input tokens and 29.4% output tokens at 89%-97% prediction accuracy, while perturbing per-agent resolve rates by only one to two percentage points on average.

</details>


### [179] [DiscoSign: Discourse-Aware Text to Sign Language Gloss Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02796)
*Vasileios Baltatzis,Mert Inan,Connor Gillis,Raja Kushalnagar,Lorna Quandt,Leah Findlater,Colin Lea*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出DiscoSign，首个面向话语层面的文本到手语语标翻译系统，基于LLM模块化框架处理空间共指消解、问答题从句和概念-语标一致性三大话语现象，并引入专用评估指标体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统手语处理系统仅停留在句子层面，忽视了手语理解中根本性的话语现象（如空间共指关系、语篇功能结构等），导致翻译缺乏话语连贯性。现有方法无法在话语层面保证空间一致性和实体跟踪的准确性。

Method: DiscoSign采用基于大语言模型（LLM）的模块化翻译框架，分别处理三大话语现象：(i) 空间共指消解——保证实体在整个话语中保持一致的空间位置；(ii) 问答题从句(QACs)——处理充当特定话语功能的伪分裂结构；(iii) 概念-语标一致性——确保英语概念与ASL手语语标之间建立稳定映射。同时设计了一套新的评估指标来分别衡量各话语连贯性维度。

Result: 在句子级和话语级数据集上的实验表明，该话语感知方法在空间一致性和实体跟踪方面显著优于仅句子级翻译，同时保持了具有竞争力的单句语标翻译质量。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了首个系统性的话语级文本到手语语标翻译框架及对应的评估方法，填补了手语翻译领域在话语层面研究的空白。

Abstract: Sign language processing systems have traditionally operated at the sentence level, ignoring critical discourse phenomena fundamental to sign language comprehension. We introduce DiscoSign, a computational approach for discourse-aware text to sign language gloss translation grounded in linguistic research. We address three key phenomena within our modular Large Language Model (LLM)-based translation framework: (i) spatial coreference resolution, where entities maintain consistent spatial locations throughout discourse; (ii) Question-Answer Clauses (QACs), pseudocleft structures serving specific discourse functions; and (iii) concept-gloss consistency, ensuring stable mappings between English concepts and American Sign Language (ASL) signs. Traditional translation metrics fail to capture discourse-level quality, so we introduce a suite of novel evaluation metrics designed to assess each dimension of discourse coherence addressed by our framework. Experiments on sentence-level and discourse-level datasets show that our approach for discourse-aware processing significantly improves spatial consistency and entity tracking relative to sentence-only translation, while maintaining competitive single-sentence gloss translation quality. Our work establishes the first systematic framework for discourse-level text to sign language gloss translation with corresponding evaluation methodology.

</details>


### [180] [User Feedback Provides a Unique Signal that LLMs Can not Detect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02859)
*Shachar Don-Yehiya,Leshem Choshen,Omri Abend*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文挑战了"用户反馈对LLM改进无效"的既有认知，指出其低效性源于当前评估范式的系统性偏差。作者通过合成数据与自然数据实验证明，基于反馈的模型修订在解决目标问题上的成功率显著高于无反馈的基线修订，并揭示了评估偏差根源：LLM裁判在面对仅因反馈才修正成功的结果时，常错误地偏好较差的基线输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来研究表明用户交互中自然产生的反馈信号具有噪声且难以有效利用，作者希望重新审视这一结论，验证用户反馈是否真正可作为LLM改进的高价值学习信号。

Method: 构建具有明确真值的合成数据以隔离反馈的有效性，同时利用自然主义数据验证发现在真实场景中的适用性；通过对比有/无反馈条件下模型修订的表现，并分析LLM裁判对修订结果的评估偏好来揭露评估偏差。

Result: 反馈引导的修订在解决特定问题上的成功率显著优于无反馈的基线修订；进一步发现，当模型因反馈而成功修正问题时，LLM裁判系统性地偏好未被修正的基线输出，而非已正确的修订结果，构成评估偏差的根源。

Conclusion: 用户反馈本质上仍是高度可用的改进信号，其表面上的低效性是评估机制偏差造成的假象；改进评估方法对于准确认识反馈的价值至关重要。

Abstract: Harnessing naturally occurring feedback from user interactions offers a promising learning signal for Large Language Models (LLMs). However, recent studies suggest this feedback is inherently noisy and difficult to leverage effectively. We challenge this conception by demonstrating that user feedback is a highly actionable signal for improvement, and that its perceived ineffectiveness stems from a systematic bias in current evaluation paradigms. To isolate the usefulness of feedback, we construct synthetic data with a definitive ground truth, alongside naturalistic data to validate that our findings hold in real-world scenarios. By comparing model revisions generated with and without access to feedback across both settings, we show that feedback-informed revisions resolve targeted issues at significantly higher rates than baseline revisions. Finally, we expose the root of the evaluation bias: when a model successfully fixes an issue exclusively due to feedback, LLM judges frequently fail to identify the genuinely corrected response, systematically preferring inferior baseline outputs instead.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [181] [RunSoC 2.0: Scheduling and Allocating Automotive Software Tasks to Hardware Partitions in Heterogeneous MPSoCs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01614)
*Daniel Krüger,Lucas Mauser,Stefan Wagner*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出RunSoC 2.0，一个面向异构MPSoC的早期设计空间探索框架，将任务调度与分配建模为多目标优化问题（最小化层次化内存预算违规与通信惩罚），支持CBC、CP-SAT和遗传算法等多种求解后端，并在Renesas R-Car V4H、NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin、TI TDA4VM等典型异构平台上以10-500任务规模验证，结果表明CP-SAT在强约束实时调度中性能最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集中式汽车架构将计算密集型工作负载整合到异构多处理器SoC上，产生严格的执行、内存和通信约束。RunSoC 1.0仅针对同构硬件上的分配问题，无法处理异构平台的处理器特定执行时间、集群级组织和领域特定处理属性，缺乏面向异构平台的早期任务调度与分配的探索工具。

Method: RunSoC 2.0扩展了1.0框架以支持异构平台，建模处理器特定执行时间、集群级组织和领域特定处理属性；将任务集表示为带严格端到端延迟与核亲和性约束的DAG；将任务调度与分配形式化为多目标优化问题（最小化层次化内存预算违规和片间/簇间通信惩罚）；支持CBC（精确法）、Google OR-Tools CP-SAT（约束规划）和遗传算法（元启发式）三种求解后端以便对比。

Result: 在10到500个任务的合成汽车任务集及Renesas R-Car V4H、NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin和TI TDA4VM平台上的评估表明：RunSoC 2.0能生成可行且最优的调度方案、暴露架构瓶颈并支持平台方案的快速比较；CP-SAT在紧约束硬实时调度实例中一致优于CBC和GA。

Conclusion: 通过引入簇感知的通信与内存建模，RunSoC 2.0提高了早期MPSoC分析的现实性，同时在大规模汽车工作负载下保持了实用的求解时间。

Abstract: Centralized automotive architectures increasingly consolidate compute-intensive workloads onto heterogeneous Multi-Processor System-on-Chip (MPSoC), creating strict execution, memory, and communication constraints. This paper presents RunSoC 2.0, a customizable framework for early-stage design-space exploration of task scheduling and allocation on heterogeneous MPSoCs. Building on RunSoC 1.0, which targeted allocation on homogeneous hardware, RunSoC 2.0 extends the framework to heterogeneous platforms by modeling processor-specific execution times, cluster-level organization, and domain-specific processing properties. It represents task sets as directed acyclic graphs (DAGs) subjected to strict end-to-end latency and core-affinity constraints, and formulates task scheduling and allocation as a multi-objective optimization problem that minimizes hierarchical memory-budget violations and inter-core/inter-cluster communication penalties. The framework supports multiple solving backends, including COIN-OR Branch and Cut (CBC), Google OR-Tools CP-SAT, and a Genetic Algorithm (GA), enabling comparative evaluation of exact, constraint-programming, and meta-heuristic approaches. We evaluate RunSoC 2.0 using synthetic automotive task sets ranging from 10 to 500 tasks, mapped to representative heterogeneous MPSoCs, including the Renesas R-Car V4H, NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin, and TI TDA4VM. The results show that RunSoC 2.0 can generate feasible and optimal schedules, expose architectural bottlenecks, and support rapid comparison of platform alternatives. Notably, CP-SAT consistently outperforms both CBC and the GA across tightly constrained hard real-time scheduling instances. By incorporating cluster-aware communication and memory modeling, RunSoC 2.0 improves the realism of early-stage MPSoC analysis while retaining practical solution times for large automotive workloads. (..)

</details>


### [182] [An Emerging NVM-Based On-Chip Training Architecture with Non-Ideality Mitigation Through Bipolar Weight Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01948)
*Peng Dang,Youna Huang,Yintao He,Huawei Li*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向片上训练的基于新型非易失存储器（eNVM）的非理想性优化加速器架构（NOVA），通过制作二维FeFET器件并建立实验校准的器件模型，首次提出非理想性规避训练（NAT）算法，引导权重收敛至eNVM最稳定的电导区域，有效缓解了器件非理想性导致的精度下降问题。实验结果显示，在严重器件不对称条件下，NAT相比基线方法平均提升15.1%的精度，NOVA架构相比GPU峰值能效平均提升约33.58倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习的高速发展对传统冯·诺依曼架构提出了巨大的能效挑战，基于eNVM的存内计算架构虽然被视为加速神经网络训练的有力方案，但其固有非理想性会导致电导更新偏离目标值，严重限制了片上训练的性能，因此需要新的解决方案来克服器件层面的非理想性障碍。

Method: 首先制备了二维FeFET器件并开发了经实验数据校准的电导调制行为模型；在此基础上，首次提出面向eNVM器件的非理想性规避训练（NAT）算法，通过引导权重收敛到eNVM器件最稳定的电导区域来缓解精度退化；最终构建了NOVA加速器架构实现片上训练。

Result: 在多个基准任务上，即使在严重器件不对称条件下，NAT相比基线方法平均提升15.1%的精度；NOVA架构相比GPU峰值能效平均获得约33.58倍的能效增益。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了通过器件层面的行为建模与算法层面的权重引导相结合，可以有效规避eNVM非理想性对片上训练精度的影响，为存内计算架构在神经网络训练中的实际应用提供了可行方案，同时实现了显著的能效提升。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of deep learning has presented significant energy efficiency challenges to the conventional von Neumann architecture. In-memory computing (IMC) architectures based on emerging non-volatile memory (eNVM) are widely regarded as a promising solution for accelerating neural network training due to their high parallelism and low power consumption. However, the intrinsic non-idealities of eNVM devices can cause conductance updates to deviate from target values, thereby limiting the performance of on-chip training. To address this challenge, this paper presents a Non-ideality Optimized eNVM Accelerator (NOVA) architecture for on-chip training. Specifically, we first fabricate a two-dimensional (2D) ferroelectric field-effect transistor (FeFET) and develop a conductance modulation behavioral model calibrated with experimental data. Building upon this device model, we propose, for the first time, a Non-ideality Avoidance Training (NAT) algorithm tailored for eNVM devices, which mitigates accuracy degradation by guiding weight convergence toward the most stable conductance regions of eNVM devices. Experimental results demonstrate that, even under severe device asymmetry, NAT improves the accuracy by an average of 15.1\% over the baseline methods across multiple benchmark tasks. Meanwhile, the NOVA achieves an average energy efficiency gain of approximately 33.58$\times$ compared with the peak energy efficiency of graphics processing units (GPUs).

</details>


### [183] [Atlas: Algorithm-Hardware Co-Design for On-Device City-Scale 3D Gaussian Splatting in VR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02352)
*He Zhu,Zheng Liu,Xingyang Li,Anbang Wu,Zihan Liu,Ruyang Li,Hui Wei,Yaqian Zhao,Jingwen Leng,Minyi Guo,Yu Feng*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: Atlas是一个面向移动VR设备的城市级3DGS渲染框架，通过分层内存卸载机制动态加载当前视角所需的Gaussian子集，实现无需互联网访问的可扩展渲染，较GPU基线提速18.5倍，较SOTA 3DGS加速器提速3.9倍，节省92.4%能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS在建筑领域受到广泛关注，但城市规模的3DGS在移动VR设备上实现仍面临重大挑战——大规模场景的内存需求远超当前移动GPU的内存容量，且需要支持离线渲染。

Method: 核心洞察：完整3DGS模型虽然庞大，但每一帧仅需当前姿态和细节层级（LoD）要求下的一小部分Gaussian子集。基于此，Atlas提出：(1)分层内存卸载机制，动态仅将必要的Gaussian数据加载入设备内存；(2)时间感知的LoD搜索以提升性能；(3)立体光栅化避免VR中的冗余计算；(4)可与现有3DGS加速器集成，硬件开销可忽略不计。

Result: Atlas较GPU基线实现18.5倍加速，较最先进的3DGS加速器实现3.9倍加速，同时获得92.4%的能耗节省。

Conclusion: Atlas证明了城市级3DGS渲染可以在移动VR设备上以高帧率和低能耗实现，且无需运行时互联网访问，并可与现有加速硬件无缝集成。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian splatting (3DGS) has drawn significant attention in the architectural community recently. However, enabling city scale 3DGS on mobile VR devices remains challenging, as the memory requirement of large scale scenes far exceeds the memory capacity of today's mobile GPUs. This paper presents Atlas, an on device city scale 3DGS rendering framework that enables scalable rendering without runtime Internet access. The key insight is that although the full 3DGS model is massive, each frame only requires a small subset of Gaussians under the current pose and level of detail requirement. Based on this insight, Atlas introduces a hierarchical memory offloading mechanism that dynamically loads only necessary Gaussian data into device memory. To further improve performance, Atlas proposes temporal aware LoD search and stereo rasterization to avoid redundant computation in VR. We further show that our technique can be integrated with existing 3DGS accelerators with negligible hardware overhead. Overall, Atlas achieves 18.5x speedup over the GPU baseline and 3.9x speedup over the state of the art 3DGS accelerators, with 92.4% energy savings.

</details>


### [184] [Batch Before You Time: Decision-Scoped Proxy Execution for Timing-Aware Logic Rewriting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02470)
*Pujun Su*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出BBYT方法，将一次重写决策的所有候选编译进单一作用域的零延迟镜像中，通过代理执行筛选候选，仅在必要时才调用带时序评估的完整链式流程，在保持与穷举时序选择一致性的同时显著降低候选选择时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SDF（标准延迟格式）标注的开关仿真能够区分功能等价重写中被延迟依赖的活动差异，但逐一评估每个候选包含时序、编译和回放三个昂贵步骤。零延迟代理可去除时序评估，但逐个候选生成代理的成本可能超过其节省的时序计算量。

Method: 提出Batch Before You Time（BBYT）方法：(1) 将一次重写决策的所有候选编译进一个作用域化的零延迟镜像；(2) 若代理结果存在良好分离的胜者则采纳之；(3) 否则回退到未修改的带时序评估完整链。

Result: 在12个反平衡的保留序列中，BBYT平均降低完整候选选择时间18.05%，设计级降幅为5.84%和30.25%且置信区间均高于零。在8,192次转换的C6288工作负载上，比逐候选代理启动快9.24%。在五个工作负载语料库中，消除了32.52%的时序候选评估，并在全部250个决策上匹配穷举时序选择结果。

Conclusion: 在按需的时序感知重写选择中，代理执行与保真度延续应共享相同的决策作用域，这既保证了代理筛选的效率优势，又通过回退机制保证了选择结果的正确性。

Abstract: Standard Delay Format (SDF)-annotated switching simulation distinguishes delay-dependent activity among functionally equivalent rewrites, but evaluating every candidate repeats timing, compilation, and replay. A zero-delay proxy can remove timed evaluations, but generating that proxy candidate by candidate can cost more than the timed work it saves. We present Batch Before You Time (BBYT), which compiles all candidates of one rewrite decision into one scoped zero-delay image and either commits a well-separated proxy winner or invokes the unchanged timed chain. Across 12 counterbalanced holdout sequences, BBYT reduces complete candidate-selection time by 18.05% on average; the design-level reductions are 5.84% and 30.25%, with both confidence intervals above zero. On a counterbalanced 8,192-transition C6288 workload, BBYT is 9.24% faster than the same gate executed with candidate-wise proxy launches. In the five-workload corpus, BBYT removes 32.52% of timed candidate evaluations and matches exhaustive timed selection on all 250 evaluated decisions. For on-demand timing-aware rewrite selection, proxy execution and fidelity continuation should use the same decision scope.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [185] [CliffRank: A Dual-Branch Framework for Activity-Cliff Ranking Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01673)
*Kewei Li,Rongying Zhang,Peiyu Yang,Zhongjian Wang,Qiuchen Zhao,Lan Huang,Fengfeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CliffRank模型，通过结合绝对活性回归、阈值列式损失和成对偏好一致性（PPC）损失来更好地利用活性标签，解决活性悬崖（activity cliff）排序难题。在三个抗菌肽数据集和三个小分子数据集上，CliffRank分别达到最高平均Spearman相关系数0.5393和0.6890。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 活性悬崖排序困难，因为局部结构微小变化可能引起巨大活性差异，且能够解析其潜在机制的高质量数据有限。现有方法未能充分利用可用的活性标签信息。

Method: CliffRank结合三种训练目标：(1)两个并行预测器的均方误差回归（绝对活性预测）；(2)阈值化列式损失（排序一致性）；(3)成对偏好一致性（PPC），即在偏好概率空间中对齐相对排序。分类器编码器采用ESM2-t12（肽）或PNA/MolCLR-GIN（小分子），PPC可在特定epoch后激活（如PNA在120 epochs后激活）。

Result: 在三个抗菌肽数据集上，CliffRank与ESM2-t12达到最高平均Spearman相关系数0.5393和平均Recall@50为21.4，但不同数据集的领先方法有所变化。在三个小分子数据集上，CliffRank与PNA（PPC在120 epochs后激活）达到最高平均Spearman相关系数0.6890，平均Recall@50为30.4，与ACANet-PNA持平。PPC结果也揭示了其实际限制。对称初始化改善了MolCLR-GIN的平均性能但未惠及所有目标；对无预训练权重的PNA，延迟PPC改善了部分指标，但没有任何调度能同时最优两个指标。

Conclusion: PPC损失对活性悬崖排序有显著帮助，但其效果存在实际边界。未来工作应评估更多靶点和抗菌肽系统，开发自适应的PPC调度策略，并在可用时整合蛋白质或膜上下文信息。

Abstract: Activity-cliff ranking remains difficult because local structural changes can cause large activity differences, while high-quality data that resolve the underlying mechanisms remain limited. To use available activity labels more effectively, we combine absolute-activity regression with ranking-consistency learning. CliffRank trains two parallel predictors with mean squared error, a thresholded listwise loss, and Pairwise Preference Consistency (PPC), which aligns relative ordering in the preference-probability space. On three antimicrobial peptide datasets, CliffRank with ESM2-t12 achieved the highest mean Spearman correlation of 0.5393 and mean Recall@50 of 21.4, although the leading method varied across individual datasets. On three small-molecule datasets, CliffRank with PNA, where PPC was activated after 120 epochs, achieved the highest mean Spearman correlation of 0.6890, while its mean Recall@50 of 30.4 matched that of ACANet-PNA. The PPC results also define its practical limits. Asymmetric initialization improved the MolCLR-GIN averages but did not improve every target. For PNA without pretrained weights, delayed PPC improved selected metrics, but no schedule was best for both mean Spearman correlation and mean Recall@50. Future work should evaluate more targets and antimicrobial peptide systems, develop adaptive PPC schedules, and incorporate protein or membrane context when available.

</details>


### [186] [WMLLM: Self-Evolving Optimization Agents via Predict-Then-Act World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01608)
*Zhongzheng Li,Qingsong Ran,Shikun Feng,Nian Ran,Wenhao Li,Xiaoyuan Zhang,Yue Wang,Xiaoguang Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出WMLLM，一种基于"先预测后行动"世界建模的自进化优化智能体框架，用于解决黑盒优化问题中样本效率低下的挑战。WMLLM通过大语言模型先预测有前景的优化方向，再生成候选解，结合多轮智能体细化、种群搜索和强化学习，在优化过程中持续精炼隐式世界模型与优化策略，在多目标分子优化基准上取得了领先结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑盒优化问题（如高维、弱结构化的搜索空间）因依赖直接候选生成或试错式细化而存在样本效率低下的问题。作者观察到LLM凭借其隐式知识能以不错的准确率预测候选结果的优劣，提出利用世界建模来在昂贵评估之前识别有前景的优化方向，从而提升搜索效率。

Method: 提出WMLLM自进化优化智能体框架，核心思想是"预测-然后-行动"（predict-then-act）世界建模：智能体首先预测有前景的优化方向，然后基于预测生成候选解。框架融合了三种关键机制：（1）智能体式多轮细化（agentic multi-turn refinement）；(2) 基于种群的搜索（population-based search）；(3) 强化学习。通过这些机制，WMLLM在搜索过程中同时精炼其隐式世界模型与优化策略，实现自我进化。

Result: 在黑盒优化任务（特别是多目标分子优化）上的实验表明，WMLLM显著提升了样本效率和最终优化性能。在受限评估预算下，WMLLM在多目标分子优化基准上取得了最先进结果。

Conclusion: 基于LLM隐式世界模型构建"先预测后行动"的优化框架是提升黑盒优化样本效率的有效途径。通过让模型在优化过程中自进化地精炼世界模型与策略，WMLLM在有限评估预算下实现了优异的优化性能，验证了该范式的可行性与潜力。

Abstract: Black-box optimization problems remain challenging because of large, weakly structured, and high-dimensional search spaces. Existing methods often suffer from poor sample efficiency because they rely on direct candidate generation or trial-and-error refinement. A natural way to improve search efficiency is to use world modeling, which can help identify promising optimization directions before costly evaluation. Large language models can predict the outcomes of these candidates with nontrivial accuracy because of their implicit knowledge. Motivated by this observation, we propose WMLLM, a self-evolving optimization-agent framework based on predict-then-act world modeling. The agent first predicts promising directions and then acts to generate candidates. Combined with agentic multi-turn refinement, population-based search, and reinforcement learning, WMLLM refines both its implicit world model and its optimization strategy during search. Experiments on black-box optimization tasks, especially multi-objective molecular optimization, show that WMLLM improves sample efficiency and final optimization performance. On the multi-objective molecular optimization benchmark, WMLLM achieves state-of-the-art results under a limited evaluation budget.

</details>


### [187] [DiDrive: A Risk-Aware Hierarchical Diffusion Framework for Safe Offline Reinforcement Learning in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01609)
*Qisong Guo,Jingtang Chen,Zhilin Chen,Pei Xu,Mingjian Fu,Wenxi Liu,Yuanlong Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DiDrive，一种面向自动驾驶的分布引导式离线扩散强化学习框架，由风险感知分层扩散架构（RHDif）和3DICE策略优化范式组成，在CARLA基准上高密度交通场景（60辆车）中达到85%的成功率和4295.68的平均奖励，优于IQL、CQL和Diffusion-QL等基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型虽能有效捕捉多模态行为先验，但离线强化学习策略仍面临分布偏移、重尾风险信号、OOD动作生成和高维状态冗余等挑战，亟需一种能够同时解决上述问题的安全驾驶决策框架。

Method: DiDrive包含两个协同组件：（1）RHDif架构在状态空间中使用低层风险门控编码器和高层上下文调制器来过滤环境冗余并聚焦安全关键威胁；（2）3DICE策略优化在动作空间中通过样本内标定引导、时空优化和基于集成的候选排序来缓解OOD过估计和梯度振荡问题。

Result: 在CARLA基准评估中，DiDrive在复杂高密度交通场景（60辆车）下优于IQL、CQL和Diffusion-QL等基线，达到85%的成功率和4295.68的平均奖励。

Conclusion: DiDrive为安全自动驾驶决策提供了一条稳健的路径，有效解决了离线扩散RL中的分布偏移、OOD动作生成和高维状态冗余等核心问题。

Abstract: While diffusion models effectively capture multimodal behavioral priors for autonomous driving, offline reinforcement learning (RL) policies remain susceptible to distribution shift, heavy-tailed risk signals, out-of-distribution (OOD) action generation, and high-dimensional state redundancy. To address these challenges, we propose DiDrive, a distribution-guided offline diffusion framework featuring two synergistic components: the Risk-Aware Hierarchical Diffusion (RHDif) architecture and the 3DICE policy optimization paradigm. In the state space, RHDif utilizes a low-level risk-gated encoder and a high-level contextual modulator to filter environmental redundancy and focus on safety-critical threats. In the action space, 3DICE mitigates OOD overestimation and gradient oscillation through in-sample calibrated guidance, spatiotemporal optimization, and ensemble-based candidate ranking. Evaluations on the CARLA benchmark demonstrate DiDrive's superiority over baselines like IQL, CQL, and Diffusion-QL, particularly in complex, high-density traffic scenarios with 60 vehicles, where it achieves an 85% success rate and a 4295.68 average reward, providing a robust pathway for safe autonomous driving decision-making.

</details>


### [188] [Prompt-Space Meta-Learning Does Not Transfer Across Users: A Frozen-LLM Negative Result](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01615)
*Liam Byrne,David Dylan,Orla Fitzgerald,Eoin Doyle,Ciara Nolan,Padraig Lynch,Sinead Gallagher*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过Muse（元学习用户适应的共享演化）框架，质疑冻结大语言模型的提示空间元学习是否真正编码了跨用户的迁移性适应，而非仅泛化的指令质量。实验表明Muse在LaMP-2和LaMP-3基准上相较于种子提示、错配对控制组均无显著提升，且被普通少样本检索显著压制。作者将失败归因于"元目标坍缩"：元验证目标对用户-支持对是否是真实对应的统计不敏感，因此无法被优化为可迁移的适应策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究常将LLM个性化框架设定为提示空间的元学习问题，但很少检验所优化的元目标是否真正编码了跨用户的迁移性适应，还是仅反映了通用指令质量与验证集上的过拟合，本文旨在严格区分这两者。

Method: 提出Muse框架：通过反思式提示演化在元训练用户群体上演化单一共享适应提示，冻结后在留出用户上零样本应用；配对照实验隔离学习信号与提示措辞、选择偏差的混淆；引入结构破坏对照（元训练于错配的用户-支持对）与不变性检验来诊断元目标。

Result: 在200名留出用户的两个标准个性化基准（LaMP-2分类、LaMP-3评分）上，Muse优于未演化的种子提示及错配支持对训练的对照组均不显著（评分任务上还被少样本检索以Delta MAE +0.175, p<0.001显著超越）；元验证目标对支持对应的真实性不敏感（LaMP-2 p=0.555，LaMP-3 p=0.622），证实元目标坍缩。

Conclusion: 提示空间的元学习在个性化场景下未能学到可迁移的跨用户适应，其优化信号主要奖励指令润色与验证过拟合。种子提示、错配支持与不变性预言机对照构成了可复用协议，用于将来分离"学到的适应"与混淆因素。

Abstract: Personalizing a frozen large language model (LLM) to individual users is often framed as a meta-learning problem in prompt space: each user is a task, and one seeks a shared natural-language adaptation policy that, given a handful of the user's labeled interactions, configures the frozen model for that user. The framing is attractive because it is backbone-agnostic and reuses the machinery of prompt optimization, yet the field rarely tests whether the optimized meta-objective encodes transferable cross-user adaptation rather than generic instruction quality. We study this question with Muse (Meta-learned User-adaptation via Shared Evolution), which evolves a single shared adaptation prompt over a meta-train user population by reflective prompt evolution, freezes it, and applies it zero-shot to held-out users; matched controls isolate learning from confounds of phrasing and selection. On two standard personalization benchmarks (LaMP-2 categorization and LaMP-3 rating) over 200 held-out users each, Muse does not significantly improve on its own un-evolved seed prompt or on a structure-broken control that meta-trains on mismatched user-support pairs, and is dominated by plain few-shot retrieval on the rating task (Delta MAE +0.175, p < 0.001). We attribute these outcomes to a single mechanism, meta-objective collapse: the meta-validation objective is statistically invariant to whether the user-support correspondence is genuine (p=0.555 on LaMP-2, p=0.622 on LaMP-3), so it cannot be optimized into transferable adaptation and instead rewards instruction polish and validation overfitting. The seed-prompt, wrong-support, and invariance-oracle controls form a reusable protocol that separates learned adaptation from these confounds.

</details>


### [189] [Efficient Context-Limited Telescope Bibliography Classification for the WASP-2025 Shared Task Using SciBERT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01647)
*Madhusudhana Naidu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于SciBERT的望远镜文献自动分类方法，将科学论文分为四个类别（科学、仪器、提及、非望远镜），在WASP-2025排行榜上排名第一，宏F1分数达到0.89，尽管面临512 token的上下文长度限制和有限的计算资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 创建望远镜书目是评估天文台科学影响和确保天文学可复现性的关键环节，但这一过程目前仍主要依赖人工操作，资源消耗大、效率低。因此需要自动化的分类方法来解决这一瓶颈。

Method: 采用基于SciBERT的方法对科学论文进行四分类（science、instrumentation、mention、not telescope）。分析了截断（truncation）对模型性能的影响，并讨论比较了截断、分块（chunking）和长上下文模型之间的权衡。

Result: 在WASP-2025排行榜上取得第一名，宏F1分数达到0.89。即使超过一半的样本超出token限制，SciBERT的领域对齐能力仍能保证稳健的分类性能。

Conclusion: SciBERT的领域预训练能力使得即使面对严格的上下文长度限制也能实现高效鲁棒的分类；研究揭示了科学文本策展中效率边界上的关键洞察，为截断、分块和长上下文模型之间的取舍提供了指导。

Abstract: The creation of telescope bibliographies is a crucial part of assessing the scientific impact of observatories and ensuring reproducibility in astronomy. This task involves identifying, categorizing, and linking scientific publications that reference or use specific telescopes. However, this process remains largely manual and resource intensive. In this work, we present an efficient SciBERT-based approach for automatic classification of scientific papers into four categories - science, instrumentation, mention, and not telescope. Despite strict context-length constraints (maximum 512 tokens) and limited compute resources, our approach achieved a macro F1 score of 0.89, ranking at the top of the WASP-2025 leaderboard. We analyze the effect of truncation and show that even with half the samples exceeding the token limit, SciBERT's domain alignment enables robust classification. We discuss trade-offs between truncation, chunking, and long-context models, providing insights into the efficiency frontier for scientific text curation.

</details>


### [190] [Sim2Signal: Sim-to-Real Benchmarks for Traffic Signal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01676)
*Ferdous Al Rafi,Susrik Mukherjee,Latika Liladhar Dekate,Jennifer Yawa Lavoe,Huaiyuan Yao,Shlok Mohanty,Longchao Da,Xuesong Zhou,Hua Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Sim2Signal，一个将交通信号控制中强化学习Sim-to-Real差距系统地分解为观测、动作、转移和奖励四种差距（对应MDP的四个组成部分）的基准测试框架，并在10个从真实场景校准的路网、33种差距设置下评估18种缓解方法，发现差距估计类方法整体优于域随机化和不变表征方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交通信号控制中的RL策略在仿真器中表现出色，但部署到现实世界时性能下降（Sim-to-Real gap）。然而该差距来源的各自影响程度以及现有缓解方法的可靠性尚未被充分理解，领域内缺乏系统测量差距和评估缓解方法的标准化基准。

Method: 将Sim-to-Real差距分解为观测、动作、转移和奖励四种差距，分别对应底层MDP的四个组成部分的失配；在共享协议下单独诱导每种差距。基于5个真实场景构建10个校准网络，在33种差距设置下评估18种缓解方法×2种基础控制器。

Result: 直接迁移在四种差距来源上均持续导致性能下降，但下降严重程度并不能预测缓解方法的有效性；缓解效果强烈依赖于网络和差距设置——除动作差距外，在某一情形有效的治理方法在另一情形可能失效；最有效的方法通常是估计差距改变的内容，而非通过域随机化或不变表征使策略对差距不敏感。

Conclusion: Sim2Signal为该领域提供了系统性评估Sim-to-Real差距及缓解方法的标砖基准，揭示了差距本质与缓解方法效用之间的复杂关系，为更有效的跨域交通信号控制策略设计提供了方向指导。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning achieves strong traffic signal control performance in simulation, yet policies trained in simulators often fail once deployed in the real world, a failure known as the Sim-to-Real gap. When RL is applied to traffic signal control, this gap arises from several sources: sensing, action execution, traffic dynamics, and the control objective. Their relative impact and the reliability of existing Sim-to-Real mitigation methods remain insufficiently understood, and the field lacks a standard benchmark for systematically measuring the gap and evaluating mitigation methods. We present Sim2Signal, a benchmark that decomposes the Sim-to-Real gap into observation, action, transition, and reward gaps, corresponding to mismatches in the four components of the underlying MDP, and induces each gap in isolation under a shared protocol. We evaluate 18 mitigation methods on 2 base controllers, across 33 gap settings and 10 calibrated networks built from 5 real-world locations. We find that direct transfer consistently degrades performance across all four gap sources, but the severity of the degradation does not predict the effectiveness of mitigation. Instead, mitigation effectiveness depends strongly on the network and gap setting: outside the action gap, a method that helps in one case may fail in another. The most effective methods generally estimate what the gap changes, rather than make the policy insensitive through domain randomization or invariant representations. Our code is available at https://github.com/Red-Pheonix/Sim2RealTSCBenchMark

</details>


### [191] [A Survey on Self-Improving Test-Time Intelligence: Feedback-Driven Adapting, Learning, and Scaling at Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01679)
*Shuaicheng Niu,Guohao Chen,Yaofo Chen,Zhiquan Wen,Jinwu Hu,Zeshuai Deng,Deyu Chen,Shuhai Zhang,Renjie Chen,Zihao Lian,Shoukai Xu,Gang Dai,Yunbei Zhang,Wei Luo,Yifan Zhang,Mingkui Tan,Cheng Deng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"反馈驱动的测试时智能"(Feedback-driven Test-Time Intelligence, TTI)这一统一视角,将测试时自适应、测试时学习和测试时扩展三种范式整合起来,系统梳理了AI系统在部署阶段通过利用测试时信息和额外计算实现自我改进的相关研究,并综述了视觉、语言、多模态、生成模型、机器人及医疗等领域的方法论、代表性应用与开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统在部署阶段自我改进的能力日益重要,但当前研究沿着"修改模型状态(测试时自适应/学习)"和"增加推理资源(测试时扩展)"两条独立路线发展,不同社区使用不同术语,导致这些方向之间的联系难以被看清,碎片化的研究阻碍了统一的认知框架建立。

Method: 本文采用综述性方法,提出反馈驱动的测试时智能(TTI)作为统一视角来理解部署阶段的改进机制;利用该视角关联测试时自适应、测试时学习和测试时扩展,阐明三者的区别及其在混合系统中的交叉融合,并系统回顾各范式下的方法论、代表性应用与开放挑战。

Result: 文章识别并梳理了测试时自适应、测试时学习与测试时扩展三类范式之间既相互区别又日益重叠的关系,特别是在混合系统中的协同;建立了统一的术语和概念基础,覆盖视觉、语言、多模态、生成模型、机器人及医疗等多个应用领域的代表性方法。

Conclusion: 统一框架有助于连接此前分散的研究思想,为自改进AI系统的研究提供清晰的概念基础和路线图;未来该领域将进一步向混合系统演进,测试时学习与测试时扩展的融合将推动更强大的推理时自我改进能力。

Abstract: The ability of AI systems to improve their behavior during deployment is becoming increasingly important.
