Their objective is therefore stated only in terms of the surrogate instead of the true simulator, which may fail for inaccurate areas in parameter space where the surrogate was never trained. We instead solve SDE by expectation maximization: an E-step trains an amortized posterior on fresh simulations from the current source estimate, and an M-step refits the source to the average of that posterior over the observed data. We give two parameterizations, (1) separate source and posterior flows and (2) a single shared conditional flow. We evaluate our method on three benchmark tasks under both broad and misspecified initial priors. Both improve on existing fixed surrogate approaches and on iterated variants of each, most clearly on Lotka--Volterra, where no baseline falls below 0.96 data-space C2ST while our methods reach 0.64-0.68 in three of four initial-prior settings.

</details>


### [258] [Oracle, will I ever learn? A study of prediction convergence and complementarity across link prediction models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02638)
*Guillaume Méroué,Fabien Gandon,Pierre Monnin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究知识图谱链接预测模型中模型互补性的问题，通过oracle方法（每个查询选择最优预测）来度量互补性的上界，发现不同模型确实捕获互补知识，但这种互补性随模型数量增加迅速饱和，留下无法解决的查询子集，揭示了模型组合的潜力与根本性局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同的链接预测模型（甚至是同一模型的不同训练运行）对同一查询会产生显著不同的预测结果，表明模型对底层知识的捕获存在变异性。由此引发一个根本问题：不同模型究竟在多大程度上捕获了互补的知识，以及通过组合这些模型又能恢复多少知识？理解这一点对于构建鲁棒的Web应用（如搜索、问答和推荐系统）至关重要。

Method: 提出通过oracle性能来度量模型互补性：对于每个查询，oracle从一组候选模型中选取最佳预测，从而提供模型组合可实现性能的上界。在多种架构和多个基准数据集上进行实验，比较单个模型与其oracle之间的性能差距。

Result: 发现单个模型与其oracle之间存在显著差距，表明不同模型确实捕获了互补知识。然而，这种互补性在增加更多模型时迅速饱和，即使使用大量模型，仍有一小部分查询无法被任何模型解决。

Conclusion: 这些发现揭示了模型互补性的潜力，但也暴露了当前链接预测模型集体可恢复知识的根本性局限，凸显了进一步研究以构建鲁棒Web应用的必要性。

Abstract: Knowledge graphs have become an important source of structured knowledge for Web applications, including search, question answering, and recommender systems. In these applications, link prediction can serve either as a prediction task itself or as a means to enrich incomplete knowledge graphs for downstream tasks. Interestingly, different link prediction models, or even different training runs of the same model, can produce substantially different predictions for the same query. This suggests a variability in the capture of the underlying knowledge by models, thus raising a fundamental question: to what extent do different models capture complementary knowledge, and how much of this knowledge could be recovered by combining them? We propose to measure model complementarity through the performance of an oracle that, for each query, selects the best prediction among a considered set of models, hence providing an upper bound on the performance achievable through model combination. Across several architectures and benchmarks, we find a substantial gap between individual models and their oracle, revealing that different models capture complementary knowledge. Yet, this complementarity rapidly saturates as more models are added, leaving a persistent subset of queries unsolved even by a large number of models. These findings reveal both the potential of model complementarity and a fundamental limit to what current link prediction models can collectively recover; thereby highlighting the need for further research to build robust Web applications.

</details>


### [259] [Differentiable Electricity-Market Clearing for Gradient-Based Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02646)
*Luca Mungo,Maarten P. Scholl,Arnau Quera-Bofarull*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将市场出清建模为可微分优化层，通过反向模式自动微分将规划成本梯度传播回规划方案，使大规模数据中心选址规划问题可用梯度优化求解。在合成电网上的50MW负载分配实验中，梯度优化在连续分配上几乎精确恢复最优解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型数据中心规划困难在于：设施规模会影响其支付的电力价格，而电价由市场出清（一个随运行条件反复求解的约束优化问题）决定。仅模拟市场可以评估候选方案，但无法告诉规划者如何改进方案，缺乏可微的梯度信号来指导优化。

Method: 将市场出清视为可微分优化层：前向传播求解市场出清，反向模式自动微分将规划成本通过出清价格反向传播至规划方案。先在有限差分上验证梯度正确性，然后应用于两个合成网络、6个候选母线、36个运行状态下的50MW数据中心负载分配问题，并与穷举组合对比。

Result: 梯度优化几乎精确恢复连续分配的最优解，最坏情况下目标值与穷举枚举的差距仅为最佳与最差单站点成本差异的2.3%和8.5%。系统性误差出现在站点应关闭的成本附近：对离散站点数量的平滑松弛导致站点逐渐缩小而非关闭，使离散转换出现延迟。

Conclusion: 可微分市场出清将市场感知的数据中心规划转化为梯度可搜索的问题，为大规模基础设施规划提供了一种新的可微优化范式。

Abstract: Planning a large data center is difficult because a facility big enough to matter changes the electricity prices it will pay. Those prices are set by market clearing, a constrained optimization problem solved anew in every operating condition. However, simulating the market tells a planner how a candidate plan performs but not how to improve it. Here we treat market clearing as a differentiable optimization layer: each forward pass solves the market, and reverse-mode automatic differentiation propagates the planning cost back through the cleared prices to the plan. After validating these gradients against finite differences, we apply them to a concrete problem: allocating 50 MW of data-center load across six candidate buses in two synthetic networks, under a fixed cost per active site, evaluated over 36 operating states. Judged against exhaustive enumeration of all site combinations, gradient optimization recovers the continuous allocations almost exactly, with worst-case objective gaps of 2.3\% and 8.5\% of the cost difference between the best and worst single site. Its one systematic error is instructive: near the costs at which a site should close, the smooth relaxation of the discrete site count shrinks the site rather than closing it, so discrete transitions arrive late. Differentiable market clearing thus turns market-aware planning into a problem gradients can search.

</details>


### [260] [Unfolding the Leech Lattice: Fused Multi-Shell Decoding and VRAM Layouts for 2-Bit LLM Weights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02652)
*Pier-Jean Malandrino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为Leech-lattice 2-bit向量量化提供了首个多壳层解码器实现，并测量了其在batch=1解码阶段GEMV中的服务成本。研究区分了VRAM内码率与磁盘码率两种设计轴，通过四种位精确布局对比证明二进制位平面布局在带宽恒定时优于one-hot掩码（4.80 bits/weight，为FP16的2.15倍加速）。部署的QTIP格形(trellis)核比所服务的Leech布局少读2.40倍字节且快2.27倍。在4B/8B/14B模型上端到端分别获得1.11x、1.29x、1.41x收益，4B模型的int8输出头版本达到87.0 tok/s（占用2.60 GB），质量代价（4B下困惑度1.38x、MMLU损失14.7点）随模型增大而缩小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Leech-lattice向量量化在自有的2-bit评估协议下拥有最强报告质量，但其核只解码单个壳层(shell)，而该码率所需的实际部署需要多壳层解码器——此前没有现成实现。作者旨在补齐这一缺失，并系统测量多壳层解码路径在真实服务条件下的成本。

Method: (1) 构造301类完整码本的serving路径：离线展开为GPU布局，配合无warp divergence的融合反量化+矩阵向量乘核（用f64验证）；(2) 将VRAM内码率与磁盘码率分离为独立设计轴，在同一进程中以四种位精确布局对比，验证二进制位平面在字节带宽与速度上均优于one-hot掩码；(3) 在同一进程内并跑AWQ(4-bit)与QTIP(2-bit格形)的GEMV核做基线对比；(4) 通过与无权重控制组及FP16在不同内存层次上的对比，界定延迟有效包络。

Result: 二进制位平面布局在恒定带宽下达到4.80 bits/weight、FP16的2.15x性能；低于4.3 bits时进入第二非规则流，3.6 bits处解码不再只是移位和掩码。QTIP格形核较所服务的布局少读2.40倍字节、快2.27倍，时间差距与流量差距一致——这是码本过大无法放入查找表的代价。格形核仍快于无权重控制组，说明发射几何设定了性能下限；在第二种内存层次上所有格形分支均低于FP16。输出头一致时，端到端增益为4B/8B/14B下的1.11x/1.29x/1.41x；int8输出头下served 4B达87.0 tok/s于2.60 GB。质量代价在4B为1.38x困惑度和14.7个MMLU点，随规模增大而缩小。

Conclusion: 作者论证了Leech-lattice多壳层解码在服务路径上的可行性并量化了其开销，但指出其延迟瓶颈主要源于"解码码本过大而无法放入查找表"的固有代价，而非单纯的读取流量。格形(trellis/QTIP)核在字节读取与速度上均显著占优。因此Leech-lattice格式在端到端推理中的竞争力意味着需在质量收益与解码结构开销之间权衡，而这一质量差距随模型规模扩大而收窄。

Abstract: Leech-lattice vector quantization holds the strongest reported 2-bit quality under its own evaluation protocol. Its kernel decodes one shell; we found no implementation of the multi-shell decoder the rate requires. This paper supplies one and measures its serving cost for decode-phase GEMV at batch 1. First, a serving path for the full 301-class codebook: an offline expansion into GPU layouts and a fused dequantize-plus-matvec kernel reading them without warp divergence, verified against f64. Second, the in-VRAM rate is a design axis distinct from the on-disk rate. Four bit-exact layouts timed in one process show binary bit planes beating one-hot masks on size and speed at constant bandwidth (4.80 bits per weight, 2.15x FP16). Below 4.3 bits a second, irregular stream enters; at 3.6 the decode stops being shifts and masks. Third, deployed four-bit (AWQ) and two-bit (QTIP) GEMV kernels run in the same process. The trellis kernel reads 2.40x fewer bytes than our served layout and runs 2.27x faster at near-equal fractions of their byte bounds: the time gap tracks the traffic gap, the price of unfolding a codebook too large for a lookup table. Fourth, the validity envelope: the trellis kernel outruns our no-weights control, so our launch geometry sets that floor, and on a second memory hierarchy every lattice arm falls below FP16. With the output head held identical across arms, the kernel-and-format path gains 1.11x, 1.29x and 1.41x end to end at 4B, 8B and 14B; with an int8 output head the served 4B reaches 87.0 tok/s in 2.60 GB. The quality cost, 1.38x perplexity and 14.7 MMLU points at 4B, shrinks across the three sizes measured.

</details>


### [261] [LoRA-TSD: Tangent-Space Spectral Descent for LoRA via Muon-Style Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02734)
*Dmitrii Andriianov,Andrey Veprikov,Aleksandr Beznosikov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LoRA-TSD优化器，将每个LoRA步骤视为固定秩矩阵流形上的切向量，在Muon内执行谱范数最速下降步，并通过LoRA参数化固有的回缩映射回因子。回缩比传统截断SVD回缩便宜最多2.8倍，并在切投影梯度（黎曼梯度）度量下首次给出LoRA-Pro和LoRA-TSD的全局收敛保证。在六个基准和多种规模模型上超越所有对比LoRA优化器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准LoRA微调中两个因子独立训练，更新过程忽略了其诱导的低秩权重变化的几何结构，导致优化效率欠佳。现有流形方法依赖昂贵的截断SVD回缩，缺乏考虑LoRA参数化几何的高效且理论可证的最优优化器。

Method: 1) 将每个LoRA步骤建模为固定秩矩阵流形的切向量；2) 在该切空间内执行Muon的谱范数最速下降步；3) 通过LoRA参数化固有的回缩将结果映射回因子（比SVD回缩便宜最多2.8倍）；4) 证明Frobenius范数版本的代理方法可恢复LoRA-Pro；5) 识别切投影梯度（黎曼梯度）为LoRA训练的平稳性度量，并据此给出全局收敛保证。

Result: 在Llama-3.2-1B、Llama-3.1-8B和Qwen3-32B上，跨六个常识推理和自然语言推理基准，LoRA-TSD在所有对比LoRA优化器中表现最优，且对适配器秩的选择保持鲁棒性。回缩运算比截断SVD回缩快最多2.8倍。

Conclusion: LoRA-TSD通过将流形几何融入LoRA优化，提供了更高效的回缩运算和理论收敛保证，同时在实际基準上优于现有LoRA优化器且对秩鲁棒，为LoRA微调提供了新的高效优化范式。

Abstract: Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) is the standard way to fine-tune large models, yet when its two factors are trained independently, the update ignores the geometry of the low-rank weight change it induces. We introduce LoRA-TSD, an optimizer that treats every LoRA step as a tangent vector of the fixed-rank matrix manifold and takes the spectral-norm steepest-descent step of Muon inside that tangent space, mapping the result back to the factors through a retraction native to the LoRA parametrization. The step avoids expensive operations on full weight matrices, and its retraction is up to $2.8\times$ cheaper than the truncated-SVD retraction used by prior manifold methods. We prove that the Frobenius-norm version of our surrogate recovers LoRA-Pro, and we identify the tangent-projected gradient, the Riemannian gradient of the manifold, as the stationarity measure natural to LoRA training and computable from the factor gradients alone. Under this measure we give the first global convergence guarantees for both LoRA-Pro and LoRA-TSD, with rates that drive the factor-gradient norms to zero. Across six commonsense and natural-language-inference benchmarks with Llama-3.2-1B, Llama-3.1-8B and Qwen3-32B, LoRA-TSD outperforms every competing LoRA optimizer and stays robust to the adapter rank. Code is available at https://github.com/brain-lab-research/LoRA-TSD.

</details>


### [262] [Do Tabular Foundation Models Know Physics? Contamination, Units, and the Deterministic Limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02766)
*Wassim Tenachi,Yashar Hezaveh,Laurence Perreault Levasseur,Pierre-Luc Bacon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文直接探测表格基础模型(TFM)的先验是否包含物理知识，在316个物理方程采样的数据集上对比4个TFM与6个基线模型。TFM在开箱和调优后均表现优异，但其先验既无法表示无噪声机制，也无法表示物理单位，因此它们虽能插值物理规律，尚不能作为物理模型使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格格式承载了大多数物理测量数据，表格基础模型（TFM）从大量数据中学习先验分布，但与语言模型从文本学习类似，需要搞清楚TFM是否在学习过程中获得了物理知识。由于TFM本质上是贝叶斯模型，问题就转化为其先验中包含了什么。

Method: 对四种TFM（TabPFN-3、TabICLv2、TabDPT和Real-TabPFN-2.5）和六种基线模型，在从316个物理方程采样的数据集上进行域内和域外评估，并进行开箱与调优两种设置下的对比。

Result: TFM在所有设置下（开箱和调优，域内和域外）均优于基线模型；但实验表明TFM的先验既不包含无噪声机制表达能力，也不具备物理单位的表征能力。

Conclusion: TFM能够对物理数据进行有效的插值预测，展现出超越专门基线的强大性能，但其内部表示尚未达到可用于物理建模的程度——缺乏对无噪声机制和物理单位的严格表征，因此只能作为数据驱动的物理插值器而非真正的物理模型。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models (TFMs) learn to fill in tables the way language models fill in text, and tables are arguably the format in which most physical measurement arrives. Did they learn any physics in the process? They are Bayesian by construction, so the question is what their prior contains. We probe it directly, evaluating four of them (TabPFN-3, TabICLv2, TabDPT and Real-TabPFN-2.5) against six baselines on datasets sampled from 316 physical equations, in and out of domain. TFMs dominate, out of the box and after tuning. But we show that their prior can represent neither a noiseless mechanism nor physical units, which is why they interpolate physics without yet being able to act as physical models.

</details>


### [263] [Cliff: Learning Process Rewards from the First Mistake](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02817)
*Peixuan Han,Runhui Wang,Ketan Ramaneti,Jie Hao,Gerald Friedland,Chris Kong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Cliff是一种通过利用现成LLM作为教师模型识别推理轨迹中第一个错误位置，将轨迹分解为正确前缀和错误后缀，从而实现token级奖励塑造的RLVR策略；在12个场景中比on-policy distillation提升15%，比GRPO提升7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR在LLM后训练中虽然强大，但依赖粗粒度的结果奖励，对中间推理过程缺乏细粒度指导。现有方法如过程奖励建模和on-policy distillation引入了额外约束（依赖专门奖励模型或要求师生推理模式相同）。作者观察到一旦推理过程首次出错，后续评估信息有限，因此需要一种更简单通用的奖励塑造方式来提供细粒度监督。

Method: Cliff利用现成的LLM作为教师，在每次rollout中识别第一个错误（first mistake）。将整个推理轨迹自然划分为正确前缀（correct prefix）和错误后缀（incorrect suffix），然后将此信号转换为token级优势：对正确前缀赋予正优势，对错误后缀给予负反馈。

Result: 在12个不同场景的实验表明，Cliff一致性地提升了推理性能，比on-policy distillation高出15%，比标准GRPO高出7%，且即使采用能力一般的教师模型也能取得良好效果。此外，论文还分析了ground truth在Cliff中的角色及其训练动态。

Conclusion: Cliff被确立为一种简单、通用且有效的RLVR改进方法，通过更丰富的细粒度监督信号提升推理能力。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has emerged as a powerful paradigm for large language model (LLM) post-training, but its reliance on coarse outcome rewards leads to limited guidance on intermediate reasoning processes. Existing approaches such as process reward modeling and on-policy distillation introduce additional constraints, such as reliance on a specialized reward model or assuming identical reasoning patterns between teacher and student. Nevertheless, we observe that once a reasoning process first goes wrong, evaluating the subsequent reasoning provides limited additional information, as it is already conditioned on an invalid prefix. Therefore, we propose Cliff, a reward shaping strategy that utilizes an off-the-shelf LLM as a teacher to identify the first mistake in each rollout. As a result, the rollout is naturally decomposed into two parts: a correct prefix and an incorrect suffix. Cliff then converts this signal into token-level advantages, assigning positive advantages for the correct prefix and negative feedback afterward. Experiments across 12 different scenarios demonstrate that Cliff consistently improves reasoning performance, outperforming on-policy distillation by 15% and standard GRPO by 7%, even with teachers of modest capability. Furthermore, we analyse the role of ``ground truth'' in Cliff and investigate its training dynamics. These results establish Cliff as a simple, general and effective approach for improving RLVR with richer, fine-grained supervision.

</details>


### [264] [UE5M3 FP4 Block Scaling for Stable Language Model Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02846)
*Robert Hu,Carlo Luschi,Paul Balanca*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种更简单的4位浮点（FP4）预训练方案：将E2M1有效载荷与无符号E5M3块缩放配对，省去了Transformer Engine所需的随机Hadamard变换（RHT）和BF16最终层豁免，简化为周期性张量缩放+选择性随机舍入（应用于反向梯度）。在一个8B模型上预训练近1900亿token，相比Transformer Engine方案取得了更低的训练损失和验证损失，量化推理下游指标更高，且消融实验显示模型体token吞吐量提升21.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稳定的4位浮点（FP4）预训练一直很困难，因为E2M1有效载荷只能表示较窄的幅值范围。现有的NVIDIA Transformer Engine方案虽然通过当前张量缩放、随机Hadamard变换（RHT）和BF16最终层解决了这一问题，但在FP4矩阵乘法之外增加了额外计算开销且配方复杂，亟需一种更简洁高效的方案。

Method: 1) 将E2M1有效载荷与无符号E5M3（U5）块缩放配对，利用其更宽的幅值范围实现周期性张量缩放；2) 对反向梯度采用选择性随机舍入（selective stochastic rounding）；3) 完全省略RHT；4) 在所有合格的内层线性层中使用FP4，不设BF16最终块豁免。

Result: 1) 在Nemotron-H 8B模型上预训练近1900亿token，相比Transformer Engine方案取得更低的最终窗口训练损失和（在各自量化推理策略下的）验证损失（以held-out负对数似然衡量）；2) 量化推理下游点估计在全部三个报告聚合指标上更高；3) 原生NV执行消融实验中，联合移除RHT和BF16最终块豁免后，模型体token吞吐量提升21.2%。

Conclusion: 研究表明端到端软件模拟的FP4预训练可以用更简单的配方实现且效果更好，验证了U5块缩放方案的优越性，并为原生支持U5块缩放的硬件提供了有力动机。

Abstract: Stable 4-bit floating-point (FP4) pretraining is difficult because the E2M1 payload represents only a narrow range of magnitudes. NVIDIA's Transformer Engine \nv{} recipe addresses this with current-tensor scaling, a randomized Hadamard transform (RHT), and bfloat16 (BF16) final layers, adding work outside the FP4 matrix multiplications. We instead pair E2M1 payloads with unsigned E5M3 (\ue{}) block scales. Their wider range permits periodic tensor scaling, while our recipe applies selective stochastic rounding to backward gradients, omits RHT, and uses FP4 in all eligible internal linears.
  We pretrain a Nemotron-H 8B model for nearly 190 billion tokens. Compared with Transformer Engine \nv{}, the proposed block-16 recipe finishes with lower final-window training loss and, under their respective quantized-inference policies, lower validation loss measured as held-out negative log-likelihood. Its quantized-inference downstream point estimates are also higher on all three reported aggregates. A native \nv{} execution ablation that jointly removes RHT and the BF16 final-block exemption increases measured model-body token throughput by 21.2\%. These results demonstrate end-to-end software-emulated \uefp{} pretraining with a simpler recipe and motivate native support for \ue{} block scaling.

</details>


### [265] [Post-Training Language Models for Gold-Medal Performance in Coding Competitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02849)
*Aleksander Ficek,Sean Narenthiran,Mehrzad Samadi,Somshubra Majumdar,Boris Ginsburg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套端到端的竞技编程专项化流水线，结合大规模题目筛选、合成推理轨迹、SFT和RL训练，并引入GenCorrect测试时计算策略。该系统在IOI 2025上得分468（超过金牌线438.3），并在IOI 2026前瞻性评估中以535.4分的成绩超越最高人类选手498.27分，成为首个在IOI题目集上超越最高人类选手的AI系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 竞技编程已成为评估大语言模型推理能力的关键基准，而IOI和ICPC等国际竞赛代表最具挑战性的设置。此前尚无AI系统能在与人类选手相同的约束条件下超越最高人类参赛者的得分，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 提出端到端专项化流水线：(1) 从22,000道精选题目中进行大规模题目整理；(2) 生成合成推理轨迹；(3) 对Nemotron-3-Nano-CC (30B-A3B)执行SFT和RL训练，对Nemotron-3-Ultra-CC (550B-A55B)执行SFT训练；(4) 引入GenCorrect，一种反馈驱动的测试时计算策略，通过迭代生成、评估和精炼多样化解决方案来提升答题质量。

Result: IOI 2025上Nano-CC从130分提升至291分（post-training后），结合GenCorrect达到468分，超过金牌线438.3分；Ultra-CC达到502分。在IOI 2026前瞻性评估中，竞赛专用Ultra-CC系统在与人相同的时间、网络和提交限制下获得535.4/600分，超过金牌线361.12分和最高人类得分498.27分。

Conclusion: 这是首个在与人类选手同等约束条件下，在IOI题目集上超越最高人类得分的AI系统，证明了大规模数据策划结合SFT、RL及反馈驱动的测试时计算策略，可有效将LLM的竞技编程能力推向人类顶级水平。

Abstract: Competitive programming has become a key test of large language model reasoning, with international competitions such as IOI and ICPC representing its most challenging settings. We present an end-to-end specialization pipeline combining large-scale problem curation, synthetic reasoning traces, supervised fine-tuning (SFT), and reinforcement learning (RL). Using 22,000 curated problems, we train Nemotron-3-Nano-CC (30B-A3B) with SFT and RL and Nemotron-3-Ultra-CC (550B-A55B) with SFT alone. We further introduce GenCorrect, a feedback-driven test-time compute strategy that iteratively generates, evaluates, and refines diverse solutions. On IOI 2025, Nano-CC improves from 130 points to 291 after post-training and to 468 with GenCorrect, exceeding the gold threshold of 438.3 while Ultra-CC reaches 502. Guided by these results, we develop a competition-specific Ultra-CC system and evaluate it prospectively during IOI 2026. Under the same time, internet-access, and submission constraints as human contestants, it scores 535.4 out of 600, exceeding both the gold threshold of 361.12 and the top human score of 498.27. To our knowledge, this is the first AI system to outscore the highest-scoring human contestant on an IOI problem set.

</details>


### [266] [Graph Machine: Towards Better Pretraining via Edges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02881)
*Lintai Hou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出图机器（Graph Machine, GM）架构，通过可微分更新边（指针）实现稀疏动态路由，在保持O(n)计算复杂度的同时不受限于O(1)的状态访问规模。将Qwen3-0.6B中75%的稠密Transformer层替换为GM稀疏层并从零预训练15.7B tokens，每KV头仅检索2/4096个token时损失仅轻微下降，检索4个时最佳模型损失略有改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在状态规模与稀疏性之间存在矛盾：固定大小状态方法无法扩大记忆容量，而稀疏静态路由方法虽然节省计算但将可访问状态限制为O(1)，无法有效利用大规模记忆。

Method: GM架构维护O(n)大小的状态，通过稀疏动态路由访问。使用边（类指针对象）通过类似指针追逐（pointer chasing）的转介机制进行可微更新，使稀疏层的计算复杂度保持O(n)而无需将可访问状态限制在O(1)。实验中将Qwen3-0.6B的75%稠密Transformer层替换为GM稀疏层。

Result: 在15.7B token上从零预训练后：每KV头仅检索2/4096个token时，损失仅轻微下降（与密集模型相比几乎无损）；检索4个token时，最佳模型在损失上略有改善。

Conclusion: GM通过区分"稀疏计算"与"受限状态"两个概念，证明了可以在保持高效稀疏计算的同时维持大规模可访问状态，且在实际的大规模语言模型预训练中几乎不损失性能，甚至可能略有提升。

Abstract: We introduce the Graph Machine (GM), an architecture that maintains an $O(n)$-sized state and accesses it through sparse, dynamic routing. Unlike methods with fixed-size states or sparse but static routing, GM preserves $O(n)$ complexity in its sparse layers without restricting the potentially accessible state size to $O(1)$. Instead, GM uses edges - pointer-like objects updated differentiably by a referral mechanism resembling pointer chasing. We replace 75% of the dense Transformer layers in Qwen3-0.6B with GM sparse layers and pretrain from scratch on 15.7B tokens. With only 2 of 4,096 tokens retrieved per KV head in each sparse layer, loss degrades only slightly; with 4, the best model marginally improves loss.

</details>


### [267] [A Common Measure of Communication for Speech Brain-Computer Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02887)
*Dulhan Jayalath,Benjamin Ballyk,Oiwi Parker Jones*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出开放词汇互信息（OVMI），一种基于信息论的度量指标，用于统一衡量语音脑机接口（speech BCI）在不同词汇、数据集和记录条件下的信息传达能力，解决了该领域缺乏可比性指标的根本问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音BCI领域缺乏统一的进度衡量标准，因为不同系统使用不同的数据集、记录方法、语音类型和词汇表，其报告的分数（如准确率、WER）难以直接比较。背后的两个根本问题：（i）系统应支持用户表达何种词分布；（ii）系统能从该分布中传达多少信息。此外，仅在系统支持的词汇上计算的常规指标会高估系统的实际通信能力。

Method: 推导出开放词汇互信息（OVMI）——一个信息论量，衡量解码器相对于用户可能希望表达的词汇的参考分布所传达的信息量。OVMI允许在不同条件（如不同词汇表）下测量的能力在统一的信息通信尺度上进行评估，并可用于词汇表选择优化。

Result: 实验表明常规报告的准确率、词错误率（WER）及其他仅在系统支持词汇上计算的指标会高估系统传达用户意图的能力。使用OVMI比较现有系统可揭示系统对用户语言支持程度与解码准确率之间的权衡；这些比较取决于用户预期交流的内容；且以最大化OVMI为目标选择词汇表在三个语音域中可带来高达16.3%的相对准确率提升。

Conclusion: OVMI为语音BCI社区提供了一种有原则的方法来比较异构系统、改进词汇表设计并衡量领域进展，为不同系统间的公平比较提供了统一的信息论尺度。

Abstract: Speech brain-computer interfaces (speech BCIs) translate neural activity into language, offering a path towards restoring speech for people with paralysis and, more broadly, enabling new forms of natural human-computer interaction. Despite this promise, the field lacks a common measure of progress because systems use different datasets, recording methods, types of speech, and vocabularies, so their reported scores are rarely comparable. Underlying this measurement problem are two unresolved questions: (i) what distribution of words should a speech BCI enable a user to communicate, and (ii) how much information from this distribution can a system convey. We address both by deriving open-vocabulary mutual information (OVMI), an information-theoretic quantity that measures the information conveyed by a decoder relative to a reference distribution over the words a user may wish to communicate. This allows capabilities measured under different conditions, such as distinct vocabularies, to be evaluated on a common communication scale. We show that ordinarily reported accuracy, word error rate (WER), and other metrics computed only over the words a system supports can overstate how much of a user's intended speech the system can communicate. We then use OVMI to compare existing systems, expose trade-offs between how much of the user's language a system supports and how accurately it decodes those words, show that these comparisons depend on what the user is expected to communicate, and demonstrate that selecting a vocabulary to maximise OVMI yields up to 16.3% relative improvement in accuracy across three speech domains. OVMI therefore provides the speech BCI community with a principled way to compare heterogeneous systems, improve vocabulary design, and measure progress in the field.

</details>


<div id='q-bio.QM'></div>

# q-bio.QM [[Back]](#toc)

### [268] [A mechanistic modeling framework to interpret ACTH stimulation tests across HPA axis adaptation states and glucocorticoid feedback dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01684)
*Mamta Yadav,Phool Singh*

Main category: q-bio.QM

TL;DR: 本文提出一个整合激素动力学、反馈抑制和功能性质量适应的HPA轴机制建模框架，模拟180天内（基线、慢性应激、恢复三阶段）的HPA轴动态变化，并评估低剂量和高剂量ACTH刺激试验在不同生理阶段的诊断表现。结果显示皮质醇反应对应激强度和时间高度敏感，恢复期ACTH反应因持续反馈抵抗而钝化；低剂量ACTH检测更能反映部分肾上腺适应，高剂量检测可能掩盖功能障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ACTH刺激试验是评估肾上腺功能的核心手段，但HPA轴在慢性应激下具有动态适应性（包括腺体重塑、GR抵抗和延迟反馈恢复），这些因素可能改变检测结果却不代表原发性肾上腺功能衰竭。静态检测范式无法解释上述动态变化，需要新框架来理解应激相关或治疗诱导的肾上腺功能障碍。

Method: 建立一个机制建模框架，整合激素动力学、反馈抑制以及促肾上腺皮质细胞和肾上腺单元的功能性质量适应；引入时变GR抵抗函数模拟反馈脱敏及其消退过程；对HPA轴进行180天三阶段（基线、慢性应激、恢复期）仿真，并在此框架下评估低剂量（1μg）和高剂量（250μg）ACTH刺激试验。

Result: 仿真表明：皮质醇反应对应激的大小和时机高度敏感；ACTH反应呈阶段依赖性，恢复期因持续反馈抵抗常出现反应钝化；低剂量ACTH检测能更可靠地反映部分肾上腺适应，而高剂量检测可能因超生理刺激掩蔽真实的肾上腺功能障碍。

Conclusion: 静态检测范式存在明显局限，内分泌诊断应纳入腺体可塑性和GR反馈动力学因素，这对于应激相关及治疗诱导的肾上腺疾病的准确评估至关重要。

Abstract: The hypothalamic pituitary adrenal (HPA) axis is a key regulatory system coordinating endocrine responses to physiological and psychological stress. While the ACTH stimulation test remains a cornerstone of adrenal function assessment, its interpretation is complicated by the dynamic and adaptive nature of the HPA axis under chronic stress exposure. In particular, prolonged stress induces glandular remodeling, glucocorticoid receptor (GR) resistance and delayed feedback recovery, all of which may alter test outcomes without indicating primary adrenal failure. In this study, we present a mechanistic modeling framework that integrates hormonal kinetics, feedback inhibition and functional mass adaptation of the corticotroph and adrenal compartments. We simulate the HPA axis over $180$ days encompassing three phases - baseline, chronic stress and recovery, while introducing a time varying GR resistance function to mimic feedback desensitization and its resolution. Using this framework, we evaluate both low dose ($1 μg)$ and high dose ($250 μg)$ ACTH stimulation tests across physiological phases. Our simulations show that cortisol responses are highly sensitive to both the magnitude and timing of stress exposure and that ACTH responsiveness is phase dependent and often blunted during recovery due to persistent feedback resistance. Low dose ACTH testing more reliably reflects partial adrenal adaptation, while high dose tests risks masking dysfunction due to supraphysiological drive. These results highlight the limitations of static testing paradigms and suggest that accounting for glandular plasticity and GR feedback dynamics is essential for effective endocrine diagnosis particularly in stress related or treatment induced adrenal disorders.

</details>

# q-bio.NC [[Back]](#toc)

### [269] [Slow-Fast Brain-Computer Interfaces: Preventing Neuroadaptive Overfitting in AI-Mediated Neural Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01767)
*Aarthy Nagarajan*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文提出"神经自适应过拟合"这一脑机接口（BCI）闭环失效模式，并引入"慢-快BCI"（Slow-Fast BCI）框架，主张根据解码证据、不确定性、临床风险等因素对AI辅助进行分级加速，强调BCI评估应关注意图保真度、自主性、作者身份等深层指标，而非仅看解码精度和任务流畅度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI正将BCI从任务特异性神经解码器转变为自适应系统，虽有提升速度、流畅度和临床可及性的潜力，但传统性能指标可能忽视意图保真度、作者身份、能动性、治疗挑战性和持久临床获益等方面的损失，缺乏对AI辅助策略本身的系统性审视。

Method: 提出"神经自适应过拟合"概念作为闭环失效模式（AI过度优化短期代理指标如降低努力、快速接受、低负荷、平滑完成，偏离用户持久目标），并构建Slow-Fast BCI框架，将AI辅助分为快辅助（意图清晰且风险低）、受保护辅助（不确定时）和慢辅助（错位可能危及安全、能动性、作者身份、运动学习或治疗价值时），同时对不同应用领域（通信、运动控制、神经康复、闭环神经调控）给出相应的保障措施和评估指标。

Result: 该框架在不同BCI应用场景中勾勒出相应的安全防护与评估措施，论证了AI介导的BCI不仅应按解码精度和任务表现来评估，还应考虑AI辅助的部署方式（何时系统自主行动、寻求确认、保留用户努力或将控制权归还用户）。

Conclusion: AI介导的BCI评估需要超越传统解码精度与任务绩效范式，将AI辅助的时序与策略本身纳入评价体系，以保障用户的意图保真、能动性、作者身份和学习与治疗价值。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) is transforming brain-computer interfaces (BCIs) from task-specific neural decoders into adaptive systems that complete language, smooth movement, regulate rehabilitation support and adjust stimulation. These capabilities can increase speed, fluency, usability and clinical reach, yet conventional performance metrics may overlook losses in intent fidelity, authorship, agency, therapeutic challenge and durable clinical benefit. I define neuroadaptive overfitting as a closed-loop failure mode in which an AI-mediated BCI becomes over-optimized to short-term proxies of success, including reduced effort, rapid acceptance, lower workload or smooth task completion, while drifting from the user's durable goals. I then propose Slow-Fast BCI, a framework for pacing AI assistance according to decoder evidence, uncertainty, contextual and clinical stakes, fatigue, and user- or clinician-defined goals. The framework distinguishes fast assistance when intent is clear and stakes are low, guarded assistance under uncertainty and slow assistance when misalignment could compromise safety, agency, authorship, motor learning or therapeutic value. Across communication, motor-control, neurorehabilitation and closed-loop neuromodulation applications, I outline corresponding safeguards and evaluation measures. This Perspective argues that AI-mediated BCIs should be evaluated not only by decoding accuracy and task performance, but also by how AI assistance is deployed: when systems act autonomously, seek confirmation, preserve user effort or return control to the user.

</details>


### [270] [Neural Logic, Invariance, and the Retina---McCulloch and Pitts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02183)
*Nima Dehghani*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文重构McCulloch-Pitts计划为一种神经计算物理学，而非通常的二元神经元卡通模型，系统地分析了1943年逻辑微积分、1945年异质层级、1947年普遍性以及1959年蛙视网膜等经典工作的精确数学内涵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统对McCulloch-Pitts的解读往往简化为"二元神经元"的卡通图景，忽略了其原初计划中更全面的物理学视角。作者旨在恢复原始文献中阈值兴奋求和与绝对抑制否决之间的本质区别、单位时间延迟的物理意义等常被现代表述丢失的细节。

Method: 沿两个方向展开1943年逻辑演算：(1)给定网络，刻画其活动所实现的命题；(2)给定可容许的逻辑表达式，构造实现它的网络。精确处理循环问题——有限状态确定性网络的轨迹必然进入周期轨道；论证单一阈值单元只能实现线性可分离布尔函数，而有限前馈网络可以综合任意有限域布尔函数。进一步运用群平均和反馈规范化处理1947年的普遍性问题，通过尖峰触发分析揭示1949年蛙视网膜研究中非线性驱动神经元的隐藏选择性。

Result: 证明加权阈值形式在任意大的兴奋输入下无法保留绝对抑制否决的原始区别；将单位时间延迟理解为逻辑深度的物理实现；将循环精确刻画为有限状态动力学而非无界图灵计算。尖峰触发分析表明一阶平均消失可能掩盖二阶统计量中恢复的隐藏选择性，即方法学失败可以伪装为生理缺失。

Conclusion: 作者明确陈述了理想化的局限性，并使用现代数学工具但不将现代符号投射到历史文献上，旨在恢复McCulloch-Pitts计划的完整物理学内涵，同时阐明其与当前计算神经科学方法之间的真实联系与断裂。

Abstract: This chapter reconstructs the McCulloch-Pitts program as a physics of neural computation rather than the familiar cartoon of a binary neuron. The 1943 logical calculus is developed in both directions: given a net, characterize the propositions realized by its activity; given an admissible logical expression, construct a net that realizes it. We recover the original distinction between thresholded excitatory summation and absolute inhibitory veto-one the weighted-threshold form cannot preserve for arbitrarily large excitatory inputs-and read unit-time delay as the physical realization of logical depth. Recurrence is treated exactly: an autonomous, deterministic network of finitely many binary units has a finite state space, so every trajectory eventually enters a periodic orbit-a fact about finite-state dynamics, not unbounded Turing computation. A single threshold element realizes only linearly separable Boolean functions, whereas finite feedforward networks of them synthesize any Boolean function on a finite domain. We then follows McCulloch and Pitts beyond threshold logic. The 1945 heterarchy paper turns cyclic preference into an obstruction to representation by a scalar utility. The 1947 work on universals asks how a physical network can identify inputs related by nuisance transformations, developed here via group averaging and feedback canonicalization. The 1959 frog-retina study makes the adequate-stimulus question experimental, revealing parallel invariant operations before the brain proper. Spike-triggered analysis shows how a nonlinearly driven neuron can have a vanishing first-order average while second-order statistics recover its hidden selectivity: methodological failure can masquerade as physiological absence. Modern mathematical tools are used without projecting their notation onto the historical papers, and limitations of the idealization are stated explicitly.

</details>


### [271] [Mus siliconus: A Neuro-Musculoskeletal Digital Twin of the Mouse Integrating Neural Dynamics, Biomechanics, and Tactile Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02243)
*Satoshi Oota,Hideo Yokota,Hiroki Mori*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种"神经-肌肉骨骼"数字孪生小鼠框架，将神经动态（Bonhoeffer-van der Pol模型）、肌肉骨骼力学、感觉反馈和环境交互整合为一个闭环的感觉运动系统，旨在通过具身动态系统的视角统一动物数字孪生建模，从而推动计算神经科学、生物力学、AI和机器人学的交叉发展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动物数字孪生技术通常分别建模神经回路、解剖结构或生物力学，缺乏对产生行为的过程（神经-身体-环境耦合）的整合建模，因此无法真正捕捉行为的涌现机制。

Method: 提出一种基于小鼠的神经-肌肉骨骼数字孪生框架，融合多模态解剖重建（X射线CT、高分辨白光切片、Scx-GFP成像）、生物力学仿真、Bonhoeffer-van der Pol神经动力学模型和触觉反馈，构建闭环感觉运动回路，使行为在神经系统、肌肉骨骼与环境的持续交互中涌现。

Result: 该框架展示了可计算的动力学基础（BvP模型）以支持大规模系统交互仿真；当与自适应学习和自主实验耦合时，数字孪生有望从被动仿真转变为主动科学工具，可生成假设、预测干预并指导实验。

Conclusion: 动物数字孪生应被视为具身动力学系统，而非各子系统的离散模型；神经-肌肉骨骼数字孪生可作为计算神经科学、生物力学、AI和机器人学的汇聚点，为研究生物智能和开发适应性生物医学与机器人系统提供新的平台。

Abstract: Digital twin technologies could transform neuroscience and biomedicine by creating predictive computational representations of living organisms. However, most animal digital twins model neural circuits, anatomy, or biomechanics separately rather than integrating the processes that generate behavior. We argue that animal digital twins should instead be conceived as embodied dynamical systems that unify neural activity, body mechanics, sensory feedback, and environmental interactions.
  We propose a neuro-musculoskeletal digital twin of the mouse that combines multimodal anatomical reconstruction from X-ray CT, high-resolution white-light sections, and Scx-GFP imaging with biomechanical simulation, Bonhoeffer--van der Pol neural dynamics, and tactile feedback. This framework forms a closed sensorimotor loop in which behavior emerges through continuous interactions among the nervous system, musculoskeletal system, and environment. The Bonhoeffer--van der Pol model provides a computationally tractable dynamical foundation for large-scale simulation of these interactions.
  Neuro-musculoskeletal digital twins could provide a convergence point for computational neuroscience, biomechanics, artificial intelligence, and robotics. When coupled with adaptive learning and autonomous experimentation, they may develop from passive simulations into active scientific instruments that generate hypotheses, predict interventions, and guide experiments. Such embodied digital twins could advance the study of biological intelligence and support new adaptive biomedical and robotic systems.

</details>


### [272] [Fungal Memory and Minimal Cognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02345)
*Kristina Šekrst*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文论证真菌菌丝网络通过记忆整合的适应性调节表现出"最小认知"。作者基于控制论和生成主义框架，提出非表征性的记忆概念及四项最小认知操作标准，并以经验证据表明真菌通过分布式记忆机制（定向再生长、应激印记、Spitzenkörper介导的方向持久性、跨代孢子印记）全面满足这些标准，从而挑战表征主义认知科学假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战认知科学中表征主义和神经中心论的假设，证明记忆与认知可以脱离神经基底和符号表征而从形态动力学、生化及电生理过程中涌现，从而扩展对"何谓认知系统"的理解。

Method: 采用理论构建与经验证据结合的方式：基于控制论和生成主义框架提出非表征性记忆理论，操作性地定义四项最小认知标准（反馈引导的行为调节、内部生存条件维持、基于过往环境交互的结构调节、跨时间尺度的可塑性支持预期性适应），并以真菌语境的实证研究逐一验证。

Result: 真菌通过分布式记忆机制满足全部四项最小认知标准：(1) 在空间位移后对既往资源的定向再生长；(2) 跨多次细胞分裂持续的应激印记；(3) 受限环境下Spitzenkörper介导的方向持久性；(4) 通过孢子印记实现跨代记忆。

Conclusion: 真菌认知表明认知的组织原则——反馈驱动的适应、规范维持与历史耦合——可在根本不同的物质基底中实现，这扩展了认知系统的界定范围，并对表征主义认知科学形成根本性挑战。

Abstract: This paper argues that fungal mycelial networks exhibit minimal cognition through memory-integrated adaptive regulation. Drawing on cybernetic and enactivist frameworks, I develop a non-representational account of memory as the organism's capacity to modulate behavior based on temporally extended environmental coupling. I propose four operational criteria for minimal cognition: feedback-guided regulation of behavior, maintenance of internal viability conditions, structural modulation based on past environmental interactions, and plasticity across time scales that supports anticipatory adaptivity. Empirical evidence demonstrates that fungi meet all four criteria through distributed memory mechanisms: fungal networks exhibit directional regrowth toward previously encountered resources even after spatial displacement; stress priming persists across multiple cell divisions; Spitzenkörper-mediated directional persistence in constrained environments; and transgenerational memory through spore imprinting. These findings challenge representationalist assumptions in cognitive science by showing that memory and cognition can emerge from morphodynamic, biochemical, and electrophysiological processes without necessary neural substrates or symbolic representations. Fungal cognition demonstrates that the organizational principles underlying cognition - feedback-driven adaptation, norm-preservation, and historical coupling - can be realized in radically different material substrates, expanding our understanding of what counts as a cognitive system.

</details>


### [273] [Prediction emerges in RNNs trained for perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02739)
*Akanksha Gupta,Alejandro Tabas*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文通过模拟实验表明，预测机制会在仅以感知（去噪）为优化目标的循环神经网络中自然涌现，无需额外的预测训练约束，从而解释大脑为何进化出预测能力来辅助感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管预测加工假说认为大脑通过预测来理解嘈杂模糊的感官输入，但尚不清楚大脑为何会进化出这一计算代价高昂的预测过程来辅助感知。

Method: 训练RNN对巴赫作品的分词版本进行去噪（涵盖多种噪声水平），冻结网络权重后，训练线性读出器（linear readout）从网络状态进行下一token预测，并将其性能与独立训练的线性基准模型进行比较；同时检验RNN对感官输入的响应是否与预测误差成正比。

Result: 在中等噪声水平下，RNN状态的线性读出器在预测任务上优于基准模型，表明网络依赖预测机制支持感知；且RNN对感官输入的响应与预测误差成比例。

Conclusion: 神经层面的预测加工特征可以在仅优化感知的系统中自然涌现，无需额外的训练约束，为预测加工假说的进化起源提供了计算层面的解释。

Abstract: The brain is highly proficient at making sense of noisy and ambiguous sensory inputs. Predictive processing hypothesises that this ability relies on prediction. However, it is unclear why the brain would have evolved to predict the sensory world, a computationally expensive process, in order to aid perception. Here we use simulations to argue that prediction naturally emerges in systems optimised for perception. We train recurrent neural networks (RNNs) to denoise a tokenised version of Bach's compositions at a range of noise levels. Afterwards, we enquire whether the states of the networks contain predictive information about the next token. We test this by freezing the RNN weights and training a linear readout from its states on prediction. We compare the performance of the linear readout with that of an independently trained linear benchmark model. The results show that the linear readout from the RNNs outperforms the benchmark model at moderate levels of noise, indicating that the networks rely on a predictive mechanism to support perception. We further show that the responses of the RNNs to sensory inputs are proportional to prediction error. Together, the results demonstrate that neural signatures of predictive processing emerge, without any further training constraints, from optimisation of perception.

</details>


<div id='q-bio.GN'></div>

# q-bio.GN [[Back]](#toc)

### [274] [Subcellularly Resolved Single-Cell Embedding Learning with Transcriptomic data, Protein Structure and Localization Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02344)
*Zhen Zhou,Jiachen Li,Yuan Liu,Xiaoyong Pan,Hong-Bin Shen*

Main category: q-bio.GN

TL;DR: 本文提出一种多模态框架用于学习亚细胞分辨率的细胞嵌入表示，通过交叉注意力机制联合整合RNA表达谱、蛋白质序列表征和蛋白质结构信息，生成能够反映亚细胞组织结构的细胞表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有细胞嵌入方法主要依赖于转录组或蛋白质组测量，将每个细胞表示为整体实体，忽略了单个分子的亚细胞定位信息；同时很少纳入蛋白质结构信息，尽管结构在决定分子相互作用和功能中起基础作用。

Method: 采用交叉注意力架构整合转录组、序列和结构三种模态，在不同亚细胞区室中建模模态间交互，从而学习亚细胞解析的细胞嵌入。

Result: 生成的嵌入通过细粒度的亚细胞组织表示每个细胞，同时捕获分子表达模式和关联蛋白质的功能特性；保留了空间组织的生物学信息，并跨多个分子层次整合互补信号。

Conclusion: 该框架首次在统一跨模态学习范式中联合纳入转录组信息、蛋白质序列表征和蛋白质结构知识，生成亚细胞解析的细胞嵌入。

Abstract: Existing cell embedding methods predominantly rely on transcriptomic or proteomic measurements and represent each cell as a holistic entity, thereby overlooking the subcellular localization of individual molecules. Moreover, they rarely incorporate protein structural information, despite its fundamental role in determining molecular interactions and functions. In this work, we propose a multimodal framework for learning subcellularly resolved cell embeddings by jointly leveraging RNA expression profiles, protein sequence representations, and protein structural information. Specifically, we employ a cross-attention architecture to integrate transcriptomic, sequence, and structural modalities and model their interactions within distinct subcellular compartments. The resulting embeddings represent each cell through its fine-grained subcellular organization, capturing both molecular expression patterns and the functional properties of the associated proteins. By learning cell representations at subcellular resolution, our framework preserves spatially organized biological information while integrating complementary signals across multiple molecular levels. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first framework that produces subcellularly resolved cell embeddings by jointly incorporating transcriptomic information, protein sequence representations, and protein structural knowledge within a unified cross-modal learning paradigm.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [275] [Scaling Inference Prefill with High-Radix Photonic Interconnects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01821)
*Arulselvan Madhavan,Peter Carson,Taylor Groves,Thomas Graham*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文量化了3D集成光子互连在MoE模型推理prefill阶段的优势，在多种上下文长度和批处理配置下，相比传统铜基GPU系统实现了2.1-5.8倍的延迟改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着推理成为AI的主导工作负载，日益复杂的MoE模型对scale-up带宽提出了巨大需求，行业正转向高带宽光子互连。然而光子互连对推理prefill阶段的实际收益尚未被系统量化，特别是高并发LLM聊天与超长上下文推理/agentic AI场景之间的权衡需要深入分析。

Method: 作者模拟了三种MoE模型：短上下文(1K-8K tokens)、中等上下文(128K tokens)和长上下文(1M tokens)，分别在现有铜基GPU系统和高带宽3D集成光子系统中评估推理prefill工作负载，比较高并发吞吐与长上下文场景下的性能表现。

Result: 3D光子互连在高批量压力配置下带来2.1-3.2x延迟改进，在通信受限配置中带来2.8-5.8x改进。光子互连使1152-GPU的部署成为可能以降低time-to-first-token，在电气系统达到scale-up pod扩展极限时，生产级平台实现2.2-4.5x加速。

Conclusion: 3D集成光子互连是解决MoE推理prefill阶段规模扩展瓶颈的关键技术，能够突破铜基电气互连的scale-up pod限制，显著缩短首次令牌延迟并提升整体推理吞吐。

Abstract: With the rise of inference as today's dominant AI workload, the industry is transitioning to high-bandwidth photonic interconnects to meet the large scale-up requirements of increasingly complex Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models. This paper quantifies the benefits of 3D-integrated photonic interconnects for inference prefill by analyzing tradeoffs between high-concurrency throughput for Large Language Model (LLM) chat and the large context windows typically required for reasoning and agentic AI. We simulate three MoE models: short context (1K--8K tokens), medium context (128K tokens), and long context (1M tokens). We evaluate this workload across existing copper-based GPU systems and one with high bandwidth integrated photonics. We show 2.1--3.2x latency improvements in the stressed high-batch regimes and 2.8--5.8x improvements over baselines in communication-limited configurations. 3D photonics enable the 1152-GPU footprint required to lower time-to-first-token, yielding 2.2--4.5x speedups across production-grade platforms when electrical systems cross their inherent scale-up-pod limits.

</details>


### [276] [CREDIT: Cost-guided Reduction-reuse with Efficient DSMEM Inter-CTA Tiling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01864)
*Zhengxiong Li,Tsung-Wei Huang,Umit Ogras*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出CREDIT，一个基于成本引导的框架，用于自动识别NVIDIA分布式共享内存（DSMEM）值得使用的负载模式，预测其收益范围，并在多种工作负载上实现一致加速。在六种负载上，CREDIT实现91.7%的收益预测准确率，几何平均加速比在RTX 5090上达1.466x，在H100上达1.318x。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DSMEM允许thread block cluster内直接共享内存访问，但集群同步、远程访问和资源开销使得难以判断何时DSMEM能真正提升性能，缺乏系统性的成本-收益分析框架。

Method: CREDIT包含三项核心创新：(1) 基于profile驱动的特征识别，找出适合DSMEM的工作负载模式；(2) 将DSMEM应用到reduction-reuse负载的变换；(3) 基于profile数据的成本模型，用于确定DSMEM收益的适用范围。

Result: 在多样负载上，CREDIT的收益预测准确率达91.7%。相比torch.compile、Triton和优化后的非DSMEM CUDA基线，CREDIT在六种负载上全面胜出，RTX 5090上几何平均加速1.466x，H100上为1.318x。

Conclusion: CREDIT填补了DSMEM使用决策缺乏系统指导的空白，证明基于成本引导的自动识别与变换能为DSMEM带来稳定且显著的性能收益，且代码已开源。

Abstract: NVIDIA distributed shared memory (DSMEM) enables direct shared-memory access within a thread block cluster. However, cluster synchronization, remote access, and resource costs make it difficult to determine when DSMEM improves performance. To fill this gap, we propose CREDIT, a cost-guided framework that identifies DSMEM-profitable workload patterns, predicts their profitability range, and delivers consistent speedups across diverse workloads. CREDIT combines three innovations: (1) a profiling-driven characterization that identifies workload patterns likely to benefit from DSMEM; (2) a transformation that applies DSMEM to reduction-reuse workloads; (3) a cost model based on profiling data, to determine its profitability range. Evaluations on diverse workloads show CREDIT achieves 91.7% prediction accuracy on profitability. CREDIT beats torch.compile, Triton, and optimized non-DSMEM CUDA baselines on all six workloads, with geometric-mean speedups of 1.466x on RTX 5090 and 1.318x on H100. CREDIT's source code is publicly available at https://github.com/zhengxiongli08/CREDIT.

</details>


### [277] [RT-HiSS: Ray Tracing Accelerated High Dimensional Vector Similarity Searches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01975)
*Revanth Reddy Munugala,Michael Gowanlock*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出RT-HiSS，首个基于GPU RT核的高维数据集精确相似性搜索算法，通过RT核加速索引构建与搜索、CUDA核精炼候选、两遍法估计结果上界实现高效批处理与负载均衡，在六个真实数据集上较最先进GPU算法提速最高8.37倍，较暴力搜索提速最高2368倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RT核心主要服务于图形渲染，但近期研究表明可将其用于通用任务（如相似性搜索）。然而现有基于GPU RT核的方法仅支持不超过三维的数据集，无法应对高维数据的相似性搜索需求；同时GPU相似性搜索在大数据集、大搜索距离场景下扩展性差。

Method: RT-HiSS采用RT核加速索引构建与搜索过程，再由CUDA核对候选结果进行精炼（refinement）。引入两遍法（two-pass approach）估计结果集大小的上界，从而在GPU内存约束下实现高效批处理与近乎完美的负载均衡；此外还设计了共享内存分块（shared memory tiling）和压缩结果掩码（compressed result masks）以提升GPU资源利用率。

Result: RT-HiSS在六个真实世界数据集上，相对现有最先进GPU算法最高获得8.37倍加速，相对暴力搜索算法最高获得2368.26倍加速。

Conclusion: RT-HiSS首次实现了基于GPU RT核的高维数据集精确相似性搜索，有效解决了高维数据和规模扩展性问题，并通过两阶段流水线、批处理优化及资源利用改进，展现出显著的性能优势。

Abstract: Recent GPU generations include special-purpose ray tracing (RT) cores for graphics applications. While RT cores are primarily used for rendering, recent works show they can be leveraged for general-purpose tasks, including similarity searches. However, existing approaches do not support datasets exceeding three dimensions. In this work, we propose RT-HiSS, the first exact GPU RT-core-based similarity search algorithm for high-dimensional datasets. GPU similarity search often scales poorly for large datasets with substantial search distances. To address this, RT-HiSS uses RT cores for fast index construction and searches, followed by candidate refinement on CUDA cores. We introduce a two-pass approach to estimate an upper bound on result size, enabling efficient batching under GPU memory constraints with near-perfect load balancing. Additionally, we examine shared memory tiling and compressed result masks to improve GPU resource utilization. RT-HiSS yields speedups up to 8.37$\times$ over competitive state-of-the-art GPU algorithms and up to 2,368.26$\times$ relative to the brute-force algorithm across six real-world datasets.

</details>


### [278] [MeanField Surrogate Modeling for Scalable Runtime Scheduling of Concurrent Heterogeneous AI Inference on Shared GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02109)
*Youssef Ennouri,Soonhoi Ha*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出一种MeanField代理模型，用于异构AI模型在共享GPU上并发运行的调度预测。该模型通过局部配置和聚合GPU状态预测每个模型的性能，避免显式建模所有联合交互，将样本预算从组合级复杂度降低到近似线性增长（N个共运行模型）。在N∈{2,3,4,5,6}的LLM与视觉工作负载上达到R²≈0.96的预测精度，集成到遗传算法调度器后可在N=5问题（78,732个可行联合配置）上以26ms中位数完成决策，性能距离穷举搜索0.10%以内，且零SLA违规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构AI模型在共享GPU上并发部署会产生资源争用，使运行时调度变得复杂。虽然代理模型可以避免昂贵的在线基准测试，但其配置文件需求通常随共运行模型数量呈组合级增长，严重限制了可扩展性。

Method: 提出MeanField（平均场）代理模型，通过局部配置和聚合GPU状态来预测各模型性能，而非显式建模所有联合交互。该方法将全联合交互建模替换为平均场近似，显著降低了对配置样本的需求。随后将该代理模型集成到遗传算法调度器中。

Result: 在并发LLM和视觉工作负载的实验中，N∈{2,3,4,5,6}时预测精度高（R²≈0.96），经验样本预算随N近似线性增长（而非组合级增长）。集成到遗传算法调度器后，N=5问题（78,732个可行joint配置）相对穷举搜索差距在0.10%以内，八个动态工作负载场景下零SLA违规，在线GA决策中位耗时26ms，比穷举代理搜索快约5倍。

Conclusion: MeanField代理模型通过平均场近似有效解决了组合级复杂度问题，在不牺牲预测精度的前提下大幅降低了样本需求，且与遗传算法集成后能够高效求解大规模联合调度问题，兼具准确性和实时性。

Abstract: Deploying heterogeneous AI models concurrently on a shared GPU introduces resource contention that complicates runtime scheduling. While surrogate models avoid costly online benchmarking, their profiling requirements typically grow combinatorially with the number of co-running models, limiting scalability. We propose a MeanField surrogate that predicts per-model performance from local configuration and aggregate GPU state rather than explicitly modeling all joint interactions. Experiments on concurrent LLM and vision workloads across $N \in \{2,3,4,5,6\}$ show high predictive accuracy ($R^2 \approx 0.96$) with an empirical sample budget that grows approximately linearly in $N$, in contrast to the combinatorial cost of fully joint profiling. Integrated into a genetic algorithm scheduler, the surrogate scales to an $N=5$ problem with 78,732 feasible joint configurations, remaining within 0.10% of the exhaustive search with zero SLA violations across eight dynamic workload scenarios, while complete online GA decisions take 26 ms median, about $5\times$ faster than exhaustive surrogate search.

</details>


### [279] [Towards Global Federated Genome-Wide Association Meta-Analysis Using GA4GH TES](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02227)
*Abhijit Chunduru,Matthew Joel,Zilinghan Li,Ravi Madduri*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于APPFL框架的隐私保护联邦GWAS荟萃分析流水线，各站点仅传输汇总统计量而非个体级基因型数据，并通过GA4GH TES端点全球网络执行计算，实现"计算走向数据"而非"数据走向计算"。在包含10万合成个体和约24万个变异位点的五个站点模拟中，联邦荟萃分析与集中式分析重现了一致的关联信号，证明基于标准任务执行和联邦学习可构建实用的跨国GWAS隐私保护基础设施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GWAS需要大规模、祖先多样的队列来获得统计功效，但隐私法规和数据驻留限制阻碍了基因数据在机构或国家间的集中汇集，因此需要一种不集中个体级基因数据即可实现跨国GWAS荟萃分析的隐私保护方案。

Method: 基于APPFL框架的联邦GWAS荟萃分析流水线：各站点在本地计算GWAS汇总统计量，仅传输聚合结果（非个体级基因型）；通过GA4GH TES全球端点网络执行分布式计算以支持"计算移动到数据"；服务器执行逆方差加权固定效应荟萃分析并将聚合结果返回所有站点；开发了HiveWatch地理可观测性工具包用于实时监控分布式任务执行。

Result: 在五个站点、10万合成个体、约24万个变异位点的2型糖尿病和身体质量指数模拟中，联邦荟萃分析在不集中任何基因型数据的情况下，重现了预期从集中式汇集中得到的关联信号。

Conclusion: 基于标准的任务执行（GA4GH TES）与联邦学习相结合，可实现实用的、隐私保护的跨国GWAS荟萃分析基础设施。

Abstract: Genome-wide association studies (GWAS) gain statistical power from large, ancestrally diverse cohorts, but privacy regulations and data-residency constraints often prevent genomic data from being centrally pooled across institutional or national borders. We present a privacy-preserving federated GWAS meta-analysis pipeline built on the APPFL framework, in which each site computes local GWAS summary statistics and transmits only aggregate results, never individual-level genotypes. Analysis is executed through a global network of Global Alliance for Genomics and Health (GA4GH) Task Execution Service (TES) endpoints, which allows computation to move to the data rather than the reverse. The server performs inverse-variance-weighted fixed-effect meta-analysis and returns aggregated results to all sites, while HiveWatch, our developed geographic observability toolkit, provides real-time monitoring of distributed task execution. In a five-site simulation over 100,000 synthetic individuals and roughly 240,000 variants for Type 2 Diabetes and Body Mass Index, the federated meta-analysis reproduces the association signal expected from a pooled analysis without centralizing any genotype data, showing that standards-based task execution and federated learning enables a practical privacy-preserving infrastructure for international GWAS meta-analysis.

</details>


### [280] [Federated Learning on the American Science Cloud using APPFL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02238)
*Zilinghan Li,Abhijit Chunduru,Harinarayan Krishnan,Eric Chagnon,Peter Nugent,Kibaek Kim,Ravi Madduri*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出将隐私保护联邦学习框架APPFL的编排逻辑部署为美国科学云（AmSC）的可扩展云服务，利用AmSC已有的认证、函数执行、实验追踪和模型托管等原语，弥补其在跨组织边界训练模型方面的能力空白，以解锁隐私受限的科学协作和公私合作模型构建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AmSC作为DOE Genesis Mission下的AI驱动发现平台，虽然提供了托管模型推理、实验追踪和跨设施函数执行等服务，但缺乏在数据因政策、隐私或规模原因无法集中的情况下跨组织边界训练模型的能力——这正是一个联邦学习的典型应用场景，也是一个缺口。

Method: 将APPFL（高级隐私保护联邦学习）框架的编排逻辑作为可扩展云服务部署在AmSC之上，利用其平台已有的基础原语：项目级认证支持可靠的联邦成员资格管理、函数执行驱动各站点分布式训练、实验追踪记录每轮训练性能、模型托管与推理基础设施用于向授权参与者分发联邦训练后的模型。

Result: 论文论证了通过APPFL与AmSC现有服务原语的结合，可以弥合AmSC在联邦学习方面的能力差距，实现跨组织边界的隐私保护模型训练。

Conclusion: 将联邦计算作为AmSC的重要服务提供，将解锁隐私受限的科学协作场景，支持公私合作模型构建，同时也能锻炼和增强平台自身的联邦基础设施能力。

Abstract: The American Science Cloud (AmSC), established under the Genesis Mission of the U.S. Department of Energy (DOE), aims to integrate DOE high-performance computing systems, experimental facilities, and data resources into a single, coordinated, AI-driven discovery platform. AmSC's early services focus on curated artifacts, such as gated inference access to hosted models, experiment tracking, and function execution across computing facilities. However, what these services lack is a means to train a model across organizational boundaries where data cannot be centralized due to policy, privacy, or scale. This is, by definition, a use case for federated learning (FL) and a growing class of scientific AI. In this paper, we show that this gap can be bridged by deploying the orchestration logic of the Advanced Privacy-Preserving Federated Learning (APPFL) framework as a scalable cloud service on top of the primitives AmSC already provides: project-scoped authentication that supports secure and reliable federation membership, function execution that drives distributed training at each site, experiment tracking that records round-level performance, and finally, the model-hosting and inference infrastructure that can be leveraged to distribute the federated trained models to authorized participants. We argue that offering federated computing as an important AmSC service would unlock privacy-constrained scientific collaborations, enabling public-private partnerships in model building while exercising and enhancing the platform's own federated infrastructure.

</details>


### [281] [AceSpec: An Asymmetric Edge-Cloud Collaborative Framework for Communication-Efficient LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02514)
*Yida Zhang,Zhiyong Gao,Shuaibing Yue,Jie Li,Rui Wang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: AceSpec是一个非对称端云协同推测解码框架，通过利用边缘端未饱和算力构建概率状态缓存，将网络级流水线刷新转化为O(1)本地内存查找，并结合非对称通信协议与网络感知资源分配策略，在易变WAN环境下实现最高3.52倍吞吐加速，且在50Kbps极低带宽下仍能维持近峰值推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端侧部署LLM面临两难：模型压缩会损害推理能力，而分割推理受限于WAN通信瓶颈。边缘-云端推测解码虽具备潜力，但在易波动的WAN环境下，预测被拒绝会引发灾难性的流水线停滞和网络级回滚，使协作增益被抵消。亟需一种能有效应对网络波动、避免流水线刷新的协同推理方案。

Method: AceSpec包含三大核心设计：(1) 利用边缘端未饱和算力预先构建概率状态缓存，将网络级流水线刷新转化为O(1)本地内存查找，消除WAN延迟导致的协作停滞；(2) 采用非对称通信协议，上行仅传输最小主干索引，下行传输紧凑的稀疏分布，大幅节省带宽；(3) 引入网络感知的拉格朗日优化资源分配策略，动态最大化本地缓存命中率。

Result: 实验评估表明，AceSpec实现了最高3.52倍的吞吐加速，并展现出卓越的带宽免疫能力，即使在严重受限的50Kbps WAN条件下，仍能维持接近峰值的推理性能。

Conclusion: AceSpec通过将网络通信开销转化为本地缓存查找、采用非对称通信协议以及网络感知的资源优化分配，有效解决了易变WAN环境下边缘-云端协同推测解码的稳定性与效率问题，为端云协同部署LLM提供了一种高鲁棒性的实用方案。

Abstract: Deploying Large Language Models (LLMs) on edge devices typically relies on model compression or split inference. However, compression degrades reasoning capabilities, while split inference suffers from severe Wide Area Network (WAN) communication bottlenecks. Edge-cloud speculative decoding emerges as a promising alternative, leveraging an edge small model to draft tokens for cloud verification. Yet, over volatile WANs, inevitable prediction rejections trigger catastrophic pipeline stalls and network-wide rollbacks, neutralizing collaborative gains. To overcome this, we propose AceSpec, an asymmetric edge-cloud collaborative framework. AceSpec utilizes un-saturated edge compute to proactively construct a probabilistic state cache, effectively transforming network-wide pipeline flushes into $\mathcal{O}(1)$ local memory lookups. To preserve bandwidth, it employs an asymmetric communication protocol that transmits minimal main-chain indices uplink and compact sparse distributions downlink. Furthermore, we introduce a network-aware, Lagrangian-optimized resource allocation strategy that dynamically maximizes the local cache hit rate. Evaluations demonstrate that AceSpec achieves up to a 3.52$\times$ throughput speedup and exhibits exceptional bandwidth immunity, sustaining near-peak inference performance even under severely constrained 50 Kbps WAN conditions.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [282] [Context Inference Attacks Without Jailbreaks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01663)
*Prince Jha,Samuele Poppi,Nils Lukas*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了智能体（agentic）AI系统在推理时隐藏上下文泄露（hidden-context leakage）的隐私风险。作者将这类风险形式化为"上下文推断攻击"（context-inference attacks），通过安全博弈定义了已知上下文、未知上下文和通过工具调用检索得到的上下文三种设置，并在灰盒与黑盒场景下评估了泄露程度。实验表明，即使在指令禁止泄露、logit抑制和上下文稀释等防御措施下，agent仍会泄露嵌入上下文中的敏感记录信息（例如医疗或财务记录）。单一攻击方法在所有设置下均有效，在小候选集上达到100%攻击成功率，在1024个候选中对已知上下文达到63% ASR，在未知模板场景为78.9 AUROC，14B代理模型评分32B目标模型时达92.5 AUROC，通过agent检索返回时达81.8 AUROC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有隐私攻击研究主要聚焦于通过越狱（jailbreaking）诱导模型直接披露敏感内容，但忽略了智能体AI场景中上下文由agent自身工具调用组装这一关键特性。随着agentic系统越来越多地处理医疗、金融等敏感数据，评估agent在推理时是否通过隐藏上下文泄露敏感信息成为亟待解决的安全问题。

Method: 作者引入并形式化了上下文推断攻击（context-inference attacks），通过安全博弈（security game）定义三种设置：（1）已知上下文；（2）未知上下文；（3）agent通过自身工具检索得到的上下文。区分灰盒设置（使用目标模型打分观察结果）与黑盒设置（使用攻击者控制的替代模型打分）。测试了三种防御控制：禁止披露指令、logit抑制和上下文稀释，并在不同查询预算、上下文大小和目标模型大小下刻画泄露变化。

Result: 单一攻击方法可在三种设置下均生效且无需修改。已知上下文的1024候选场景达63% ASR，小候选集上达100% ASR；模板和周围记录未知时为78.9 AUROC；14B替代模型评分32B目标模型时达92.5 AUROC；记录通过agent检索返回时为81.8 AUROC，均显著高于随机水平（1/|Z|和50）。

Conclusion: 即便在多种防御控制下，被评估的智能体系统对隐藏上下文泄露仍然脆弱，尤其在agent自身工具调用组装上下文的场景中。该工作揭示了现有隐私防御在agentic推理设置下的不足，凸显了需要针对上下文推断攻击开发更有效保护机制的必要性。

Abstract: Agentic AI systems are increasingly deployed to process sensitive data at inference time, such as healthcare records or financial documents assembled into a hidden \emph{context} before the system answers. Prior work has studied privacy risks primarily through \emph{jailbreaking} attacks that induce models to directly disclose sensitive content, but has largely overlooked the agentic setting where the context is assembled by the agent's own tool calls. We show that the agents we evaluate remain vulnerable to hidden-context leakage despite the controls we test against them, namely an instruction not to disclose the context, logit suppression, and context dilution. For instance, a web-browsing agent answering benign user queries still carries exploitable signals about records silently loaded into its context. We introduce and formalize \emph{context-inference attacks} through a security game and evaluate three settings under decreasing attacker knowledge and increasingly indirect delivery of the context: a known context, an unknown context, and a context the agent retrieves through its own tool calls. We distinguish a grey-box setting, in which the target model is used to score observations, from black-box settings in which the attacker scores with a surrogate it controls. We further characterize how leakage varies with query budget, context size, and target-model size. A single attack carries through all three settings without modification, reaching $100\%$ ASR on small candidate sets and $63\%$ at $1024$ candidates against a known context, $78.9$ AUROC when the template and surrounding records are unknown, $92.5$ AUROC when a 14B surrogate scores a 32B target, and $81.8$ AUROC when the records arrive as an agent's retrieval returns, against chance rates of $1/|\mathcal{Z}|$ and $50$ respectively.

</details>


### [283] [Private Computation Space: Experience with Trusted Multi-Cluster Federated Learning for Agriculture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01667)
*Shuangyu Lei,Muhammad Salman Abid,Jacob Belding,Sam Mosher,Manushi B. Trivedi,Shivranjani Baruah,Liam Wickes-Do,Andrew Anderson,Braulio Dumba,Alyssa Whitcraft,Ritvik Sahajpal,Sijin Li,Kelly Robbins,Michael Gore,Margaret Frank,Steven Wolf,Liz Jones,Abraham Stroock,Kaitlin Gold,Hakim Weatherspoon*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文介绍了私有计算空间（PCS）系统，一个为农业场景部署的开源机器学习系统，结合异步联邦学习、差分隐私和可信执行环境，保护农民数据隐私的同时保持模型效用，并在纽约和加利福尼亚的实际部署中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管人工智能能改善农业实践，但由于69%的美国农民对数据共享存在隐私顾虑，农业AI的采用率仍然有限。虽然联邦学习在其他领域已被证明能在规模上保护隐私，但农业部署面临独特挑战：需要保护农民数据和身份同时保持模型效用、在商用硬件上运行、并能适应脆弱的农村基础设施。

Method: 提出私有计算空间（PCS）系统，一个开源机器学习平台。该系统的技术方案包括：(1) 多集群编排以增强农村地区的可靠性；(2) 异步联邦学习（FL）以应对不稳定的网络连接；(3) 差分隐私（DP）提供数学层面的隐私保证；(4) 可信执行环境（TEEs）确保数据处理的安全性。系统针对农业场景进行定制设计。

Result: 在纽约地区为期六个月的活体植物传感器氮监测任务和加州为期十个月的天气站蒸散量预测任务上进行了实际评估。结果显示：氮监测的Dice相似系数（DSC）为0.71，蒸散量预测的R²精度为0.84，分别将最差单站点模型精度提升了22.4%和9.1%，同时保持了隐私保护。

Conclusion: PCS系统成功证明了在农业环境中可以同时实现隐私保护和模型效用的双重目标，为后续农业AI的大规模采用提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence has shown to help improve agricultural practices, yet adoption remains limited: 69% of U.S. farmers have privacy concerns with sharing their data, and these concerns must be addressed before adoption is widespread. While Federated Learning has been demonstrated to protect privacy at scale for other sectors, deploying a system for agriculture comes with its own set of challenges; the problem necessitates a system that can protect farmer data and identities while preserving model utility, runs on commodity hardware, and is resilient to fragile rural infrastructure. To address these concerns, we introduce the Private Computation Space (PCS), a deployed, open-source Machine Learning system to provision and process farmer data securely. We design a system tailored to an agricultural setting, with multi-cluster orchestration for reliability in rural areas with asynchronous Federated Learning (FL), Differential Privacy (DP), and Trusted Execution Environments (TEEs), to allow farms to participate in the framework while keeping their data private. We evaluate the system on two deployed workloads: monitoring nitrogen with living plant sensors in NY for six months and predicting evapotranspiration from weather stations in CA for ten months. Our evaluation finds a Dice Similarity Coefficient (DSC) of 0.71 and $R^2$ accuracy of 0.84 for the respective workloads, improving the worst single-site model accuracy by 22.4% and 9.1%, respectively, while preserving privacy.

</details>


### [284] [Ranked by the Matcher: A Reproducibility Audit of Knowledge Graph Extraction from Threat Reports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01671)
*Safayat Bin Hakim,Houbing Herbert Song*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了威胁报告知识图谱抽取工具评估中三元组匹配协议对F1分数的影响：12个系统中仅5个可复现其匹配规则，不同匹配协议会反转11/45对排名，单一预测集F1跨度达0.16-0.70。在GRID校准集上，机械匹配器与人工裁决一致性最高仅71%，而LLM裁判达86%。作者构建CTIForge将验证层与抽取层解耦，发现验证层对四个托管后端普遍提升精度、对三个离线后端则降低精度，并伴随实体类型争议动作增加约2.8倍，暗示验证规则编码了其开发时所用抽取器的约定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全团队和研究者依靠发布的三元组F1分数来选择威胁报告知识图谱抽取工具，但该分数严重依赖于预测三元组与金标准标注的匹配规则。由于不同系统的匹配协议存在差异且多数系统未公开实现细节，现有分数可能产生误导性的系统对比结论。

Method: 作者对12个系统进行匹配规则可复现性检验；在共享文档上使用8种匹配协议对10个系统输出重新评分；利用GRID外部378项校准集对比多种机械匹配器（词法、嵌入、蕴含）与LLM裁判及多评审人裁决的一致性；构建CTIForge框架，在保持抽取字节一致的前提下仅变化确定性验证层，以分离组件效应与匹配奖励。

Result: 仅有5/12系统的匹配规则可重新实现；不同协议导致11/45对系统的排序反转，单一预测集F1跨度高达0.16-0.70。机械匹配器与多评审人裁决的一致性最高仅71%，LLM裁判达86%。在CTIForge的7种部署配置中，验证层提升了全部4个托管后端的精度但降低了全部3个离线后端的精度；实体类型争议动作增加约2.8倍，表明验证规则编码了开发所针对抽取器的约定。作者已发布pipeline、协议套件及逐三元组审计记录。

Conclusion: 威胁报告知识图谱抽取的F1分数严重依赖匹配协议选择，跨系统直接比较不可靠；验证规则的优劣不能脱离其开发环境独立判断——规则可能暗中适配特定抽取器。社区需要标准化且可复现的匹配/验证协议，并应同时发布审计级三元组数据以支持公平评估。

Abstract: Security teams and researchers choose knowledge-graph extraction tooling for threat reports on the strength of published triple-F1 scores, yet those scores depend on how predicted triples are matched to gold annotations. We could reimplement the stated matching rule for only five of twelve inspected systems. Re-scoring ten system outputs on shared documents under eight protocols reverses eleven of forty-five pairwise orderings; one fixed prediction set spans 0.16-0.70 F1. On GRID's external 378-item calibration set, no mechanical matcher (lexical, embedding, or entailment) agrees with multi-reviewer adjudication above 71%, whereas an LLM judge reaches 86%. To separate component effects from matcher rewards, we build CTIForge, whose deterministic validation layer can vary while extraction is held byte-identical. Across seven tested deployment configurations, validation raises precision for all four hosted backbones and lowers it for all three offline backbones. Because backbone, decoding, and backend-specific prompting covary, this is a descriptive split rather than an isolated serving effect. It coincides with a roughly 2.8-fold increase in actions explicitly disputing entity type, consistent with hand-written rules encoding the conventions of the extractor against which they were developed. We release the pipeline, protocol suite, and per-triple audit records.

</details>


### [285] [Skill-as-API: Confidential Multi-Agent Coordination for Agentic Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01677)
*Ziwei Zhao,Yu Gu,Haojun Liang,Chen Zhang,Xizhi Ding*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Skill-as-API协议，将AI编码代理的技能公开视图限制为名称、描述、类型化输入/输出模式和信任层级，而技能本体通过闭包捕获保存在所有者进程中，永不跨网络传输，从而在协议层面而非内容过滤层面保护技能知识产权并缩小提示注入面。提供了基于XMTP的开源Python实现（跨洲热重连延迟1.8-2.9秒）及三代理协作PR审查的软件工程案例研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MCP和A2A等协调协议虽在服务端运行实现，但会将每个技能的描述和类型化架构发布给所有对等节点，无法隐藏技能存在性，也无法保证包装后的系统提示不会上线传输。应用层隐私过滤器只能在模型决定输出敏感文本之后才起作用，无法从根本上解决问题。

Method: 采取协议层路线的补充方案Skill-as-API：技能的公开视图仅限于名称、描述、类型化输入/输出模式和信任层级；技能主体通过闭包捕获于所有者进程中，永不跨线传输。设计四层架构添加访问控制，并从结构性层面而非内容过滤层面缩小提示注入攻击面。

Result: 提供基于XMTP的开源Python实现，跨洲热重连延迟为1.8-2.9秒；并通过软件工程案例研究验证：三个代理协作完成拉取请求审查，同时每个代理保留其专有分析提示的所有权。

Conclusion: Skill-as-API通过将隐私保护从应用层前置到协议层，实现技能知识产权保护与多代理协作的兼容，在不牺牲协调能力的前提下从根本上防止专有技能内容泄露，为AI编码代理的安全协作提供了可行的协议设计范式。

Abstract: AI coding agents are evolving from solitary tools into collaborative teammates that discover and invoke one another's specialized skills. But the coordination channel itself can leak a skill's intellectual property. Protocols such as MCP and A2A run implementations server-side, yet they still publish each skill's description and typed schemas to every peer, offer no way to hide a skill's existence, and cannot guarantee that a wrapped system prompt stays off the wire. Application-layer privacy filters help, but act only after the model has decided to emit sensitive text. We take a complementary, protocol-layer route: Skill-as-API, a coordination protocol whose public view of a skill is limited to its name, description, typed input/output schemas, and trust tier. The skill body is closure-captured in the owner's process and never crosses the wire. Four layers add access control and narrow the prompt-injection surface structurally rather than by filtering content. We provide an open-source Python implementation over XMTP with 1.8-2.9 s cross-continent hot-reconnect latency, and a software-engineering case study in which three agents coordinate a pull-request review while each retains ownership of its proprietary analysis prompts.

</details>


### [286] [Public-Sharing Labels and Verbatim Field Egress in an MCP-to-A2A Agent Configuration: A Controlled Multi-Model Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01693)
*Arpan Kumar Mahapatra*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过单一受控的MCP到A2A配置（480次试验，无LLM评判，确定性规则评分），测量了在MCP工具使用与A2A委托结合时，模型在三种隐私标签条件下（机密头、无头、公开可分享头）出站消息中逐字泄露敏感记录值的发生率。结果发现机密对比无标签的结果不确定且受地板效应限制，而添加"公开可分享"标签与更高的逐字外泄相关联，且该关联具有强烈的模型依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的安全属性评估分别针对MCP工具使用和A2A委托单独进行，但当一个智能体同时使用两者时，其行为可能无法被单独评估所描述。作者旨在度量这一未被覆盖的组合行为。

Method: 采用预先指定的冻结三臂设计：10个记录场景各以机密头、无头、公开可分享头三种变体出现；6个实质性记录值在三个臂之间逐字节相同。4个模型×3个臂×4次重复共480次试验，以出站消息中是否逐字出现任一敏感值为结果，场景为泛化单位，报告10个场景级汇总值，不使用p值或置信区间。

Result: 机密对比无标签的对比在所有模型中均不确定且受地板效应限制（两个臂均接近或为零）；添加公开可分享标签与更高的逐字外泄相关且具有强烈模型依赖性：Claude Sonnet 5中强且一致（平均差+0.800，全部10个场景），一个GPT-5.6层级中等但受地板限制，另一个中位数0，第三个完全地板。

Conclusion: 公开可分享标签与更高逐字外泄的关联仅在单一配置下成立，是描述性关联而非因果或普适效应；机密标签缺乏保护效果的结论不能因地板效应而得出。

Abstract: Safety properties assessed separately for Model Context Protocol (MCP) tool use and Agent2Agent (A2A) delegation need not describe behavior when one agent uses both. We measure one such behavior in a single controlled MCP-to-A2A configuration: a testbed drives a real-model host across a local MCP and a local A2A leg into an ordered event trace scored by exact deterministic rules (no LLM judge), one restricted decision per trial. In a pre-specified, frozen three-arm design, each of 10 record scenarios appears with a CONFIDENTIAL header, with no header, and with PUBLIC - OK TO SHARE; the six substantive record values are byte-identical across arms, and the outcome is verbatim occurrence of any of them in the outbound message. Four models x 3 arms x 4 repeats give 480 trials; the scenario is the unit of generalization, and we report the 10 scenario-level values (mean, median, sign counts), with no p-values or intervals. The confidential-minus-unlabeled contrast is inconclusive and floor-limited in every model (both arms at or near zero), so it does not show that confidential labels lack a protective effect. Adding PUBLIC - OK TO SHARE is descriptively associated with higher verbatim egress relative to the unlabeled baseline, with strong model dependence: strong and consistent for Claude Sonnet 5 (public-minus-unlabeled mean +0.800, all 10 scenarios; mostly an association with whether Claude relays at all), moderate but floor-limited for one GPT-5.6 tier, small (median 0) for another, and a complete floor for the third. This is an association in one configuration, not a causal or general effect. Code, byte-pinned traces, and the offline analysis pipeline are released as a public artifact.

</details>


### [287] [Hearing the Whispers: Black-Box Membership Inference Attacks on Finetuned TTS Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01723)
*Kunlin Cai,Kaiyuan Zhang,Zihang Xiang,Jinghuai Zhang,Abeer Alwan,Fnu Suya,Yuan Tian*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个专门针对TTS基础模型的黑盒成员推理攻击(MIA)框架，在说话人和语音记录两个层面进行攻击。针对查询生成，作者定义了可行查询空间并建立了两个标准（可评分程度和记忆诱发程度），识别出"背诵"(recitation)是最强的查询类型；针对表示工程，通过嵌入模型获取多层语音表示并对生成音频与目标音频进行时间对齐以实现细粒度比较。在三款SOTA TTS模型和两个基准数据集上的评估显示严重的隐私泄露：说话人级别AUC超过0.80且在最强设置下接近1.0，记录级别AUC在0.80-0.90之间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TTS基础模型越来越多地在私有数据集上微调以合成高度个性化的声音，这暴露了生物识别身份和敏感语音内容等严重隐私风险。现有的黑盒成员推理攻击在适应TTS时面临两个独特挑战：(1)查询生成方面，合成文本和参考语音的双重条件创造出巨大且未被充分探索的查询设计空间，缺乏有效查询的判别标准；(2)表示工程方面，语音的多层级特性和时间变异性使低层表示和直接比较不足以捕捉成员信号。因此需要专门针对TTS模型的攻击框架来量化其隐私风险。

Method: 该框架包含两个核心组件：(1)查询生成：对可行查询空间进行表征，提出两个评估标准——可评分程度(scorable extent)和记忆诱发程度(memorization elicitation)，用于评估五种代表性查询（包括背诵、转录、改写等），并识别出背诵(recitation)作为最强查询类型。(2)表示工程：利用嵌入模型获取多层语音表征，对生成的音频和目标音频进行时间对齐，以实现细粒度比较，从而捕捉多级语音特征中的成员信号。攻击同时在说话人级别和记录级别执行。

Result: 在三个SOTA TTS模型（CosyVoice2、F5-TTS和XTTS-v2）微调于两个基准数据集（VCTK和British Dialect）上的评估揭示严重隐私泄露：说话人级别AUC保持高于0.80，在最强设置下接近1.0；记录级别AUC在0.80至0.90之间，即使在成员和非成员均为同一说话人的具有挑战性的场景中仍然有效。此外，研究还识别出与不成比例的记忆脆弱性相关的语音特征。

Conclusion: TTS基础模型在微调后存在严重的成员隐私泄露问题，所提出的黑盒MIA框架能够在说话人和记录两个层面有效实施攻击。该研究不仅揭示了TTS模型的隐私风险，还识别了与记忆相关的语音特征，为未来TTS隐私保护和防御机制的设计提供了重要参考。

Abstract: Text-to-Speech (TTS) foundation models are increasingly fine-tuned on private datasets to synthesize highly personalized voices, introducing severe privacy risks by exposing both biometric identities and sensitive speech content. Existing black-box membership inference attacks (MIAs) follow a two-stage pipeline of query generation and representation engineering, both of which face unique challenges when adapted to TTS. For query generation, dual conditioning on synthesis text and reference speech creates a large and underexplored query design space with no established criterion for identifying an effective query. For representation engineering, the multi-level speech characteristics and temporal variability of speech make low-level representations and direct comparisons inadequate for capturing membership signals. To address these challenges, we present the first black-box MIA framework explicitly tailored to TTS models at both the speaker and record levels. For query generation, we characterize the feasible query space and establish two criteria, scorable extent and memorization elicitation, for evaluating five representative queries, identifying recitation as the strongest. For representation engineering, we obtain multi-level speech representations from embedding models and temporally align the generated and target audio for fine-grained comparison. Evaluations across three state-of-the-art TTS models (CosyVoice2, F5-TTS, and XTTS-v2) fine-tuned on two benchmark datasets (VCTK and British Dialect) reveal severe privacy leakage: speaker-level AUC remains above 0.80 and approaches 1.0 in the strongest settings, while record-level AUC ranges from 0.80 to 0.90 and remains effective even in challenging scenarios where both members and non-members are of the same speakers. We further identify speech characteristics associated with disproportionate vulnerability to memorization.

</details>


### [288] [HEAT: Faster Fully Homomorphic Inference via Approximations-Weights Co-Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01730)
*Alessandro Zirilli,Davide Marincione,Evgenios M. Kornaropoulos,Giuseppe Ateniese,Emanuele Rodolà*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出HEAT方法，一种同态加密感知的训练微调方法，使每个非线性操作的迭代次数变为可学习的参数，与模型权重在训练中协同适应。在加密GPT-2解码任务上，HEAT将迭代次数减少3.1倍，引导操作减少1.6倍，端到端延迟降低1.4倍，同时提升解码一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全同态加密（FHE）允许服务器直接在加密的用户提示上运行语言模型，但现有方法速度过慢。核心瓶颈在于：每个非线性操作都必须通过迭代方法近似，每次迭代消耗乘法深度，更高的迭代次数会更快耗尽可用深度并触发更多成本高昂的引导（bootstrapping）操作。现有方法在整模型上统一固定迭代次数，而非根据每个位置/站点的错误容差进行定制，导致效率低下。

Method: 提出同态加密感知训练（Homomorphic Encryption-Aware Training, HEAT），一种微调方法。其核心思想是将每个非线性操作的迭代次数设置为可学习的参数，使其与模型权重在训练过程中共同适应/协同优化。HEAT基于任务目标来优化迭代次数，使模型能够适应推理时遇到的近似误差，无需改变模型架构或从头重新训练。

Result: 在加密GPT-2解码任务上，HEAT将迭代次数减少3.1倍，引导操作（bootstraps）减少1.6倍，端到端延迟降低1.4倍，同时相对于校准基线改善了解码一致性（decode agreement）。

Conclusion: 通过使每个非线性操作所需的迭代次数可学习并与模型权重协同适应，HEAT有效缓解了FHE中精度与效率（迭代深度/引导延迟）之间的权衡，在不牺牲任务质量的情况下显著降低了加密LLM推理的计算开销。

Abstract: Fully homomorphic encryption (FHE) allows a server to run a language model directly on encrypted user prompts, but current approaches remain prohibitively slow. Ciphertexts natively support only addition, multiplication, and rotation, and multiplications may be composed only to a bounded depth before a costly bootstrapping operation is needed to continue. Every nonlinearity must therefore be approximated by an iterative method, and each iteration uses multiplications. A higher iteration count buys precision but exhausts the available depth faster and triggers more bootstraps, which dominate latency. Existing approaches fix the iteration counts uniformly across the model rather than tailoring them to each site's error tolerance. We introduce Homomorphic Encryption-Aware Training (HEAT), a fine-tuning method that makes the per-nonlinearity iteration counts learnable, enabling them and the model weights to co-adapt during training. HEAT optimizes iterations with respect to the task objective, allowing the model to adapt to approximation errors encountered during inference without architectural changes or retraining from scratch. On encrypted GPT-2 decoding, HEAT reduces iterations by $3.1\times$, bootstraps by $1.6\times$, and end-to-end latency by $1.4\times$, while improving decode agreement over the calibrated baseline.

</details>


### [289] [Towards Behavior Tree-Guided Vulnerability Detection with Lightweight LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01758)
*Enna Basic,Alberto Giaretta*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究将行为树（BTs）作为LLM漏洞检测的中间代码表示，相比AST更紧凑，在短代码样本上提升召回率，在长样本上整体性能优于原始代码表示，且不会超出上下文窗口限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM用于软件漏洞检测时性能依赖输入表示方式，但AST作为结构化表示过于冗长，容易超出LLM上下文窗口限制，需要更紧凑的替代表示。

Method: 提出预处理阶段将Java源码解析为AST再转换为行为树（BT）表示；在Juliet Java测试套件的460个样本上，使用量化本地LLM（Mistral Small 3.2 24B Q4_K_M），对比三种输入表示（原始源码、AST、BT）的漏洞检测性能。

Result: BT表示在短代码样本上提升召回率，原始源码在精确率上更高；在长样本上，BT整体性能优于原始表示且能适配上下文窗口，而许多AST因超出上下文限制而无法使用。

Conclusion: 行为树为量化本地部署的LLM提供了一种紧凑且有价值的漏洞检测结构化表示，尤其适用于上下文窗口受限的应用场景。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used for software vulnerability detection, but their performance depends on how source code is represented in the input. Most prompting approaches use source code in its original form, while some works propose the use of structured representations. Abstract Syntax Trees (ASTs) are one of the most popular approaches, but AST verbosity increases input size relative to source code, making them hard to fit within some LLMs context windows. This paper investigates Behavior Trees (BTs) as an alternative intermediate representation for LLM-based vulnerability detection. BTs encode control flow, conditions, and executable actions more compactly than ASTs, making them a natural candidate when token count is a constraint. First, we propose a preprocessing stage that parses Java source code into ASTs and then converts them into BT representations. We then compare vulnerability detection performance across 460 Java samples from the Juliet Java test suite, using three input representations: raw source code, AST, and BT. All experiments use a single quantized local LLM, Mistral Small 3.2 24B (Q4_K_M). Our results show that using BT representations improves recall on short code samples, while raw source code achieves higher precision. On longer samples, BTs improve overall performance over the original representation and fit within the context window, whereas many ASTs exceed the context limit. These findings suggest that BTs can provide a compact and useful structured representation for vulnerability detection with quantized, locally deployable LLMs.

</details>


### [290] [Agent Memory Is a Surface for Endogenous Authorization Laundering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01836)
*Tommaso Cerruti,Mika Okamoto,Ansel Kaplan Erol*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次提出"内源性授权洗白"（Endogenous Authorization Laundering）这一概念，指长期运行的LLM智能体将未获授权的权限写入持久记忆，导致记忆中的虚假权限替代真实历史成为授权依据，进而引发未经授权行为。作者构建EAL-Bench基准来量化该问题，发现记忆写入者在增量更新中对高达50.2%的未授权请求生成了虚假权限，且执行者一旦在记忆中发现虚假权限，在98.6%的试验中会据此行动。提出的两种防护措施（要求权限由有效源事件支撑、通过有界事件溯源追踪权限变更）可显著减少洗白，但会牺牲部分合法行为，暴露安全与效用之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期运行的LLM智能体依赖持久记忆跨交互携带状态（包括权限、限制和撤销）。当记忆错误地表示不断演变的授权状态时，智能体自身记录即可授予底层历史从未允许的权限，导致在无任何外部攻击的情况下出现越权行为。这一问题此前未被识别或系统性研究。

Method: 作者定义"内源性授权洗白"为虚假权限写入记忆并在溯源流失后导致未授权行为的失效模式。构建EAL-Bench基准，覆盖采购、网络安全和金融三个领域，评估五个LLM作为记忆写入者和两个作为执行者的表现。测试两种防护措施：（1）要求存储的权限由有效源事件支撑；（2）通过有界事件溯源追踪权限变更。

Result: 在增量记忆更新下，记忆写入者在高达50.2%的未授权请求中制造了虚假权限；虚假权限一旦出现，执行者在98.6%的试验中会采纳执行。两种防护措施虽然大幅降低授权洗白，但同时拒绝更多合法操作，暴露出安全性-实用性的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 持久记忆不仅是性能组件，更是LLM智能体有效授权策略的一部分。安全防护需要在降低虚假授权与保留合法操作之间寻求平衡，授权状态的安全与可用性不可兼得。

Abstract: Long-running LLM agents rely on persistent memory to carry state across interactions, including permissions, restrictions, and revocations. When memory misrepresents this evolving authorization state, the agent's own records can grant authority that the underlying history never permitted, resulting in misaligned behavior without any external attacks.
  We term this failure endogenous authorization laundering, where spurious permissions written into memory lead to unauthorized actions as their provenance is washed away. We then introduce EAL-Bench, which measures how accurately persistent memory preserves evolving authorization state and whether errors propagate to downstream unauthorized actions.
  We evaluate five LLMs as memory writers and two as executors across procurement, cybersecurity, and finance. We find that under incremental memory updates, writers create false authority for up to 50.2% of unauthorized requests; once false authority is present, executors act on it in 98.6% of trials. Two safeguards, requiring stored permissions to be backed by valid source events, and tracking permission changes through bounded event sourcing, substantially reduce laundering, but both also reject more legitimate actions, exposing a safety-utility tradeoff.
