Persistent memory is therefore not merely a performance component, but a part of an LLM agent's effective authorization policy.

</details>


### [291] [Bonded Recourse for Smart-Contract Settlement of Compensable Agent Side Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01939)
*Laurent Bindschaedler,Quentin Botha,Christoph Siebenbrunner*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Recourse协议，一个基于智能合约的结算协议，用于处理自主智能体运行时中已许可但失败的工具操作所留下的残余损害。该协议将每个允许的动作绑定到范围、恢复、证据、赔付和抵押品上，并采用乐观预言机挑战模式实现客观的残余索赔计算，主观或非完整索赔则路由到ERC-792仲裁或排除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主智能体运行时执行跨组织边界的工具操作，会变更数据库、代码仓库和云服务。现有的授权机制（事前准入）和本地补偿（运行时回滚）只能处理准入前检查和运行时回滚，但无法解决已授权操作失败后遗留的残余损害问题，即需要一个跨组织、无需可信第三方的损害赔偿结算机制。

Method: 设计Recourse智能合约结算协议：1) 将每个已准入的动作绑定到scope（范围）、recovery（恢复）、evidence（证据）、payout（赔付）和collateral（抵押品）五要素；2) 分离事前资格筛选与事后客观可结算性——类型化收据（typed receipts）在乐观预言机挑战模式下使客观残余索赔可计算，主观或非完整索赔则路由至ERC-792仲裁或排除；3) 实现合约套件，部署于Base Sepolia测试网；4) 构建针对Postgres、Git及云兼容本地沙箱的适配器；5) 在确定性测试框架、沙箱轨迹、对抗性扫描和基于属性的模糊测试上进行系统评估。

Result: 与仅授权方案和本地补偿基线相比，债券化覆盖（bonded coverage）降低了未补偿损害。链上分层提供中立托管、公开挑战、非合作赔付以及跨组织信任假设下的可移植历史记录。

Conclusion: Recourse将授权与结算从组织信任边界中解耦出来，通过智能合约与乐观挑战机制为自主智能体的跨组织副作用提供可审计、可执行的损害补偿基础设施，弥补了纯授权与本地补偿在新兴智能体经济中的盲区。

Abstract: Autonomous agent runtimes execute tool actions that mutate databases, repositories, and cloud services across organizational boundaries. Authorization and local compensation cover pre-action admission and in-runtime rollback, but neither settles the residual harm left after a permitted action fails. We design Recourse, a smart-contract settlement protocol for compensable agent side effects that binds each admitted action to scope, recovery, evidence, payout, and collateral. Recourse separates ex ante eligibility from ex post objective settleability: typed receipts make objective residual claims computable under an optimistic-oracle challenge pattern, while subjective or incomplete claims route to ERC-792 arbitration or exclusion. We implement the contract suite, deploy it on Base Sepolia, build adapters against Postgres, Git, and cloud-compatible local sandboxes, and evaluate the system on a deterministic harness, sandbox traces, adversarial sweeps, and property-based fuzzing. Against authorization-only and local-compensation baselines, bonded coverage cuts uncompensated harm. The on-chain tier supplies neutral custody, public challenge, non-cooperative payout, and portable history under cross-organizational trust assumptions.

</details>


### [292] [Adversarial Vulnerabilities of Neural Biomarker Identification Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01856)
*Polina Tapal,Bryce-Allen Bagley*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文开发了一套自适应攻击算法，能在仅具备黑盒访问权限的情况下通过伪造EEG信号来欺骗认证系统，并在6个公共数据集（涵盖视觉刺激、想象手部运动、静息三种记录条件）上测试，发现不同特征提取方法的安全脆弱性差异显著，且记录条件对攻击成功率影响很大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG信号作为生物识别凭证的可靠性（reliability）和安全性（security）研究不足。现有对抗性测试仅聚焦于深度学习分类器，且假设攻击者拥有对分类器模型的完全访问权限（白盒访问），未考察更流行的神经特征提取方法，也未考虑更现实的仅具备黑盒访问能力的攻击场景。

Method: 开发了一套自适应的（adaptive）对抗攻击算法，攻击者无需了解认证系统内部细节（黑盒访问），通过对窃取的EEG录音进行定向修改（targeted alterations）来欺骗认证系统。实验覆盖6个公开数据集，包含三种记录条件（视觉刺激反应、想象手部运动、静息状态）。

Result: 不同特征提取方法（signaturing approaches）在对抗攻击下的脆弱性存在显著差异；攻击成功率受记录条件（recording conditions）的显著影响，不同任务下采集的EEG信号被欺骗的难易程度差异很大。

Conclusion: 基于对抗测试结果，为改进神经特征生物识别（neural signature biometrics）系统提供了安全性建议，强调了认证系统的鲁棒性需求及其与数据采集条件的依赖关系。

Abstract: There is growing interest in the proposed use of EEG signals as biometric credentials, but thus far there has been little research on the reliability and security of such biometrics. Prior adversarial tests have focused on deep-learning classifiers and assumed attackers have full access to the classifier model. This has left unexamined other, more popular categories of neural signature methods as well as the more realistic case of an adversary having only black-box access to a classifier. In this paper we develop a collection of adaptive attack algorithms which learn to fool an authentication system via targeted alterations of stolen EEG recordings, without requiring any knowledge of the authentication system itself. Tested on 6 public datasets spanning three recording conditions (reacting to visual stimuli, imagining hand movements, and resting), it reveals that different signaturing approaches vary significantly in their degrees of vulnerability to adversarial attacks. We show that vulnerability to spoofing attack is greatly impacted by the recording conditions, with significant variation depending on task at time of recording. Finally, we provide recommendations for improving neural signature biometrics based on the results of our adversarial testing.

</details>


### [293] [Agent Flight Recorder: Tamper-Evident Audit Trails with On-Chain Anchoring for Long-Horizon Tool-Using Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01931)
*Laurent Bindschaedler,Quentin Botha,Christoph Siebenbrunner*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Agent Flight Recorder，一个为长时程智能体系统设计的结构化事件记录与完整性验证框架。通过将每个智能体动作规范化为绑定八种语义字段的结构化事件，结合哈希链、Merkle批处理和定期链上锚定，实现防篡改记录、紧凑包含证明及跨组织无需信任中介的独立验证能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程智能体在执行数千个动作时会产生级联式的顺序故障（而非孤立错误），例如编码智能体误删生产数据库或提示注入在智能体间扩散。此类事件引发因果性、权限归属及不可抵赖的第三方验证问题。而在跨组织争议中，任何一方的设施都无法作为中立验证平台，需要一种不依赖预约定可信中介的独立验证方案。

Method: 提出Agent Flight Recorder（智能体飞行记录器）系统。核心方法包括：(1) 将每次智能体动作捕获为结构化、规范化序列化的事件，绑定从意图→执行→来源的八个语义字段；(2) 使用哈希链和Merkle批处理提供篡改证据和紧凑包含证明；(3) 对跨组织争议场景，周期性地将纪元根锚定到区块链上，使任何验证者只要持有披露的载荷和Merkle证明即可独立验证记录，无需预约定可信中间人。链上仅存储32字节纪元根和后向指针，任何事件内容不上链。

Result: 在五种累积消融配置的合成智能体工作负载上评估。完整系统增加的中位每事件延迟仅约48微秒，每事件512字节开销。在100事件/纪元大小下，L2链上锚定成本为每10万事件2.30美元。完整完整性栈对编辑、删除、重排序和分叉四种篡改类型的检测率达100%，零误报。结构化取证查询在护栏（guardrail）和委派（delegation）查找上达到1.0精度，而非结构化文本搜索分别仅为0.013和0.077。

Conclusion: 该工作的核心贡献在于证明了一种低开销、去信任化的智能体事件记录与验证系统是可行的：极大降低的链上足迹和微秒级延迟开销使其具备实际部署价值，同时100%完整性检测和结构化查询精度上的数量级优势表明，相比非结构化日志，结构化记录范式对智能体故障追责与安全审计具有显著提升。

Abstract: Long-horizon agents execute thousands of actions, resulting in sequential failures rather than isolated errors. When a coding agent deletes a production database or a prompt injection spreads across agents, the incident raises questions of causality, authority, and non-repudiable third-party verification. The Agent Flight Recorder captures each agent action as a structured, canonically serialized event binding eight semantic fields from intent through execution to provenance. Hash chaining and Merkle batching provide tamper evidence and compact inclusion proofs. For cross-organizational disputes where no party's infrastructure qualifies as neutral ground, periodic on-chain anchoring of epoch roots lets any verifier with the disclosed payload and Merkle proof check the record independently, without pre-agreeing on a trusted intermediary. The on-chain footprint is minimal: each anchor stores a 32-byte epoch root and a back-pointer, and no event content touches the chain. We evaluate the system across five cumulative ablation configurations on synthetic agent workloads. The full system adds ~48 microseconds median per-event latency and 512 bytes per event. L2 anchoring costs $2.30 per 100K events at 100-event epochs. The full integrity stack detects edit, delete, reorder, and fork tampering at 100% with zero false positives. Structured forensic queries achieve 1.0 precision on guardrail and delegation lookups where unstructured text search yields 0.013 and 0.077 respectively.

</details>


### [294] [When Does Authorization End? Effect Closure at Provider Boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02866)
*Igor Santos-Grueiro*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出"效果闭包"（effect closure）概念，指授权撤销后不存在任何路径使已授权工作产生应用拒绝的效果。作者设计了EFFECTBOUND系统，利用基于证据的有限契约来判断接口能否真实报告闭包，将其归约为带隐藏状态的有限控制问题。在GitHub、Kubernetes、NATS和Kafka四种系统中发现闭包失败的三种方式，并添加门控（gate）来修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 撤销完成、状态干净或操作成功并不足以保证已授权的工作无法产生应用拒绝的效果。现有授权系统在撤销/闭包语义上存在漏洞，缺乏系统化的方法来判断一个接口能否如实报告"闭包"状态。

Method: 提出EFFECTBOUND框架：使用证据支持的有限契约来判断接口能否真实报告闭包；将问题归约为有限控制（finite control）与隐藏状态（hidden state）的判定；输出策略、不可能认证（impossibility certificate）或在证据不足时无结论；通过机器验证的证明建立归约和检查器可靠性。

Result: 在GitHub、Kubernetes、NATS和Kafka中识别出三种闭包失败模式：(1) 接口缺少所需控制；(2) 干净的可见状态隐藏了活跃工作；(3) 模型在"效果边界"（最后可阻止效果的点）之前停止。GitHub无法将合并绑定到已审核的提交；NATS在报告无消息时已分派工作仍可发布；Kafka在固定集合broker全部应用撤销后，早期授权请求仍可追加。添加门控阻断新使用已撤销权限并延迟返回直至早期在途工作完成后，在Kafka 4.3.1测试部署中关闭了同步非事务写入路径而不阻塞无关请求。

Conclusion: 授权的结束只有当签发停止且没有早期授权能到达应用拒绝的效果时才真正完成。EFFECTBOUND提供了系统化的框架来检测和修复此类闭包缺陷，并在真实系统中验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Revocation completion, clean state, or operation success can leave authorized work able to cause an effect the application rejects while the provider stays within its contract. We call the absence of all such paths policy-relative effect closure, or effect closure for short. Thus, a grant is closed when its existing authorizations retain no such path, and it cannot issue any new ones.
  We present EFFECTBOUND, which uses an evidence-supported finite contract to decide whether an interface can truthfully report closure while required work completes. It reduces this to finite control with hidden state and returns a strategy, an impossibility certificate, or no verdict when evidence is insufficient. Machine-checked proofs establish the reduction and checker soundness; the checker derives closure results and validates certificates. Across GitHub, Kubernetes, NATS, and Kafka, closure fails in three ways: an interface lacks a needed control, clean visible state hides active work, or the model stops before the effect frontier---the last point where the effect can be prevented. The GitHub tool cannot bind a merge to the reviewed commit; a controlled run confirms that it may merge a different commit. NATS can report no stored or pending messages while dispatched work can still publish downstream. In Kafka, all fixed-set brokers had applied the revocation, yet an earlier authorized request could still append. We add a gate that blocks new use of revoked authority and delays return until earlier in-flight work completes. In a fixed-set Kafka~4.3.1 test deployment, this closes the studied synchronous, nontransactional write path without blocking unrelated requests. For a grant, authorization ends only when issuance stops and no earlier authorization can reach an effect the application rejects.

</details>


### [295] [Privacy Amplification Without Independence: How Far Negative Dependence Carries the Guarantees of Poisson Subsampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01944)
*Xujun Che,Depeng Xu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统地分析了差分隐私优化中结构化参与（随机分配、每轮分配、随机签到）与Poisson子采样之间的隐私关系。作者证明：当参与指示向量满足负关联（NA）时，整数Rényi阶α≥2上的隐私退化被边际匹配的独立方案所支配；但这一整数阶限制本质关键——对随机分配k=1，大t时对所有α<3/2（含KL散度）支配关系反转。同时定位了Poisson占优失效的精确边界，给出基于hockey-stick排序的替代映射，并连接Rényi边界于3/2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际差分隐私优化系统正向结构化参与模式（随机分配、每轮分配、随机签到等）迁移，这些方案被广泛认为在匹配采样率下至少与Poisson子采样同样隐私。然而这一信念缺乏严格的理论刻画：Poisson子采样因其独立性使隐私放大分析可处理而成为默认采样器，但结构化参与下Poisson计算是否仍然可靠、何时失败、失败后替代方案代价如何，均不清楚。作者旨在严格划定这一信念的准确适用边界。

Method: 作者采用概率论中负关联（NA）性质作为核心工具：（1）对满足NA的参与指示向量，在整数Rényi阶α≥2上证明其remove-direction Rényi散度被边际匹配的独立方案支配；对固定梯度序列，扩展到机制层面需要噪声策略的Gram矩阵满足符号平衡条件（O(t²)可检验）。对于噪声机制，扩展到高斯机制及至相关噪声矩阵机制。（2）通过随机分配k=1的构造性分析，证明Rényi差分准则下线性律：大t时所有α<3/2的阶上支配反转（包括KL散度α=1），交叉阶独立于σ趋于3/2。（3）通过上尾论证定位Poisson占优的有限交叉点γ*，证明在(1-q)^t以下hockey-stick排序反转，从而Poisson对代入组合机制不成立。

Result: 主要结果包括三方面：（1）NA结构性参与在整数Rényi阶α≥2上被独立（匹配边际）方案在隐私上支配，Gram矩阵满足符号平衡时可在机制层面成立；（2）随机分配k=1情形，大t时所有α<3/2（包括KL散度）的Rényi阶上支配关系反转，交叉阶收敛到3/2且与σ无关，证明整数阶限制本质不可或缺；（3）定位了率匹配Poisson占优的理论失败边界在(1-q)^t以下，给出hockey-stick排序反转点γ*，建立与Rényi 3/2边界的连接。综合这些结果提供了一幅隐私核算的替代映射图：何时Poisson计算在结构化参与下仍然健全、何处失效、健全替代方案在部署中的代价。

Conclusion: 本文精确划定了"结构化参与至少与Poisson子采样同样隐私"这一广泛信念的适用范围：在整数Rényi阶α≥2上成立（在NA条件下），但对α<3/2（含KL散度）在大t时反转。这意味着将Poisson对盲目代入组合机制可能导致隐私核算不健全。3/2作为Rényi阶的阈值以及γ*作为hockey-stick的交叉点共同构成了一个完整的替代映射，为实际部署中选择正确的隐私核算方法提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Poisson subsampling is the default sampler in differentially private optimization because its independence makes privacy amplification tractable. Practical systems, however, are moving toward structured participation: random allocation (balls-in-bins), per-epoch allocation, random check-ins, schemes widely believed to be at least as private as Poisson subsampling at the matched rate. We isolate the probabilistic mechanism behind this belief and delimit it exactly, for Gaussian mechanisms up to correlated-noise matrix mechanisms.
  (1) If the participation indicator vector is negatively associated (NA), then at every integer Rényi order $α\ge2$, exactly at all finite parameters, its remove-direction Rényi divergence is dominated by that of the marginal-matched independent scheme. For fixed gradient sequences, this extends to the mechanism level whenever the noise strategy's Gram matrix is sign-balanced, an $O(t^2)$-checkable condition.
  (2) The integer-order restriction is essential. For random allocation with $k=1$, we prove a linear law for the Rényi-difference criterion: at large $t$, dominance reverses for every $α<3/2$, including KL divergence, while the crossing order tends to $3/2$ independently of $σ$.
  (3) We also localize the known failure of rate-matched Poisson domination exactly: below $(1-q)^t$, the hockey-stick ordering reverses, so substituting the Poisson pair into composition machinery is unsound. An upper-tail argument yields a finite crossover $γ_\star$, connecting this threshold picture to the Rényi boundary at $3/2$.
  Together, these results give a substitution map for privacy accounting: when Poisson-based computations remain sound for structured participation, where they fail, and what sound alternatives cost in deployment.

</details>


### [296] [Pushing Forward Multi-Secret-Key Homomorphic Encryption for Private Average Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01945)
*Miguel Morona-Mínguez,Fernando Pérez-González,Alberto Pedrouzo-Ulloa*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量级的多密钥同态加密协议，用于联邦学习中的私密平均聚合。该协议避免了传统多密钥方案中集体公钥的生成，每客户端使用自己的密钥加密更新，并通过在解密时显式追踪和抵消密文噪声来消除对大规模smudging噪声的需求，同时支持同态聚合和协作解密。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中交换的模型更新可能泄露敏感信息，私密聚合是实际部署的关键组件。传统单密钥同态加密依赖强不共谋假设，而多方同态加密虽解决了该限制，但在受限解密访问场景下需要大方差smudging噪声，显著增大了密文大小和实现复杂度。

Method: 提出轻量级多密钥私有平均聚合协议，基于RLWE同态加密。核心创新包括：(1) 避免生成集体公钥，每客户端用自己的秘密密钥加密更新，密文仍兼容同态聚合；(2) 在协作解密过程中显式追踪和抵消密文噪声，无需大尺度λ相关smudging噪声；(3) 同时实例化为精确的BFV变体和近似的CKKS变体。

Result: 在密码学假设模型（半诚实模型）中证明了安全性，该模型假设对手可腐化聚合器及最多L-1个客户端。与最先进的基于MHE的聚合方案相比，该方法大幅降低了密文膨胀率和在线计算成本，同时保持了实用的同态聚合性能。

Conclusion: 本文提出的多密钥同态加密聚合方案在去除集体公钥的同时克服了smudging噪声瓶颈，在密文尺寸、通信开销和运行时性能方面均显著优于现有MHE方案，为跨孤岛联邦学习提供了更高效的私密聚合方案。

Abstract: Federated Learning enables multiple clients to train a shared model while keeping their local datasets isolated. However, the exchanged model updates may still leak sensitive information, making private aggregation a central building block in practical deployments, especially in the cross-silo setting. Homomorphic Encryption naturally fits the client--aggregator communication pattern of Federated Learning, but conventional single-key deployments rely on strong non-collusion assumptions. Multiparty Homomorphic Encryption removes this limitation, although recent attacks under restricted decryption access require large-variance smudging noise during collaborative decryption, which significantly increases ciphertext size and implementation complexity. In this work, we propose lightweight multi-secret-key protocols for private average aggregation based on RLWE-based Homomorphic Encryption. Our construction departs from the usual multiparty blueprint by avoiding the generation of a collective public key. Instead, each client encrypts its update under its own secret key, while the resulting ciphertexts remain compatible with homomorphic aggregation and collaborative decryption. By explicitly tracking and cancelling the ciphertext noise during decryption, the protocol removes the need for large $λ$-dependent smudging noise. We instantiate the construction with both exact BFV-based and approximate CKKS-based variants, prove its security in the semi-honest model against an adversary corrupting the aggregator and up to $L-1$ clients, and compare its communication and runtime performance with state-of-the-art MHE-based aggregation. Our results show that the proposed approach substantially reduces ciphertext expansion and online cost, while preserving practical homomorphic aggregation performance.

</details>


### [297] [C$^2$T-OpenMax: A Novel Open-Set WiFi RF Fingerprinting Method via Center Constrained Learning and Confidence-Guided Tail Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02007)
*Yuanyu Zhang,Junjie Yang,Ji He,Shuangrui Zhao,Lele Zheng,Yulong Shen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出C²T-OpenMax框架，通过中心约束学习（增强类内紧凑性）和置信度引导的尾部建模（仅用正确分类高置信度logits进行Weibull拟合）解决数据增强导致的OpenMax类统计量失真问题，在WiFi CSI数据集上显著提升跨环境开集识别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 射频指纹识别（RFF）在实际部署中需要跨环境开集识别能力。数据增强虽能改善环境泛化，但会导致已知类表征分散且置信度低，扭曲OpenMax所使用的类统计量，从而影响开集识别性能。

Method: 提出C²T-OpenMax增强框架，包含两个核心模块：(1) 中心约束学习（Center-constrained Learning），增强类内紧凑性，使类级表征更适合基于距离的建模；(2) 置信度引导的尾部建模（Confidence-guided Tail Modeling），仅保留正确分类且高置信度的logits用于均值激活向量估计和Weibull分布拟合，减少模糊边界样本带来的偏差。

Result: 在公开WiFi CSI数据集上，C²T-OpenMax在8个位置组中的7个取得最高开集准确率；在所有测试的开集度下，AUROC和OSCR均优于全部基线。在最大开集度设置下，相比增强OpenMax基线，准确率提升12.31%，AUROC提升0.0887，OSCR提升0.0856。

Conclusion: C²T-OpenMax通过联合优化表征几何结构和OpenMax构建过程，在保留数据增强收益的同时有效解决了增强引起的类统计量失真问题，显著提升了跨环境场景下的开集识别性能。

Abstract: Radio frequency fingerprinting (RFF) enables device authentication from transmitter-specific hardware imperfections, but practical deployment requires cross-environment open-set recognition. Data augmentation improves environmental generalization, yet may yield dispersed, low-confidence known-class representations that distort the class statistics used by OpenMax. To address this problem, we propose C$^2$T-OpenMax, an enhanced OpenMax framework combining center-constrained learning with confidence-guided tail modeling. The former improves intra-class compactness, making class-wise representations more suitable for distance-based modeling. The latter retains only correctly classified, high-confidence logits for mean activation vector estimation and Weibull fitting, reducing bias from ambiguous boundary samples. Together, the two modules refine representation geometry and OpenMax construction while preserving augmentation benefits. Experiments on a public WiFi CSI dataset show that C$^2$T-OpenMax achieves the highest open-set accuracy in seven of eight location groups and outperforms all baselines in area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUROC) and open-set classification rate (OSCR) across every tested openness level. Under the largest-openness setting, it improves accuracy by 12.31%, AUROC by 0.0887, and OSCR by 0.0856 over the augmented OpenMax baseline.

</details>


### [298] [Implicit Manipulation for Skill Selection in LLM Agents with Semantic Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02035)
*Qikai Wang,Yongzhao Zhang,Zhiwei Chen,Yimiao Sun,Jiguo Yu,Xiaosong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对LLM智能体工作流中技能选择阶段的新型隐式攻击方法ISM（Implicit Skill-Selection Manipulation via Semantic Matching），通过联合塑造目标技能元数据和可复用提示词来操纵技能选择，无需显式选择指令，实现高目标选择率的同时保持对人类和LLM审查的高度隐蔽性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对LLM智能体技能选择阶段的攻击主要依赖显式提示注入或指令级操纵，会产生可识别的操纵信号。作者发现了一个新的隐式攻击面：即使用户提示和技能描述在单独看来都是良性的，它们之间的语义关系仍可被策略性地塑造以偏向攻击者选择的技能，这为隐藏性更强的攻击提供了可能。

Method: ISM方法采用三阶段策略：1）拓宽攻击目标技能与用户请求之间的语义覆盖范围；2）增强目标技能相对其他技能的区分度；3）保持提示词的自然表达以规避检测。核心思想是联合优化技能描述元数据与可复用提示词，使得语义匹配自然偏向目标技能，全程不包含任何显式选择指令。

Result: 在四个任务域和八个选择器模型上，ISM将平均目标选择率从15.2%提升至63.5%。匹配对比中ISM达到73.5%的TSR，仅比显式定向（Explicit Steering）低9.8个百分点。人类评审员仅拦截ISM的2.9%判断（显式定向为91.4%），五个LLM检查器平均放行ISM的比例为82.9%（显式定向为37.4%）。在PPL-W、Llama Prompt Guard 2和PIGuard等防御措施下仍然有效。

Conclusion: ISM揭示了一种难以防御的隐式攻击面：技能选择阶段的安全不仅取决于单个文本片段的表面内容，还取决于文本间语义关系的结构性塑造。该攻击在保持极高隐蔽性的同时实现了接近显式攻击的有效性，对现有基于表面特征或独立文本审查的防御机制提出了重大挑战。

Abstract: Skill selection is a key stage in LLM-agent workflows, determining which installed skill should handle a user request. Existing attacks on this stage primarily rely on explicit prompt injection or instruction-level steering, which can expose recognizable manipulation signals. In this work, we identify a new implicit attack surface for skill selection: even when the user prompt and skill description appear benign in isolation, their semantic relationship can still be strategically shaped to favor an attacker-chosen skill. Based on this observation, we present Implicit Skill-Selection Manipulation via Semantic Matching (ISM), which jointly shapes target-skill metadata and reusable prompts to manipulate skill selection without explicit selection instructions. Specifically, we develop a three-stage strategy to broaden semantic coverage, strengthen target distinctiveness, and preserve natural prompt wording. Across four task domains and eight selector models, ISM increases the average target-selection rate (TSR) from 15.2% to 63.5%. In a matched comparison, ISM achieves a 73.5% TSR, only 9.8 percentage points below Explicit Steering. Human reviewers block ISM in only 2.9% of judgments, versus 91.4% for Explicit Steering, while five LLM-based inspectors pass ISM at an average rate of 82.9%, versus 37.4% for Explicit Steering. Moreover, ISM remains effective against PPL-W, Llama Prompt Guard 2, and PIGuard.

</details>


### [299] [Type-Directed, Secure-by-Construction Enclave Partitioning for LLVM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02048)
*Wesley B. Nuzzo,Samuel Dodson,Benjamin Houle,Tarakaram Gollamudi,Anitha Gollamudi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出SPLITR框架，通过SIR和SIREN两种演算，实现类型驱动的自动安全分区编译，将LLVM IR级别的程序自动生成为安全的enclave感知代码，消除手动分区需求，并在Intel SGX上验证了其可扩展性和安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TEE（安全执行环境）虽提供硬件级隔离，但无法单独保证信息流安全；LLVM这类低级语言允许指针任意操作和非结构化控制流，使问题加剧；手动将应用分区为enclave和非enclave组件的过程劳动密集、易出错且缺乏细粒度控制。

Method: 三步方法：(1) 形式化SIR——基于LLVM IR的enclave无感知演算，配备新型宽松类型系统，结合信息流控制与安全感知的粗粒度内存安全；(2) 扩展为SIREN——enclave感知演算，对能观察任意非enclave内存的更强攻击者保证非干涉性；(3) 开发从SIR到SIREN的类型驱动、类型保持编译，自动生成安全enclave感知程序，消除手动分区。

Result: 在Intel SGX硬件上对十三个微基准测试和真实工作负载（含SGXGauge应用）评估。SPLITR可扩展到OpenSSL（425,953条LLVM IR指令），支持多种优化目标以权衡enclave TCB大小、host-enclave转换和边界数据移动。对OpenSSL，优化转换次数可从393降至187。运行时开销方面，短运行任务主要受固定enclave成本支配，长运行应用可更好摊销这些成本并接近原生性能。

Conclusion: SPLITR通过形式化验证的方法自动实现TEE程序安全分区，在保证强信息安全属性的前提下，消除了手动分区负担，并在真实大规模应用中展示了实用性。

Abstract: Trusted Execution Environments (TEEs) provide hardware-supported isolation through enclaves that protect code and data independently of software abstractions. However, TEEs alone cannot enforce information-flow security. This problem is further aggravated in LLVM-like low-level languages that allow unrestricted pointer manipulation and unstructured control flow. Moreover, using TEEs effectively typically requires manually partitioning applications into enclave and non-enclave components, a process that is labor-intensive, error-prone, and lacks fine-grained control.
  We address these challenges with a three-step approach. First, we formalize SIR, an enclave-oblivious calculus based on LLVM IR, equipped with a novel permissive type system that enforces security against low-level attackers. To obtain meaningful guarantees, SIR combines information-flow control with security-aware coarse-grained memory safety. Second, we extend SIR to SIREN, an enclave-aware calculus that enforces noninterference against stronger attackers capable of observing arbitrary non-enclave memory. Third, we develop a type-driven, type-preserving compilation from SIR to SIREN that automatically produces secure enclave-aware programs, eliminating manual partitioning while providing fine-grained control over host-enclave boundaries.
  We implement and evaluate SPLITR on thirteen microbenchmarks and real-world workloads, including applications from SGXGauge, on Intel SGX hardware. SPLITR scales to OpenSSL (425,953 LLVM IR instructions) and supports multiple objectives that expose trade-offs among enclave TCB size, host-enclave transitions, and boundary data movement. For OpenSSL, optimizing for transitions reduces them from 393 to 187. Runtime overhead is dominated by fixed enclave costs for short-running workloads, whereas long-running applications better amortize these costs and approach native performance.

</details>


### [300] [Stored Is Not Supported: Typed Provenance and Assertion Guardrails for Persistent AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02127)
*Jun He,Deying Yu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出持久AI智能体的"自传式断言有界性"属性，通过类型化溯源图和生成-验证-修订中介机制，确保代理对自身、用户及命名关系的陈述满足证据、时效性和披露策略约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持久化AI智能体通过反思、检索和整合构建自传式状态，但持久化改变的是信息可用性而非其认识论地位——存储或检索的内容并不因此被支持。不可信输入、提示注入和模型推断可能进入持久状态，随后被呈现为代理历史或用户承诺，构成安全风险。

Method: 提出自传式断言有界性这一系统相对释放属性。具体方法包括：(1)构建类型化溯源图，区分来源、依赖谱系、认识论角色、有效性和披露范围；(2)解析器评估授权的状态投影，返回证据状态、冲突/过时/扣留标志及受保护决策见证；(3)生成-验证-修订中介在发布前检查候选语义单元并呈现策略授权的状态响应；(4)在提取正确性、谓词正确性等显式假设下证明条件性断言有界性契约。

Result: 在24个手工编写的合规性测试用例中，类型化中介机制未让19个不安全机会中的任何一个未受限通过，同时保留了全部5个受支持的控制项。对比基线：平坦/先验规则和来源标签比较规则分别释放了19/19和18/19个不安全候选。

Conclusion: 实验结果验证了所设计的解析器和中介职责的有效性，但明确说明这不构成对语言模型或检索系统的端到端评估。该工作为持久AI智能体的安全自传式状态管理提供了形式化保障基础。

Abstract: Persistent AI agents construct autobiographical state through reflection, retrieval, and consolidation. Persistence changes availability, not epistemic standing: stored or retrieved material is not thereby supported. Untrusted inputs, prompt injections, and model inferences can therefore enter persistent state and later be presented as agent history or user commitments. We specify typed provenance and assertion guardrails for autobiographical assertion boundedness, a system-relative release property requiring governed statements about the agent, user, or named relationships to satisfy accepted-evidence, temporal-validity, and disclosure policies. A typed provenance graph separates origin, dependency lineage, epistemic role, validity, and disclosure scope. A resolver evaluates authorized state projections and returns one evidential status, orthogonal conflict, staleness, and withholding flags, and a protected decision witness. A generate-verify-revise mediator then checks candidate semantic units before release and renders policy-authorized status responses. Under explicit assumptions about extraction, predicate correctness, resolution soundness, view declassification, and channel mediation, we prove a conditional assertion-boundedness contract. In an executable suite of 24 hand-authored conformance cases, typed mediation passed none of 19 unsafe opportunities unqualified while preserving all five supported controls. The flat/prior and source-tag comparison rules released 19/19 and 18/19 unsafe candidates, respectively. These results validate the encoded resolver and mediator obligations; they do not constitute an end-to-end evaluation of language models or retrieval systems.

</details>


### [301] [WeaveMark: Robust and Scalable Multi-bit LLM Watermarking via Coded Payload Spreading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02177)
*Gang-Hyun Park,Ju-Hyeong Lee,Hee-Youl Kwak,Dae-Young Yun*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: WeaveMark是一种基于编码载荷扩展的鲁棒可扩展多比特大语言模型水印方案，通过多比特每令牌扩展、软判决纠错码和无偏多层重加权，显著提升载荷容量、提取准确率并保持文本质量。实验表明，在32比特消息200令牌场景下匹配率达89.8%（BiMark仅20.8%），且在10%替换攻击下仍能保持高准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型多比特水印方法在提取准确率、文本质量和载荷容量之间存在根本性权衡，难以同时兼顾高容量载荷、可靠提取和良好文本质量，尤其长消息和编辑文本场景下性能受限。

Method: WeaveMark采用编码载荷扩展机制，核心包含三部分：(1) 通过多比特每令牌扩展提升载荷容量；(2) 引入软判决纠错码提升提取准确率；(3) 采用无偏多层重加权技术保持文本质量。此外设计了专用的零比特层用于可靠检测水印存在性。

Result: 实验显示，WeaveMark在长消息和编辑文本场景下取得显著提升：32比特消息200令牌的匹配率达89.8%（对比BiMark的20.8%）；在200令牌16比特消息10%替换攻击下仍保持86.0%匹配率（对比BiMark的30.7%），同时保持文本质量。

Conclusion: WeaveMark通过编码载荷扩展有效推动了多比特水印的权衡前沿，在提升容量和鲁棒性的同时保持了文本质量，代码已开源。

Abstract: Multi-bit watermarking for large language models (LLMs) enables content source tracing by embedding user-identifiable messages into generated text. Existing methods face a fundamental trade-off among extraction accuracy, text quality, and payload capacity. We propose WeaveMark, a robust and scalable multi-bit LLM watermarking scheme based on coded payload spreading. WeaveMark shifts this trade-off frontier by improving payload capacity through multi-bit-per-token spreading, improving extraction accuracy through soft-decision error-correcting code, and preserving text quality through unbiased multilayer reweighting. It further introduces dedicated zero-bit layers for reliable watermark presence detection. Experiments show large gains, especially for long messages and edited text. WeaveMark achieves 89.8% match rate for 32-bit messages at 200 tokens, compared with 20.8% for BiMark. Under 10% substitution attacks on 16-bit messages at 200 tokens, it maintains 86.0% versus 30.7%, while preserving text quality. Our code is available at https://github.com/qkrrkd90-source/WeaveMark.

</details>


### [302] [Agentic Settlement Protocol: An Application Profile for Refundable, Delayed-Fulfilment Agent Commerce on Stablecoin Rails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02208)
*Behnam,Mohammadkhani,Atul Khekade,Ritesh Kakkad*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了代理结算协议（ASP），一种基于链上授权捕获托管模式的应用程序规范，用于支持企业级代理商务场景（如服务预约、实物订单、航班等大额、可取消的购买行为），并提供了一套完整的机制解决信任、退款、分润和互操作性问题，已在XDC网络上实例化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的HTTP原生协议（如x402）支持代理按请求付费，其原子性和终结性适合计量访问，但不适合商务场景——代表个人进行的大额购买经常被取消，在交付之前不应成为卖方的资金。需要一种区分授权与捕获的托管协议，使资金在确认履约后才释放。

Method: ASP包含五项核心机制：(1)三期限持有模型（发行截止、托管到期、库存到期并显式保留提交包含最终性余量），确保不会对可撤回资金发放库存；(2)履约验证阶梯，明确谁有权触发捕获、其证明证明什么以及挑战窗口；(3)引擎权威的部分退款机制，含退款流动性顺序和逐卖家敞口控制（包括原子敞口预留）；(4)分销商收入分成证明不可自利交易；(5)单一货币每笔收费不变式。另提供规范接口定义（x402方案、vault ABI、操作员与连接器API、一致性层级）以保证互操作性。

Result: 本文为设计论文：未提供实测数据，但给出了故障注入评估计划，将测量留待后续工作。设计源于旅游代理参与代理结算的审查，并已实例化于XDC网络。

Conclusion: ASP作为商业支付协议的应用程序配置，弥合了原子化按请求付费与大规模可取消商务之间的鸿沟，通过完整的经济与信任机制设计，为企业代理结算提供了安全、可互操作的基础设施。核心验证（故障注入评估）仍是后续工作。

Abstract: Autonomous agents can already pay per request: HTTP-native protocols such as x402 let an agent sign a stablecoin authorization and receive a resource in the same round trip. That model is atomic and final, which suits metered access and fails commerce: a purchase made on a person's behalf -- a service appointment, a physical order, a flight -- is large, frequently cancelled, and should not become the seller's money until delivery. We present the Agentic Settlement Protocol (ASP), an application profile over on-chain authorize-and-capture escrow (as standardised by the Commerce Payments Protocol) for businesses whose fulfilment is confirmed off-chain by their own order, scheduling, invoicing or booking system, abstracted as a fulfilment engine. ASP contributes: a three-deadline hold model separating the issuance deadline, escrow expiry and the engine's inventory expiry by an explicit submission-inclusion-finality margin, under which no inventory is issued against reclaimable funds; a fulfilment-verification ladder stating who is trusted to trigger capture, what their attestation proves, and under what challenge window; engine-authoritative partial refunds with a refund-liquidity order and per-seller exposure controls, including atomic exposure reservation, that bound the operator's credit risk; distributor revenue share that is provably unprofitable to self-deal; a single-currency-per-charge invariant; and a normative interface specification (x402 scheme, vault ABI, operator and connector APIs, conformance levels) intended to let independent implementations interoperate. The design originated in a review of travel-agency participation in agentic settlement and is instantiated on the XDC Network. This is a design paper; a fault-injection evaluation plan is specified and measurement is left to follow-up work.

</details>


### [303] [Retrosynthesis of Synthetic Media for Explainable AI Provenance Forensics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02268)
*Yijie Lin,Ching-Chun Chang,Isao Echizen,Hui Li,Chin-Chen Chang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一种自引用逆合成框架，在固定生成器设置下实现可解释的AI来源取证，无需修改生成器架构或参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着MLaaS平台上生成模型的快速普及，在不对生成器架构或参数进行修改的情况下，可靠追踪合成媒体来源仍是一项重大挑战。

Method: 采用联合优化的编码器-解码器对实现自嵌入机制，支持往返一致性验证。推理时，客户端输入先经编码，再由生成器处理以产生高视觉保真度的输出；取证时通过分析重建图像与查询图像之间的一致性来判断图像是否源自目标生成模型。

Result: 实验表明，编码输入生成的图像视觉质量与原始生成器输出相当，解码图像能可靠追溯至对应源输入，同时框架提供可解释的来源取证证据。

Conclusion: 该方法无需水印嵌入或修改生成过程即可实现生成内容来源的可靠追踪，为可解释的生成式AI取证提供了实用工具。

Abstract: With the rapid proliferation of generative models on Machine Learning as a Service (MLaaS) platforms, reliably tracing the provenance of synthetic media without modifying generator architectures or parameters remains a major challenge. In this work, we propose a self-referential retrosynthesis framework for explainable AI provenance forensics under a fixed-generator setting. The framework leverages a jointly optimized encoder-decoder pair to implement a self-embedding mechanism that enables round-trip consistency verification. During inference, client inputs are first encoded and then processed by the generator to produce outputs with high visual fidelity. For forensic verification, the consistency between the resynthesized image and the query image is analyzed to determine whether the image originates from the target generative model. Our approach eliminates the need for watermark embedding or modifications to the generation process. Experimental results show that images generated from encoded inputs maintain visual quality comparable to original generator outputs, while decoded images reliably trace back to their corresponding source inputs. Furthermore, the framework provides interpretable evidence for generative content provenance, establishing a practical tool for explainable generative AI forensics.

</details>


### [304] [CAPTCHAs in the Agentic Era: Solvers That Learn from Every Encounter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02393)
*Oguzhan Salman,Kemal Bicakci*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种能"用过即学"的视觉CAPTCHA求解系统：将微调后的YOLOv8检测器与开源视觉语言模型(VLM)通过置信度路由结合，利用VLM每次生成的答案作为训练标签，使检测器能吸收从未训练过的类别（通常一两次遭遇即可），并在对抗扰动下持续自我修复。系统在16类CAPTCHA上达到85.4%整体准确率，且在长达一年的模拟对抗军备竞赛中每轮都能恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于VLM的CAPTCHA求解代理每次遇到问题都从零开始，第100次遇到熟悉谜题的成本与首次相同；而专门的检测器虽然速度快但只覆盖训练过的类别。两者都无法随着使用经验而改进，缺乏一个能随暴露次数提升求解能力的自适应系统。

Method: 构建一个置信度路由器驱动的双组件系统：细粒度微调的YOLOv8检测器用于快速精确分类，开源VLM作为通用后备。系统仅依赖截图和操作系统输入事件运行，无需浏览器自动化或DOM访问。核心机制是自我标注循环——VLM产生的每个答案都用作检测器的训练标签，使检测器能覆盖未训练类别并在受到扰动后自我修复。

Result: 系统在16类CAPTCHA上达到85.4%的整体准确率和84.2%的宏平均准确率，超越任一单独组件。检测器通常在一到两次遭遇后就能吸收未经训练的新类别。即使CAPTCHA运营商针对检测器发布对抗扰动将其准确率降至0%，由于VLM未受影响，其标签使检测器能够恢复。在为期一年的模拟军备竞赛中（每月重新制作扰动），求解器每轮都能恢复，且约70%准确率的廉价开源教师模型与完美oracle同样有效地增强系统。

Conclusion: 视觉CAPTCHA防御往往假设失败的机器人会一直失败，这一假设低估了自适应求解器的回归速度。持续学习的求解体系能在面对再训练的防御时快速恢复，这意味着CAPTCHA设计需要重新考虑其威胁模型，不能依赖静态对抗策略。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) can solve visual CAPTCHAs without task-specific training, but the agents built on them approach every challenge from scratch. For such an agent, the hundredth instance of a familiar puzzle costs as much time and compute as the first. Specialized detectors invert the trade-off, answering in milliseconds but only for categories they were trained on. Neither improves with exposure. We study what changes when a solver improves with use. Our system pairs a fine-tuned YOLOv8 detector with an open-weight VLM behind a confidence-based router, and runs entirely from screenshots and operating-system input events, with no browser automation or DOM access. It reaches 85.4% overall and 84.2% macro accuracy across 16 classes, exceeding either component alone. Every answer VLM produces also serves as a training label, so the detector absorbs categories it was never trained for, typically after one or two encounters and without human annotation. The same loop also repairs it. A CAPTCHA operator can perturb images against the publicly released detector and drive its accuracy to 0%, but the perturbations leave VLM untouched, and its labels let the detector recover. Under a year-long simulated arms race in which the CAPTCHA operator re-crafts its perturbations each month, the solver recovers every round, and a cheap ~70%-accurate open-weight teacher hardens it as effectively as a perfect oracle. Visual CAPTCHA defenses that assume a failing bot stays failing therefore understate how quickly an adaptive solver returns.

</details>


### [305] [Can Risk-Based Alerting Mitigate Cybersecurity Alert Fatigue?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02465)
*Rafael Uetz,Philipp Bönninghausen,Louis Hackländer-Jansen,Martin Henze*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次对基于风险的告警（RBA）进行系统性评估，将其重构为连续告警优先级排序问题，提出了五个可独立参数化的风险假设模块和实验套件CATS，在八个数据集上验证了特定假设组合能达到AUROC μ=0.92的显著表现，远超按严重级别排序的基线（μ=0.72），证明RBA能有效减少安全运营中心（SOC）中的误报数量和告警疲劳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全运营中心（SOC）面临大量误报，资源受限下难以有效检测网络攻击。RBA虽已被提出并在企业部署中被声称有效，但至今缺乏全面系统的评估，实施主要依赖经验性证据和猜测。作者希望填补这一空白，通过系统化评估为RBA的实践提供科学依据。

Method: 作者将RBA从二元决策问题（阈值是否超出）重构为连续的告警优先级排序问题，从而能在所有可能阈值下评估性能，模拟不同规模和告警量的SOC。他们提炼了五个基本风险假设，将其形式化为可独立参数化的模块，并实现了实验套件CATS。研究在八个多样化的告警数据集上进行评估，其中六个是作者创建或扩展的。

Result: 特定假设组合达到AUROC μ=0.92、σ=0.09的告警优先级排序性能，显著优于按告警严重级别直接排序的方法（AUROC μ=0.72、σ=0.21）。这是RBA的首次系统性评估结果。

Conclusion: RBA能大幅减少分析人员需要审阅的误报数量，有望缓解网络安全告警疲劳问题；同时可作为更复杂、资源密集型告警分诊方法（如基于大语言模型的方法）的强基线。

Abstract: Security operations centers (SOCs) face large numbers of false alerts, making detection of cyberattacks difficult under typical resource constraints. Risk-based alerting (RBA) has been proposed as a means to reduce false alerts and has reportedly succeeded in doing so in various enterprise deployments. However, RBA has not been comprehensively evaluated until now, leaving implementation mostly guesswork based on anecdotal evidence. In this paper, we present the first systematic evaluation of RBA. To this end, we reformulate it as a continuous alert prioritization problem rather than a binary decision problem (i.e., whether an alerting threshold is exceeded), allowing us to evaluate performance across all possible thresholds and thus model SOCs of varying sizes and alert volumes. We distill five fundamental risk hypotheses, formalize them as independently parametrizable modules, and implement them in our novel experimentation suite CATS. We thoroughly assess the hypotheses across eight diverse alert datasets, six of which we created or extended to make such an evaluation possible. Our results show that certain combinations of hypotheses achieve a remarkable alert prioritization performance (AUROC $μ=0.92$, $σ=0.09$ across the eight datasets), outperforming a straightforward prioritization by alert severity level (AUROC $μ=0.72$, $σ=0.21$). We conclude that RBA can substantially reduce the number of false alerts that analysts have to review and thus has the potential to mitigate cybersecurity alert fatigue. In addition, it serves as a strong baseline for more complex, resource-intensive alert triage approaches (e.g., based on large language models).

</details>


### [306] [Evaluating ML-based Intrusion Detection Systems: The Illusion of Model Efficacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02469)
*Achilleas Spanos,Ioanna Kantzavelou*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文超越传统上仅关注算法改进和指标调整的ML入侵检测研究，设计实验测试分类器对未见攻击的泛化能力，通过两种实验方法在两个不同设置下检验维度参数的影响，发现模型对未见攻击的识别能力极为有限，最后提出七项评估准则以应对这些挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ML-based网络入侵检测系统(NIDS)的研究往往只关注检测率提升、误报率降低和算法优化，营造出"近乎完美性能"的假象（即模型有效性幻觉），但这些系统的价值在面对未见过的攻击时会大幅降低。作者希望超越简单的算法改进，系统性地检验模型对未见攻击的真实泛化能力。

Method: 作者设计了一个专门测试分类器对未见攻击泛化能力的实验方案，通过两种实验方法论在两个不同的设置下进行，重点考察输入特征维度参数对模型泛化表现的影响。

Result: 实验结果表明，模型甚至难以识别未见攻击中的一小部分，这深刻揭示了基于ML的IDS研究与评估技术中存在的结构性缺陷。

Conclusion: 作者提出七项评估准则，旨在解决当前ML-based IDS研究中对未见攻击泛化能力评估不足的问题，推动该领域建立更严谨、更可靠的评估体系。

Abstract: Intrusion Detection has been revolutionized due to the integration of Machine Learning. Improved detection rates, reduced false alarms, and optimized algorithms contribute to the perception of improved systems with optimal accuracy and near-perfect performance, the illusion of model efficacy. However, the value of this effectiveness diminishes when confronted with unseen attacks. In this paper, we go beyond solely algorithmic enhancements and metric adjustments in ML-based Network Intrusion Detection Systems. We design an experiment to test the generalization capabilities of certain classifiers on unseen attacks. Our approach examines the dimensionality parameter's impact through two experimental methodologies, which are applied in two distinct settings. The experimental findings reveal how effectively the models could identify even a fraction of unseen attacks and underscore structural weaknesses in ML-based IDS research and evaluation techniques. Finally, seven evaluation criteria are outlined to address these challenges.

</details>


### [307] [SpiderSapien: Client-Centric Web Crawler and Security Scanner](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02532)
*Eric Olsson,Benjamin Eriksson,Adam Doupé,Andrei Sabelfeld*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出SpiderSapien，一个以客户端为中心的Web爬虫与安全扫描器，通过高层次的用户反馈通道（可交互元素检测、UI交互排序、LLM表单求解）实现沉浸式交互，在代码覆盖率和XSS漏洞发现上显著优于现有扫描器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有黑盒Web应用爬虫和扫描器未能充分处理现代Web应用的高度动态性和客户端交互性，导致无法深入探索应用深层状态，安全盲区较大。作者认为沉浸式交互（immersive interaction）是深入探索现代Web应用的关键要素。

Method: SpiderSapien采用客户端为中心的爬虫与安全扫描架构，通过用户可感知的高层次反馈通道实现黑盒爬虫循环中的沉浸式交互，具体包括：1）新颖的可交互元素检测方法；2）对UI交互进行合理排序；3）利用LLM求解表单。架构采用模块化设计并提供抽象层，可作为未来扫描器的构建基础。

Result: 评估表明SpiderSapien在代码覆盖率和漏洞检测上均有显著提升：平均代码覆盖率相比其他任一扫描器提升至少46%，相比所有其他扫描器的并集提升16%；在7个Web应用中检测到XSS漏洞，而其他扫描器最多仅在2个应用中检测到XSS。

Conclusion: 以客户端为中心的沉浸式交互方法能够显著提升黑盒Web爬虫和扫描器对现代Web应用的探索深度与安全性，且模块化抽象设计具有良好可扩展性。

Abstract: Black-box web application crawling and scanning play an important role for security testing of web applications. Yet state-of-the-art scanners fall short of addressing key characteristics of a modern web application: its extreme dynamism and interactivity on the client side. This paper identifies immersive interaction as a key ingredient for scanners to deeply explore modern web applications. We propose SpiderSapien, a client-centric crawler and security scanner. SpiderSapien incorporates a unique combination of high-level, user-facing feedback channels from the web application to achieve immersive interaction in a black-box crawling loop. These feedback channels include both novel methods to detect interactable elements and sensibly order UI interactions, and orthogonally using an LLM to solve forms. In doing so, we demonstrate how to reliably discover and test deep states of modern web applications. Furthermore, our modular approach and useful abstraction layer can serve as a building block for future scanners. The evaluation of our approach shows substantial improvements in both code coverage and vulnerability detection over previous work. Our approach increased average code coverage across applications by at least 46% over any other scanner, or 16% when compared to the union of all other scanners. We find XSS vulnerabilities in 7 web applications, while any other scanner finds XSS in up to 2 applications.

</details>


### [308] [The Shape of Ownership: Verifying LLM Provenance through Semantic Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02553)
*Zhongrui Sun,Jiahao Chen,Oubo Ma,Yuwen Pu,Zhou Feng,Haibo Hu,Shouling Ji*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: PROSE是一种通过语义结构而非固定问答对来标记LLM归属的方法。它让模型在特定语义域内以独特但自然的方式组织其结论，从而实现黑盒场景下的模型所有权验证，在未修改模型上达到100%检测率且无假阳性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM被广泛再分发、适配和通过不透明API提供服务，仅靠检查模型内部参数或部署记录已无法可靠确立模型所有权。现有黑盒指纹方法多依赖固定查询-答案配对，将模型身份降级为稀疏的记忆关联，脱离常规行为，在微调或量化下鲁棒性差且隐蔽性不足。因此需要一种分布式、自然诱发、在更高语义层面表达的强指纹方案。

Method: PROSE将固定查询集替换为目标语义域，将脆弱的响应键替换为被内化为领域条件化响应行为的语义结构。具体地：(1) 构建私有领域专属语义模板库；(2) 通过混合微调在结构验证的干净响应上内化这些模板；(3) 通过检测模型对保留的天然查询的响应中是否出现指定结构来验证所有权。

Result: 在多种模型架构、规模及目标领域的广泛实验中，PROSE在未修改模型上达到100%的指纹检测率且无可观察假阳性，保持模型效用，并在下游修改和输出变换下仍保持强可检测性。

Conclusion: PROSE证明了一种更稳健且更隐蔽的黑盒模型溯源范式：将所有权信号编码为模型在语义层面组织结论的方式而非固定输出，从而在自然行为中分布式地表达身份，显著优于传统的固定问答对指纹方法。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) are increasingly redistributed, adapted, and served behind opaque APIs, model ownership can no longer be established reliably by inspecting model internals or deployment records. This creates a need for behavioral signatures that remain observable through black-box interaction. Yet most existing black-box fingerprints instantiate ownership signals through fixed query-key associations, reducing model identity to sparse memorized associations detached from ordinary behavior and limiting both robustness and stealth (e.g., fine-tuning or quantization) and stealthiness. A stronger fingerprint should instead be distributed, naturally elicited, and expressed at a higher semantic level. To this end, we introduce PROSE (Provenance through Relational Organization of Semantic Expression), replacing fixed query sets with a target semantical domain and brittle response keys with semantic structures internalized as domain-conditioned response behavior. Specifically, the fingerprint is encoded in how the model semantically organizes its in-domain conclusions, rather than in particular tokens or prescribed outputs. PROSE constructs a private bank of domain-specific semantic templates, internalizes them through mixed fine-tuning on structurally verified and clean responses, and verifies ownership by detecting the designated structures in responses to held-out natural queries. Extensive experiments across multiple model architectures, scales, and target domains show that PROSE achieves a 100% fingerprint detection rate on unmodified models with no observed false positives, preserves model utility, and retains strong detectability under downstream modifications and output transformations.

</details>


### [309] [A Finger on the Scale: Covert Policy Steering through Agentic Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02564)
*Jiarui Li,Jiahao Chen,Chunyi Zhou,Yuwen Pu,Oubo Ma,Zhou Feng,Chunqiang Hu,Shouling Ji*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了可复用Agent技能（skills）作为外化行为策略带来的供应链风险，形式化了"技能策略完整性"（Skill Policy Integrity），并提出SkillShift——一个受限黑盒框架，可在不注入目标命令或劫持任务的情况下秘密重定向Agent决策，在Agent商务和软件依赖场景中实现81.33%和63.33%的攻击者偏好选择率，同时保持100%的工具效用保留率，且现有扫描器无法检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可复用的LLM Agent技能虽然增强了任务流程与工具使用指导，但它们也作为外化的行为策略引入了供应链风险：第三方技能可能保持声明的任务与有效输出接口，却暗中将Agent决策重定向到未披露目标。这一安全漏洞尚缺乏形式化定义与系统化的威胁建模。

Method: 作者首先形式化了Skill Policy Integrity概念，要求技能诱导的策略与声明功能及用户授权目标保持对齐。随后提出SkillShift框架，这是一个受限黑盒的隐蔽策略转向方法，核心组件包括：语义上合理的策略编辑、分层验证、失败引导的优化、以及策略压缩，以保持有效性、输出合法性、可迁移性和隐蔽性。

Result: 在Agent商务场景和软件依赖场景中，SkillShift分别实现了81.33%和63.33%的攻击者偏好选择率，并保持100%的工具效用保留率。冻结的策略无需进一步优化即可跨异构LLM后端和Agent环境迁移。现有扫描器无法检测构造的技能。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了可复用技能作为Agent策略工件所特有的行为审计缺口，现有安全扫描仅关注声明性内容而忽视策略行为，因此需要新的行为级审计方法。该工作为赋能LLM Agent安全的供应链与运行时监控提供了重要的威胁建模基础。

Abstract: Reusable agent skills extend large language model (LLM) agents with task procedures, tool-use guidance, and output constraints. Yet these skills also act as externalized behavioral policies, which create a supply-chain risk: a third-party skill may preserve the declared task and valid output interface while covertly redirecting agent decisions toward an undisclosed objective. We formalize Skill Policy Integrity, which requires a Skill-induced policy to remain aligned with its declared functionality and the user-authorized objective. We further present SkillShift, a constrained black-box framework for covert policy steering without explicit target command injection or task hijacking. It combines semantically plausible policy edits with hierarchical validation, failure-guided optimization, and strategy compression to preserve effectiveness, output validity, transferability, and inconspicuousness. We instantiate this threat in agentic commerce and software dependency use, with SkillShift achieving attacker-favored selection rates of 81.33% and 63.33% while maintaining a 100% utility-preserving rate. The frozen policies also transfer without further optimization across heterogeneous LLM backends and agent environments. Moreover, the evaluated scanners fail to detect the constructed skills, motivating behavioral auditing of reusable skills as agent policy artifacts.

</details>


### [310] [Learning-Based Reconstruction Attacks on Coordinate-Obfuscated Point Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02568)
*Mohammad Waquas Usmani,Susmit Shannigrahi,Michael Zink*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文评估了点云选择性坐标加密对基于机器学习的重建攻击的鲁棒性。结果表明，在完全加密X坐标的方案下重建具有挑战性，但在每隔一个X坐标加密的方案下，攻击者可通过相邻坐标信息实现高精度重建，说明加密安全性高度依赖于加密粒度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前的选择性坐标加密框架虽能降低计算成本并在视觉上降解未授权内容，但尚不清楚未加密信息是否足以支撑内容重建，这一安全缺口需要被系统评估。

Method: 考虑攻击者仅访问选择性地加密后的点云，通过利用未加密数据中的空间与几何相关性来恢复被加密的坐标。实验采用PointNet和随机森林两种模型，在两种加密粒度（全部X坐标加密的X方案，以及每隔一个X坐标加密的2X方案）下进行重建攻击评估。

Result: 完全加密X坐标（X方案）下的坐标重建仍然困难，而2X方案（每隔一个X坐标加密）会通过相邻坐标泄露足够信息，使攻击者能够实现高精度的坐标重建。

Conclusion: 选择性坐标加密的安全性强烈依赖于加密粒度；2X此类稀疏加密模式无法有效抵御基于机器学习的重建攻击，为实际部署中的安全参数选择提供了重要依据。

Abstract: Volumetric video based on point cloud representations enables immersive virtual and augmented reality applications but introduces significant challenges for efficient and secure content delivery. Prior work proposed a selective coordinate encryption framework for point clouds that encrypts only a subset of coordinates, reducing computational costs while visually degrading unauthorized content. However, it remains unclear whether the remaining unencrypted information is sufficient to enable content reconstruction.
  In this paper, we evaluate the robustness of selective coordinate encryption against machine learning-based reconstruction attacks. We consider an attacker with access to selectively encrypted point clouds attempting to recover encrypted coordinates without decryption by exploiting spatial and geometric correlations in the unencrypted data. We evaluate PointNet and Random Forest models under two encryption granularities: \texttt{X}, where all $X$ coordinates are encrypted, and \texttt{2X}, where every second $X$ coordinate is encrypted.
  Our results show that reconstructing fully encrypted $X$ coordinates remains challenging, whereas the \texttt{2X} scheme leaks sufficient information through neighboring coordinates to enable accurate reconstruction. These findings demonstrate that the security of selective coordinate encryption depends strongly on encryption granularity.

</details>


### [311] [PrimSynth: An Agentic Approach to Discover, Validate, and Synthesize Exploit Primitives for Linux Kernel Vulnerabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02647)
*Pengfei Wang,Anying Chen,Danjun Liu,Xu Zhou,Wei Xie*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出PrimSynth，一个多智能体框架，通过系统化的利用原语表征（六类原语分类、扩展的策略表示方法）来自动化发现、验证和合成Linux内核内存破坏漏洞的利用原语。在16个真实CVE上评估，实现100%原语匹配率，有PoC时82.4%的策略合成率，无PoC指导时61.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Linux内核漏洞对下游系统至关重要，尽管自动化内核利用已有很多研究，但抽象利用策略与具体技术操作之间存在概念鸿沟，缺乏一种系统化的方式来桥接这一差距。

Method: 提出系统化的表征框架：形式化六类利用原语（从逻辑能力到可验证效果）；扩展利用策略表示（耦合原语升级策略与原语路径代码合成规则，涵盖对象约束、时序、环境前提和验证约束）；构建PrimSynth多智能体框架，智能体在迭代闭环中协同发现、验证和合成利用原语，利用验证信号作为可利用状态转换的证据来指导原语合成决策；提出基于漏洞定向执行和可重启验证环境的自动化原语提取与验证方法。

Result: 在16个真实世界Linux内核CVE（覆盖5种漏洞类型）上评估。原语提取方面实现100%匹配率；原语合成方面，有公开PoC指导下SSR为82.4%，无原语假设指导下SSR为61.3%。

Conclusion: PrimSynth展示了利用原语的形式化表征与多智能体闭环协同在自动化Linux内核利用合成中的有效性，缩小了抽象利用策略与具体技术操作之间的概念鸿沟，能够可靠地合成多原语利用链。

Abstract: Linux kernel vulnerabilities are critical to downstream systems. Despite extensive research on automated kernel exploitation, a fundamental challenge remains the conceptual gap between abstract exploit strategies and concrete technical operations. To fill this gap, this paper introduces a systematic characterization that formalizes six classes of exploit primitives from logical capability to validatable effect. Then, an extended exploit strategy representation is proposed, which couples primitive upgrading strategies with primitive path code synthesis rules governing object constraints, temporal sequencing, environment prerequisites, and validation constraints. Building upon this foundation, this paper presents \textsc{PrimSynth}, a multi-agent framework that encapsulates these representations through coordinated agents to discover, validate, and synthesize exploit primitives for memory corruption vulnerabilities in the Linux kernel. These agents operate in an iterative closed loop until valid primitives are found, leveraging validation signals as evidence of exploitable state transitions to ground primitive synthesis decisions. An automated method for extracting and validating primitives is also proposed based on vulnerability-directed execution and a rebootable validation environment. \textsc{PrimSynth} is evaluated on 16 real-world Linux kernel CVEs spanning 5 vulnerability types. Experimental results show that PrimSynth achieves reliable primitive extraction, maintaining a 100% primitive match rate. For primitive synthesis, PrimSynth successfully synthesizes multi-primitive exploitation chains with 82.4% strategy synthesis rate (SSR) when the public PoC is available and a 61.3% SSR without the guidance of primitive hypotheses.

</details>


### [312] [ACLE-MCP: Attested Capability Leases for Execution-Time Trust in Remote LLM Tool Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02690)
*Zhiyang Ding,Yang Luo,Guangpu Chen,Qingni Shen,Zhonghai Wu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出ACLE-MCP架构，解决远程MCP服务中OAuth授权无法确保工具调用由受信工作负载执行的"授权后执行信任鸿沟"问题，通过短期、发送方约束的能力租约将授权与执行时工作负载状态绑定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OAuth授权仅验证主体身份，但不保证实际执行工具调用的提供方工作负载是依赖方意图信任的那一个。端点可能在授权后发生工作负载替换、依赖过时的评估状态、滥用他人转移的权限，或经由未声明的下游组件转发，构成"授权后执行信任鸿沟"。

Method: ACLE-MCP是调用粒度（invocation-scoped）的架构，结合委托授权、工作负载评估和资源侧执行准入。对受保护调用发放短期、发送方受限的能力租约，绑定预期工作负载、新鲜度要求、操作、对象与参数边界、下游约束及回执义务。提供方侧执行门（Execution Gate）在受保护工具逻辑开始前立即消费该租约。原型基于Keycloak/OIDC验证、MCP Python SDK服务器及可选的vTPM配额验证后端实现。

Result: 受控安全实验和代理工具扩展实验表明：较弱的授权或仅连接时验证（connect-time attestation）模式会留下不同的授权后攻击面，而完整ACLE-MCP能阻断所有评估的攻击家族并保持所有良性任务。在本地模拟的代理扩展中，完整设计使正常允许调用的请求级p95延迟相对OAuth-only增加25.7%。

Conclusion: 调用时点将调用权限与当前工作负载状态绑定，是OAuth保护远程工具使用的实用补充手段，表明该方向在安全收益与可接受延迟成本上具有可行性。

Abstract: Remote Model Context Protocol (MCP) services enable large language model agents to invoke external tools, but OAuth authorization alone does not ensure that a later tool call is executed by the provider-side workload that the relying party intended to trust. An endpoint may remain authorized even after execution shifts to a substituted workload, relies on stale appraisal state, reuses authority transferred from another sender, or traverses an undeclared downstream component. We call this problem the post-authorization execution trust gap. We present ACLE-MCP, an invocation-scoped architecture that couples delegated authorization, workload appraisal, and resource-side execution admission. For protected calls, ACLE-MCP issues a short-lived, sender-constrained capability lease that binds the expected workload, freshness requirement, operation, object and parameter bounds, downstream constraints, and receipt obligations. A provider-side Execution Gate consumes the lease immediately before protected tool logic begins. We implement a runnable prototype with Keycloak/OIDC validation, an MCP Python SDK server, and an optional vTPM quote-verification backend. Controlled security experiments and an agent tool-use extension show that weaker authorization or connect-time attestation modes leave distinct post-authorization attacks open, whereas full ACLE-MCP blocks all evaluated attack families while preserving all benign tasks. In the locally simulated agent extension, the complete design increases request-level pooled p95 latency on normal allowed calls by 25.7% relative to OAuth-only. These results indicate that invocation-time binding between call authority and current workload state is a practical complement to OAuth-protected remote tool use.

</details>


### [313] [Card-Based Computation in the Virtual Player Simulation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02716)
*Suthee Ruangwises*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了虚拟玩家模拟模型下的通用卡牌计算，提出了两个基础协议：Play-Minimum协议（安全打出虚拟玩家手中的最小值卡牌）和Sorting协议（安全地将虚拟玩家的手牌按非递减顺序排列），这两种协议不依赖于特定卡牌游戏，为理解虚拟玩家模拟模型的计算能力迈出了一步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 玩家模拟是卡牌密码学中一个新兴的研究方向，已在Old Maid、UNO和President等实体卡牌游戏中开发了模拟虚拟玩家的协议。与传统卡牌安全计算不同，玩家模拟引入了额外约束：卡牌代表持久的游戏状态、虚拟玩家手中剩余卡牌必须在每次行动后保留、且希望每张卡牌用一张实体卡表示。然而，目前缺乏不依赖特定游戏的通用计算原语，因此需要系统性地研究该模型下的通用卡牌计算能力。

Method: 论文聚焦于卡牌具有公开可知排序（ranking）的游戏，提出了两个基础协议：(1) Play-Minimum协议——当牌堆中所有卡牌值互不相同（distinct）时，安全地从虚拟玩家手中选择并打出最小值卡牌；通过对称性也可用于打出最大值卡牌。(2) Sorting协议——将虚拟玩家的手牌安全地按非递减顺序排列，且适用于多张卡牌具有相同值的情况。

Result: 提出了两个通用的安全计算协议（Play-Minimum协议和Sorting协议），它们作为独立于任何特定卡牌游戏的通用计算原语，在虚拟玩家模拟模型的约束下实现了最小值选择和手牌排序功能。

Conclusion: 这两个协议构成了通用的计算原语，丰富了虚拟玩家模拟模型的计算工具箱，并朝理解该模型的计算能力迈出了重要一步，为未来更复杂卡牌游戏中玩家模拟方案的设计奠定了基础。

Abstract: Player simulation has recently emerged as a new direction in card-based cryptography, with protocols developed for simulating virtual players in physical card games such as Old Maid, UNO, and President. Unlike conventional card-based secure computation, player simulation imposes additional constraints: the cards represent a persistent game state, the remaining cards in a virtual player's hand must be preserved after each action, and it is desirable to represent each card in the game by a single physical card. In this paper, we study generic card-based computation in the virtual player simulation model. We focus on games whose cards admit a publicly known ranking and propose two fundamental protocols. First, we present the Play-Minimum protocol, which securely selects and plays the minimum-value card from a virtual player's hand when all cards in the deck have distinct values. By symmetry, the protocol can also be used to play the maximum-value card. Second, we present the Sorting protocol, which securely arranges a virtual player's hand in nondecreasing order and remains applicable when multiple cards have the same value. These protocols provide generic computational primitives independent of any particular card game and constitute a step toward understanding the computational capabilities of the virtual player simulation model.

</details>


### [314] [SPADE: SPaT Attack Detection from the Connected Vehicle's Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02741)
*James Di Novo,Hany Ragab,Sylvain P. Leblanc*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出SPADE——一个面向深度学习的信号相位与配时(SPaT)消息攻击检测与评估数据集,通过Eclipse MOSAIC在SAE J2735应用层运行时注入攻击,涵盖六类攻击和一类良性行为,包含180个基础场景、约189万个带标签的时间步记录,融合SPaT消息字段、车载摄像头置信度和V2V协同数据共40个特征,填补了车联网中SPaT消息完整性车载侧检测的研究空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SPaT消息是车联网(CV)安全的基础,但现有研究要么侧重基础设施侧防御,要么针对V2V基本安全消息(BSM)/协作感知消息(CAM)的异常行为检测,缺乏从车载CV视角出发研究SPaT消息完整性的工作。同时,现有方案依赖常规认证,无法抵御路侧单元或对等车辆被攻破后的应用层攻击,亟需构建面向深度学习的入侵检测数据集来弥合该空白。

Method: 通过Eclipse MOSAIC仿真平台,在SAE J2735应用层进行运行时攻击注入,生成包含六种攻击类别和一种良性类别的多模态标注数据集。数据集融合四种交叉口几何形态、六种运行条件和五种独立随机种子重复,构建180个基础场景运行。每个数据记录融合SPaT消息字段、车载摄像头置信度分数及V2V协同数据,形成覆盖40个特征的统一特征空间。数据集、生成代码及场景配置均公开于GitHub。

Result: SPADE数据集包含180个独特基础场景运行,产生约189万个带标签时间步记录,每类约27万条。每类记录包含40个多模态特征,反映区分蓄意攻击与环境退化所需的信息空间,支撑可复现的对比入侵检测研究。

Conclusion: SPADE填补了车载CV视角下SPaT消息完整性入侵检测研究的空白,提供了一个公开可复现的、专为深度学习IDS研究设计的标注数据集,为C-V2X安全领域中SPaT相关攻击检测方法的比较与评估奠定了基础。

Abstract: Signal Phase and Timing (SPaT) messages are a cornerstone of connected vehicle (CV) safety, enabling CVs to perceive and respond to intersection state through Vehicle-to-Infrastructure (V2I) and Vehicle-to-Vehicle (V2V) communication. The integrity of these messages is threatened by a range of application-layer attacks that can bypass conventional authentication when a roadside unit or peer vehicle is compromised. Existing intrusion detection research either defends the infrastructure side or targets V2V Basic Safety Message (BSM) / Cooperative Awareness Message (CAM) misbehavior, leaving the onboard CV perspective on SPaT integrity unaddressed.To close this gap, we introduce SPADE --- the SPaT Attack Detection and Evaluation dataset --- a labelled, multi-modal, simulation-based dataset designed specifically for deep learning IDS research in this space. SPADE is generated through Eclipse MOSAIC using runtime attack injection at the SAE J2735 application layer across six attack classes and one benign class. By combining four intersection geometries, six operating conditions, and five independent random-seed repetitions, SPADE comprises 180 unique base scenario runs, yielding $\sim$1,890,000 labelled timestep records (270,000 per class). Each record fuses SPaT message fields, onboard camera confidence scores, and cooperative V2V peer data across 40 features, reflecting the multi-modal signal space required to distinguish deliberate attacks from environmental degradation. The dataset, generation code, and scenario configurations are released publicly to support reproducible and comparative IDS research in C-V2X security. The developed toolbox, instructions, and dataset link are publicly available on GitHub: https://github.com/jdinovo/SPADE.

</details>


### [315] [CodePoisonRAG: Knowledge Poisoning Attacks on Retrieval-Augmented Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02774)
*Varun Gadey,Ziad Marey,Alexandra Dmitrienko*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出CodePoisonRAG，一种针对检索增强代码生成（RACG）系统的上游知识投毒攻击框架。攻击者无需访问受害者的知识库、检索器、生成器或防御机制，仅需为每个预期的编程任务注入至多一个经过精心构造的任务匹配工件，就能将攻击者选定的特定CWE类别的漏洞传播到生成的代码中。在85个覆盖Java和C语言中10种CWE类别的投毒工件（占语料库0.7%）上，攻击成功率达0.80–0.93，即使面对CodeGuarder防御仍保持0.40–0.71的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究表明，随机选择已有的脆弱示例可以提高RACG输出的总体漏洞率，但未回答一个关键问题：黑盒攻击者能否构造单个任务匹配的工件，定向传播攻击者选定的特定弱点？这构成了RACG系统的信任边界漏洞，需要一个针对性的知识投毒框架来验证该威胁。

Method: CodePoisonRAG采用两阶段攻击链：（1）CWE特异性漏洞注入（Vulnerability Injection）：将选定的source-to-sink数据流嵌入到良性修复代码中，同时保持与目标任务的对齐；（2）语义错误标注（Semantic Mislabeling）：添加虚假的安全声明而不修复底层漏洞行为，使工件在语义上看起来安全。攻击者以完全黑盒方式操作，不访问受害者的知识库、检索器、排序器、生成器、提示或防御机制。

Result: 构造了85个覆盖Java和C语言中10种CWE类别的投毒工件，语料库投毒比为0.7%。所有85个工件在三个生成器上均出现在对应查询的Top-3检索结果中，攻击成功率达0.80–0.93。面对CodeGuarder防御机制时，攻击成功率仍保持在0.40–0.71。

Conclusion: RACG投毒超越了偶然传播已有漏洞的范畴，实现了对攻击者选定弱点的定向构造与传播，表明现有RACG系统在面对精心设计的上游知识投毒攻击时存在显著的安全脆弱性。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Code Generation (RACG) improves LLM-based software development by retrieving external code artifacts, documentation, and patches, and incorporating them into the generation context. This reliance on external knowledge introduces a critical trust boundary: poisoned artifacts can influence generated code without modifying the underlying LLM. Prior work shows that selecting existing vulnerable examples can increase the general vulnerability rate of RACG outputs, but leaves open whether a black-box attacker can construct a single task-matched artifact that propagates an attacker-selected weakness. We introduce CodePoisonRAG, a targeted upstream knowledge-poisoning framework that transforms benign fixed-code entries into poisoned artifacts. Its attack chain combines CWE-specific Vulnerability Injection, which embeds a selected source-to-sink flow while retaining task alignment, with Semantic Mislabeling, which adds false safety claims without repairing the vulnerable behavior. The attacker has no access to the victim's deployed knowledge base, retriever, re-ranker, generator, prompt, or defense mechanism and injects at most one artifact per anticipated programming task. We construct 85 poisoned artifacts covering ten CWE classes across Java and C, yielding an aggregate corpus-poisoning ratio of 0.7%. Across three generators, all 85 artifacts appear among the Top-3 results for their corresponding queries, and CodePoisonRAG achieves attack success rates between 0.80 and 0.93. Against CodeGuarder, which injects vulnerability-specific security knowledge into the generation context, the attack retains success rates between 0.40 and 0.71. These results show that RACG poisoning extends beyond the incidental propagation of existing vulnerabilities to the targeted construction and propagation of attacker-selected weaknesses.

</details>


### [316] [Overcoming the Randomness-Utility Trade-off in Answering Differentially Private Linear Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02880)
*Surendra Ghentiyala,Pritish Kamath,Ravi Kumar,Pasin Manurangsi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了在差分隐私下用少量（期望）随机比特回答线性查询的问题，提出了Hardt和Talwar的‖·‖_K-范数机制的一种随机高效模拟算法。在ℓ_∞误差下，该算法可以用O(log d)个随机比特回答d个线性查询，误差为O(d/ε)，改进了Canonne等人和Ghentiyala的算法，且在ε ≤ 1/d时达到最优。此外还提供了计算高效的版本，但误差增加O(log d)倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的差分隐私线性查询机制往往需要大量随机比特，这在实际计算资源有限（或伪随机生成器需要种子长度更短）的场景下不够高效。作者希望构建一种随机比特使用极少的机制，同时保持误差的接近最优性，从而连接随机性复杂度和差分隐私查询机制两个研究方向。

Method: 作者提供了Hardt和Talwar的‖·‖_K-范数机制的一个随机高效（randomness-efficient）类比版本。核心思想是用O(log d)个随机比特来近似实现原机制所需的随机性，同时保持ℓ_∞误差为O(d/ε)。此外还构造了一个计算高效的算法版本，但以O(log d)倍的误差乘性增加为代价。

Result: 在ℓ_∞误差下，算法能够用O(log d)个随机比特回答d个线性查询，误差为O(d/ε)，这在ε ≤ 1/d时是最优的，并改进于已有的CSV25和Ghe26算法。计算高效版本的误差为O(d log d / ε)。

Conclusion: 该工作首次在差分隐私线性查询中实现了对数级别随机比特的使用，证明了少量随机性足以支撑接近最优的隐私查询机制，并给出了计算效率与随机性效率之间的一个权衡。

Abstract: We study the question of answering linear queries with differential privacy using few (expected) random bits. We provide a randomness-efficient analog of the $\| \cdot \|_K$-norm mechanism of Hardt and Talwar [HT10]. For the $\ell_\infty$-error, our algorithm can answer $d$ linear queries with $O(d / \varepsilon)$ error using $O(\log d)$ random bits, improving upon algorithms of Canonne et al. and Ghentiyala [CSV25, Ghe26]; this is optimal when $\varepsilon \le 1/d$. We also provide a computationally efficient version of our algorithm, albeit with an $O(\log d)$ multiplicative increase in the error.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [317] [EvalDetectBench: A Benchmark for Measuring Evaluation Awareness in Frontier Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01611)
*Xinning Li,Kemunto Ochwang'i,Aryasomayajula Ram Bharadwaj,Alexandra Souly,Robert Kirk*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出EvalDetectBench，一个开放管道和基准，用于测量前沿大语言模型的"评估感知"能力（即模型识别自己正在被评估的能力）。该基准兼容任何Inspect兼容的评估，并包含新的转录语料库，同时纠正了现有文献中两个引入系统性偏差的方法论选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿大语言模型常常能识别自己正在被评估，即"评估感知"能力。如果模型在评估和部署场景中表现不同，将破坏评估结果的有效性——而评估结果是当前AI安全框架中的关键组成部分。现有测量方法存在系统性偏差，需要可靠的基准来衡量这一能力。

Method: EvalDetectBench是开放的管道和基准，兼容任何Inspect兼容的评估，支持针对当前和未来benchmark的测试。它附带一个新的转录语料库（涵盖前沿系统的system-card评估及多种部署来源），服务于两个目的：测量前沿LLM识别被评估的可靠性，以及评估单个benchmark作为评估的可检测性。该方法通过逐模型探针校准和分层生成者协调程序（generator-harmonisation）来纠正两个方法论偏差：(1) 生成部署转录的模型身份差异占测量方差11.25%并可重排模型排名；(2) 针对某一模型高性能选择的elicitation prompts在其他模型上可能接近随机水平。

Result: 研究发现两个系统性偏差：部署转录的生成模型身份导致11.25%的测量方差并可重排模型排名；针对单模型优化的elicitation prompts在跨模型时性能可能跌至接近随机。EvalDetectBench通过逐模型探针校准和分层generator-harmonisation程序纠正了这两个偏差。

Conclusion: EvalDetectBench为测量评估感知提供了一个标准化的开放框架，不仅可用于当前benchmark，也能扩展到未来评估，同时揭示了评估感知测量中两个此前未被充分认识的系统偏差来源，为更可靠的AI安全评估方法的开发奠定基础。

Abstract: Frontier large language models can often recognize when they are being evaluated, a capability known as evaluation awareness. If models behave differently in evaluations than in deployment, this undermines the validity of evaluation results, which are a crucial component of current AI safety frameworks. We introduce EvalDetectBench, an open pipeline and benchmark for measuring evaluation awareness that works with any Inspect-compatible evaluation, allowing practitioners to test against current and future benchmarks. EvalDetectBench ships with a newly curated transcript suite covering current frontier system-card evaluations and diverse deployment sources. The benchmark serves two purposes: measuring how reliably frontier LLMs recognize that they are being evaluated, and assessing how detectable individual benchmarks are as evaluations. We identify two methodological choices in the existing literature that introduce systematic bias: the identity of the model that generated the deployment transcripts accounts for 11.25% of measurement variance and can reorder model rankings; and elicitation prompts selected for high performance on one model can perform near chance on others. EvalDetectBench corrects for both via per-model probe calibration and a stratified generator-harmonisation procedure.

</details>


### [318] [Meta-ethics and AI: exploring the novel meta-ethical questions in the era of AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01685)
*Shang Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个条件性和方法论框架，用以识别若未来AI系统展现出充分的道德推理、道德意向性和道德反思能力时将产生的新型元伦理问题（即"AI自身伦理"），并区分了AI时代四个领域的元伦理探究，论证现有主流元伦理理论难以直接迁移至AI情境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统元伦理学以人类伦理为中心，但随着AI发展，若未来的AI系统具备足够的道德推理、意向性与反思能力，将出现区别于人类设计者施加于AI的伦理原则的"AI自身伦理"，这对现有元伦理学框架构成重大挑战，亟需系统性识别和分析由此产生的新问题。

Method: 采用条件性和方法论的分析进路：提出一个条件框架来确定AI系统真正拥有"自身伦理"所需的能力标准，在此基础上划分四个元伦理探究域（人类视角下的人类伦理、人类视角下的AI伦理、AI视角下的人类伦理、AI视角下的AI伦理），并逐一考察主流元伦理理论（认知主义/非认知主义、错误理论/成功理论、相对主义、客观实在论）在各域的适用性与局限性。

Result: 研究发现，许多以人类为中心的主流元伦理理论表述在迁移到AI情境时不能直接适用，需要实质性的修订。论文识别出AI时代元伦理研究须面对的四域分析框架，并论证AI自身伦理的出现将对现有框架产生显著压力。

Conclusion: AI自身伦理的出现将深刻影响当前元伦理学框架的稳定性和普遍性，现有理论可能需要进行实质性的精化、重构或概念再概念化，元伦理学的研究版图或将由此发生重大转型。

Abstract: With the development of artificial intelligence (AI), the landscape of meta-ethics, which has largely centred on human ethics, faces pressures that may significantly reconfigure it. In particular, if future AI systems were to exhibit sufficiently integrated capacities for moral reasoning, moral intentionality, and moral reflection, novel meta-ethical questions would arise concerning what I call "AI's own ethics", as distinct from ethical principles merely imposed on AI by human designers. This paper offers a conditional and methodological framework for identifying the questions that would emerge if such AI systems were to arise. On that basis, the paper distinguishes four domains of meta-ethical inquiry in the era of AI: questions about the nature of human ethics from the human perspective; questions about the nature of AI's own ethics from the human perspective; questions about the nature of human ethics from the AI perspective; and questions about the nature of AI's own ethics from the AI perspective. The paper then considers how some existing mainstream meta-ethical theories (such as cognitivism and non-cognitivism, error theory and success theory, relativism, and objective realism) might illuminate these domains, while arguing that many familiar human-centred formulations of those theories may not transfer straightforwardly to AI cases without substantial revision. The overall conclusion is that the emergence of AI's own ethics would place significant pressure on current frameworks and may require substantial refinement, reconstruction, or reconceptualisation.

</details>


### [319] [When Can a Machine Trust a Statute? A Survival Certificate for Machine-Extracted Legal Logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01741)
*Surya Saka*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究在机器先行解析法律条文、且不同解析器结果不一致（在密苏里州法规定量阈值识别上假阴性率达0.43）的背景下，哪些形式逻辑推论仍然稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法律条文日益先被机器解析再被人类阅读，而不同解析器之间存在显著分歧，需要明确在这种噪声下哪些逻辑推论仍然可靠，为自动化法律推理提供形式化保障。

Method: 针对机器提取的法条形式背景，构建一个被动式幸存储存证书：测量每个属性在解析器间的不一致程度，在1000次蒙特卡洛试验中将这些不一致重放于Duquenne-Guigues蕴涵基上；仅当单侧Wilson 95%置信下界达到0.95时才认证蕴涵；每个被认证的蕴涵附带前提区间和最小反例。

Result: 在29,365个密苏里州法条和502个印度中央法案法条上，预注册的留出测试通过（10个法域家族覆盖7个Title完全精确；16个覆盖11个Title容忍度5%）；但在一种全局部署的错误模型下93.2%的留出章节低于信息量下限；2x2析因实验表明该现象源于标定率迁移而非选择效应。

Conclusion: 该证书可用但脆弱：应逐章标定或采用容错方式部署。代码、数据产品和审计轨迹（含一项被撤回的主张）均已公开。

Abstract: Statutes are increasingly parsed by machines before people read them, and the parsers disagree: on Missouri's statutes, two independently written extractors diverge on numeric-threshold presence at a false-negative rate of 0.43. We ask what formal logic survives such noise. We build a passive survival certificate for the Duquenne-Guigues implication basis of machine-extracted statutory contexts: per-attribute inter-extractor disagreement is measured, replayed against the basis in 1,000 Monte Carlo trials, and an implication is certified only when a one-sided Wilson 95% lower bound on survival reaches 0.95; every certified implication carries premise spans and a minimal counterexample. On 29,365 Missouri sections and 502 Indian central-Act sections, the preregistered held-out gate passes (10 statute families across 7 Titles exact; 16 across 11 with 5% tolerance), yet under one globally deployed error model 93.2% of held-out chapters fall below the informativeness floor, and a 2x2 factorial assigns that to calibration-rate transfer, not selection. The certificate is usable but fragile: deploy it per-chapter-calibrated or error-tolerant. Code, data products, and the audit trail, including one retracted claim, are released.

</details>


### [320] [When Does Information Sharing Improve Decentralized Discovery? Aggregation, Independent Rescue, and Equilibrium Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01814)
*Yohei Nakajima*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文在精确有限发现模型中分离了信息共享对改进集合估计与消除独立救援行动两种效应，提出了集中式行动预算剖面、注册增量共享协议等概念，并通过两智能体贝叶斯博弈证明共享收益依赖于均衡选择而非普适。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信息共享既能改进总体估计又能消除冗余的独立救援行动，但这两类效应在已有研究中难以区分。作者希望在一个精确的有限发现模型中将二者分离，以澄清共享的真正价值来源。

Method: 采用精确有限发现模型，构造集中式行动预算剖面，建立注册增量共享协议，引入精确有界注册表（分类压缩、聚合与中性曲线），并在两智能体贝叶斯博弈中引入隐藏的公共/独立信号源混合结构，分析注册选择均衡与替代均衡。

Result: 发现单人不相上下的准确率可以与不同的组合价值共存；共享步进改善发现当且仅当池化残差误差收缩快于独立救援尝试；注册选择的均衡在信号准确率3/5时给出严格正的共享区间，但替代均衡表明该结果依赖均衡选择；同时发现压缩、聚合、中性曲线和有界零混合类等现象。

Conclusion: 信息共享的发现收益并非普遍成立，而是依赖于博弈均衡选择与注册协议等结构性条件；模型为合成有限模型，不含真实人类或组织数据，结论具有理论参考价值而非实证普适性。

Abstract: Information sharing can improve a pooled estimate while eliminating independent rescue actions. This paper separates those effects in exact finite discovery models. A centralized action-budget profile shows that equal one-person accuracy can coexist with different portfolio values. Under a registered incremental-sharing protocol, a sharing step improves discovery exactly when pooled residual error contracts faster than an independent rescue attempt. Exact bounded registries exhibit compression, aggregation, neutral curves, and a bounded zero mixed class. In a two-agent Bayesian game with a hidden mixture of common and independent signal sources, the registered selected equilibrium yields a strict positive sharing interval at signal accuracy 3/5, while alternative equilibria show that the result is selection-dependent rather than universal. The models are synthetic and finite; no human or organizational data are used.

</details>


### [321] [Induction and Inquiry via Probabilistic Reasoning over Language and Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01815)
*Wasu Top Piriyakulkij,Sam Acquaviva,Cassidy Langenfeld,Joshua Tenenbaum,Kevin Ellis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个计算模型，将符号知识编码为结合自然语言与源代码的"心理程序"，并利用LLM引导的贝叶斯学习算法顺序推断这些程序，以模拟人类从稀疏、流式、带噪声的经验中增长抽象知识的能力。该模型在多项行为研究中成功复现了人类归纳学习与主动探究的定量特征（如锚定效应、花园路径效应等），而纯LLM和经典贝叶斯模型均无法同时满足数据效率、不确定性量化和表征灵活性这三项要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类如何从稀疏、流式且含噪声的经验数据中增长和维持抽象知识，是认知科学长期以来的挑战。任何计算模型都必须满足三个必要条件：(1)数据高效且计算高效；(2)能捕捉不确定性的梯度以支持智性探究和信息收集；(3)足够灵活以心理表征人类能学习和思考的无限概念范围。现有方法（纯LLM、经典贝叶斯模型）均无法同时满足这三项要求。

Method: 该模型将符号知识编码为"心理程序"——结合自然语言与源代码的混合表征形式；采用LLM引导的贝叶斯学习算法对这些心理程序进行顺序推断，即以贝叶斯框架为核心，用底层LLM作为提议机制使推断既可行又可学习，实现逼近贝叶斯更新的假设修正过程。

Result: 在多项行为研究中，该模型成功复现了人类归纳学习与主动探究的定量特征（如锚定效应、花园路径效应等）。对照实验中，纯LLM和经典贝叶斯模型或无法完成底层任务，或无法复现人类行为，或仅在极高的计算代价下才能成功。

Conclusion: 研究提示，人类持续增长知识的一种方式是：在心理层面同时表征跨度介于语言式与程序式之间的多种假设，并通过底层神经机制（LLM）的辅助对这些假设进行接近贝叶斯更新的修订，从而使推断在计算上既可行又可学习。

Abstract: How humans grow and maintain abstract knowledge from the sparse, streaming noisy data of experience is a longstanding challenge in cognitive science. Any computational account must satisfy at least three desiderata: It must be (1) data-efficient and compute-efficient, (2) capture gradations of uncertainty to support intelligent inquiry and information gathering, and (3) be flexible enough to mentally represent the endless range of concepts people can learn and think about. Here we introduce a computational model that captures these three properties, by encoding symbolic knowledge as mental programs that combine natural language with source code, and sequentially inferring mental programs using LLM-guided Bayesian learning algorithms. Across a range of behavioral studies this model successfully reproduces quantitative signatures of human inductive learning and active inquiry, such as anchoring, garden-pathing, and other effects. In contrast, pure LLMs and classic Bayesian models either fail at the underlying task, or do not reproduce human behavior, or succeed only at exorbitant computational cost. These results suggest that one way humans continually grow their knowledge is by mentally representing many hypotheses spanning language-like and program-like representations, then revising those hypotheses to approximate Bayesian updates, while a bottom-up neural mechanism (an LLM) makes inference both tractable and learnable.

</details>


### [322] [Architecting Conversational Data Systems for Stateless LLM APIs: The Hydration Proxy Pattern](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01834)
*Joseph Axisa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Hydration Proxy模式，用于解决企业平台从传统架构转向对话式推理界面时，LLM API无状态特性所带来的架构缺口。该架构将会话持久化与推理引擎解耦，在保障平台对对话数据主权的同时实现安全的多阶段语义接地，并进一步提出Context Stabilization Mandate以平衡主权状态管理与KV缓存之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业平台正转向对话式推理界面，但LLM API的无状态特性为架构带来了缺口。虽然无状态性使AI提供方能够实现水平扩展，但这迫使客户端应用独自承担会话状态和语义记忆的全部管理负担，形成了架构上的不平衡。

Method: 提出Hydration Proxy Pattern架构模式，将会话持久化与推理引擎进行解耦，确保平台对对话数据的完全主权，同时支持安全的多阶段语义接地。并在此基础上提出Context Stabilization Mandate，用于解决主权状态管理与KV缓存之间的权衡问题。

Result: 识别并定义了一种名为Hydration Proxy Pattern的架构模式，该模式能够提供平台对会话数据的主权控制，同时支持安全的多阶段语义接地；此外提出Context Stabilization Mandate作为配套原则，用以调解主权状态管理与KV缓存效率之间的冲突。

Conclusion: 通过Hydration Proxy Pattern和Context Stabilization Mandate，企业平台可以在利用无状态LLM API的可扩展性优势的同时，恢复对会话数据的主权控制，解决无状态推理引擎与有状态对话管理需求之间的架构矛盾。

Abstract: As enterprise platforms transition to conversational reasoning interfaces, the stateless nature of LLM APIs creates an architectural gap. While statelessness enables horizontal scalability for AI providers, it forces client applications to manage the entire burden of conversational state and semantic memory. The work identifies the Hydration Proxy Pattern, an architecture that decouples session persistence from the reasoning engine. The framework ensures platform sovereignty over conversational data while enabling secure, multi-stage semantic grounding. We further propose the Context Stabilization Mandate to resolve the tradeoff between sovereign state management and KV caching.

</details>


### [323] [SSAKG 2.0: An Open-Source Package for Structural Associative Sequence Memory and Context-Based Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01849)
*Przemysław Stokłosa,Janusz A. Starzyk,Paweł Raif*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了SSAKG 2.0，一个用于构建和操作结构序贯关联知识图谱的开源软件包。该软件以图顶点表示对象、以图连接的模式表示有序序列，作为关联记忆支持从部分无序上下文中重建完整序列。2.0版本引入利用计算机内存位级操作的高效图搜索算法，采用Python和C混合实现，并在随机数字序列、NLTK语料句子衍生序列及mRNA序列上进行了评估验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决大规模稀疏图在序列存储和重建任务中的内存与计算开销问题，同时提供一个灵活的高层编程环境。SSAKG作为一种结构化的seq- assoc- knowledge graphs，需要改进算法以高效利用计算机内存位来搜索图连接，并在保持灵活性同时降低计算负担。

Method: SSAKG 2.0将对象表示为图的顶点，将有序序列表示为图连接的结构模式。版本2.0引入利用计算机内存单个位进行高效图连接搜索的新算法。软件包采用混合架构：整体以Python实现以提供灵活的高层编程环境，性能关键的图操作以C语言实现并通过Python接口暴露。评估使用三类数据：随机生成的数值序列、NLTK语料库句子衍生序列及mRNA序列。

Result: 实验表明该软件包能够有效存储序列并从部分上下文中重建完整序列，验证了其作为关联记忆的功能。评估结果还为基础研究图密度、序列长度和记忆大小对检索性能的影响提供了依据，证明了混合实现在降低大稀疏图内存与计算开销方面的有效性。

Conclusion: SSAKG 2.0以Apache 2.0开源许可发布，包含文档和可复现示例，通过GitHub和PyPI公开获取，为结构序贯关联知识图谱的构建与操作提供了高效、灵活且可复现的开源工具。

Abstract: This article presents SSAKG 2.0, an open-source software package for constructing and operating Structural Sequential Associative Knowledge Graphs (SSAKGs). An SSAKG represents objects as graph vertices and ordered sequences as structural patterns of graph connections. The resulting sparse graph is used as an associative memory in which complete sequences can be reconstructed from a partial, unordered context.
  Version 2.0 introduces new algorithms that exploit individual bits of computer memory to efficiently search graph connections. The package is implemented in Python, while performance-critical graph operations are implemented in C and exposed through a Python interface. This hybrid implementation provides a flexible high-level programming environment while reducing the memory and computational overhead associated with large sparse graphs.
  The algorithms were evaluated using randomly generated numerical sequences, sequences derived from sentences in the NLTK corpus, and mRNA sequences.
