In response we describe PROCTOR, a Teacher-Student loop in which a stateful orchestrator holds all tool access, stateless subagents diagnose failures and draft mutations they cannot apply, and a Teacher grades those mutations under five deterministic guardrails: hermetic sandboxes, capability-disjoint roles, acceptance checks that outrank the Teacher, frozen holdouts, and canary cases engineered so that a perfect score is itself evidence of cheating. We report the failures this prevented, and, because the Teacher is itself an LLM judge, the failures it did not.

</details>


### [356] [SafeEvolve: Harness-Policy Co-Evolution from Agent Experience for Safety Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02786)
*Qinghua Mao,Wanying Qu,Dadi Guo,Leitao Yuan,Qingyu Liu,Yu Li,Guanxu Chen,Yanwei Fu,Xi Lin,Xia Hu,Dongrui Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SafeEvolve 是一种基于经验驱动的自进化框架，通过"harness-策略"协同演化来实现 LLM Agent 的安全对齐，在提升安全性的同时保持甚至改善良性效用。在 Qwen3.5-4B 上，AgentDojo 基准的攻击成功率（ASR）降低为原来的 1/3，良性效用从 59.79% 提升至 61.86%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM Agent 的安全对齐机制通常只依赖单一范式——要么仅更新外部 harness（运行环境控制），要么仅进行策略优化，二者孤立使用无法将运行时控制与模型内在安全能力有效桥接。这导致 Agent 在面对多步骤执行轨迹时仍存在显著安全风险。

Method: SafeEvolve 构建了持续循环的 harness-策略协同演化框架：1）在 harness 侧，将轨迹级安全证据转化为有界、组件级别的安全提示和分层技能更新，生成可审计、可回滚的 harness 工件；2）在策略侧，采用两阶段 SFT-RL 范式——先通过 harness 使用监督微调（SFT）引导策略主动利用演化后的 harness 工件，再通过 harness 增强的强化学习，利用验证器分解的奖励信号在多步探索中塑造自主安全行为。

Result: 在多个 Agent 安全基准上的实验表明，SafeEvolve 相比现有基线取得了更强的安全-效用权衡。具体地，在 Qwen3.5-4B 模型上，AgentDojo 基准的攻击成功率（ASR）降低至原来的 1/3（即 3 倍降低），同时良性效用从 59.79% 提升至 61.86%。

Conclusion: SafeEvolve 通过将安全经验转化为演化的运行时 harness 和改善的策略行为，有效弥合了运行时控制与模型内在安全之间的鸿沟，为实现复杂多步环境下 LLM Agent 的安全对齐提供了一种经验驱动的协同演化方案。

Abstract: The performance of LLM-based agents is jointly shaped by the base model and the harness used when interacting with the environment. This exposes them to safety risks in both harmful final responses and multi-step execution trajectories. Existing safety alignment mechanisms often rely on either external harness updates or policy optimization, yet applying either paradigm in isolation fails to bridge runtime control with intrinsic safety. We propose SafeEvolve, an experience-driven self-evolving framework for agent safety alignment. SafeEvolve leverages safety experience from completed on-policy trajectories to drive a continual loop of harness-policy co-evolution. On the harness side, SafeEvolve converts trajectory-level safety evidence into bounded, component-level updates across safety prompt and hierarchical skills, yielding auditable and reversible harness artifacts. On the policy side, SafeEvolve follows a two-stage SFT-RL paradigm, where harness-use SFT bootstraps the policy to actively leverage evolved harness artifacts, and harness-augmented RL further shapes autonomous safety behaviors during multi-step exploration via verifier-decomposed rewards. Through harness-policy co-evolution, SafeEvolve converts safety experience into an evolved runtime harness and improved policy behavior. Experiments on agentic safety benchmarks show that SafeEvolve achieves a stronger safety-utility tradeoff than existing baselines. For Qwen3.5-4B, SafeEvolve achieves a $3\times$ ASR reduction on AgentDojo while improving benign utility from 59.79% to 61.86%.

</details>


### [357] [APEx: Distillation of Agent Procedural Experience for Adaptive Deep Research Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02253)
*Jie Ding,Rui Sun,Xinyuan Zhang,Zeyu Zhang,Xin Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: APEx是一个分层经验利用框架，通过实例级轨迹记忆和类别级程序性技能的耦合组织，采用Executor、Distiller、Planner三模块闭环架构，配合三阶段交替GRPO训练和技能引导的测试时强化学习，在7个基准上实现SOTA性能，超越GPT-5.4达14.7个点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度研究智能体在学习过往经验方面存在不足：要么检索冗长的任务特定轨迹，增加决策负担；要么蒸馏出的程序性技能与下游策略适配脱节，无法有效支持持续改进。

Method: APEx采用三层机制：(1)将交互历史组织为实例级轨迹记忆和类别级程序性技能的分层结构；(2)通过Executor、Distiller、Planner组成的闭环架构耦合两者；(3)使用三阶段交替GRPO训练范式实现奖励引导的技能蒸馏，替代固定提示生成；(4)测试时通过技能引导的强化学习进行Planner在线适配，利用技能对齐正则化防止策略漂移，实现无真实标签的自我改进。

Result: 在7个基准上验证，APEx达到SOTA性能：超越GPT-5.4达14.7个点，超越最强记忆增强基线3.0个点。

Conclusion: APEx证明了将分层经验组织与奖励引导的技能蒸馏及测试时策略适配相结合，能够有效实现深度研究智能体的持续自我改进，显著优于现有的记忆增强和技能蒸馏方法。

Abstract: Deep research agents augment large language models with external tools to answer complex, long-horizon questions through multi-turn reasoning. Learning from prior experience is crucial for continual improvement, yet existing methods either retrieve verbose task-specific traces that burden decision-making, or distill procedural skills that remain decoupled from downstream policy adaptation. We propose APEx, a hierarchical experience utilization framework that organizes interaction history into instance-level trajectory memories and category-level procedural skills, and couples them through a closed-loop architecture of Executor, Distiller, and Planner. The three modules are optimized via a three-stage alternating GRPO training paradigm, enabling reward-guided skill distillation rather than fixed-prompt generation. At test time, distilled skills serve as procedural priors for online Planner adaptation through skill-guided test-time reinforcement learning, allowing ground-truth-free self-improvement with skill-alignment regularization to prevent policy drift. Experiments on 7 benchmarks demonstrate that APEx achieves state-of-the-art performance, surpassing GPT-5.4 by 14.7 points and the strongest memory-augmented baseline by 3.0 points.

</details>


### [358] [Codebook Agent: Amortized Topology Design for LLM Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02264)
*Jinxi Yu,Yubei Li,Eric Hanchen Jiang,Zhi Zhang,Dong Liu,Wenxiao Zhao,Levina Li,Kai-Wei Chang,Ying Nian Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现现有LLM多智能体系统通信拓扑设计方法存在三个根本性缺陷：幸存拓扑在码本容量增大时仍收敛到约6种固定图、稀疏图反而增加token消耗、基于消息传递的评分器在智能体共享profile时具备邻接不变性而无法排序。据此提出Codebook Agent：向量量化自编码器将成功拓扑压缩为查询无关的16条目码本，reward加权MLP将查询嵌入映射到码本分布，MLP代理对扁平化邻接矩阵回归实测效用与归一化token成本以重排序。无需迭代搜索与消息传递，在六个基准上平均84.6分（超越最强基线83.0），2.4ms生成拓扑，节省21.9-33.2% LLM token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将LLM多智能体通信拓扑适配视为条件图生成问题，采用变分/自回归/扩散解码器搜索N×N邻接空间，并用图网络代理按效用加结构成本排序候选。作者通过三个实证观察指出该范式与问题本质错位：拓扑多样性被严重高估、稀疏性误导优化方向、评分代理在共享profile场景下完全失效，从而需要重新设计对齐目标的方法。

Method: Codebook Agent包含三个组件：(1) 向量量化自编码器（VQ-VAE）将成功拓扑压缩为查询无关的16条目码本，避免在N×N邻接空间进行无效搜索；(2) reward加权MLP将查询嵌入映射到码本上的概率分布，用加权解码得到候选；(3) 一个直接读取扁平化邻接矩阵的MLP代理，对实测效用和任务归一化token成本回归，在单次批量前向传播中对候选重排序。测试时无迭代搜索、无消息传递。

Result: 在六个基准上Codebook Agent取得最高平均准确率84.6%（最强基线为83.0%），拓扑生成仅需2.4ms，且相比基线节省21.9%-33.2%的LLM token消耗。三个实证发现也得以解释：幸存拓扑多样性极低（约6种）、边数与token消耗负相关（r≈-0.4）说明sparsifying图代价更高、共享profile下消息传递评分器邻接不变导致无法排序。

Conclusion: 作者认为通信拓扑设计不应在巨大的邻接空间中做条件图生成，而应先在码本空间中学习少量有代表性的成功拓扑模式，再以轻量MLP代理适配到具体查询。这种查询无关的码本压缩加查询相关重排序的设计，在准确率、速度和token效率上全面超越现有方法，为LLM多智能体系统拓扑优化提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Adapting the communication topology of an LLM multi-agent system to each query improves both accuracy and efficiency, yet current designers treat this as conditional graph generation: a variational, autoregressive, or diffusion decoder searches the $N \times N$ adjacency space, and a graph-network proxy trained on utility and a structural cost such as edge count ranks the sampled candidates. We argue that this formulation is misaligned with the problem. Empirically, topologies that survive a reward filter collapse to about six distinct graphs even when the codebook capacity grows from 8 to 64; edge count is negatively correlated with measured token consumption (Pearson $r \approx -0.4$), so sparsifying the graph makes inference more expensive; and a message-passing scorer over agent-profile nodes is adjacency-invariant whenever agents share a profile---the default configuration of published benchmarks---so it cannot rank candidates at all in that regime. These three facts motivate Codebook Agent: a vector-quantized autoencoder compresses successful topologies into a query-independent 16-entry codebook; a reward-weighted MLP maps the query embedding to a distribution over codes; and an MLP proxy that reads the flattened adjacency, regressed on measured utility and per-task normalized token cost, reranks the top decoded candidates in a single batched forward pass. With no iterative search and no message passing at test time, Codebook Agent is the most accurate method on all six benchmarks we compare (84.6 average against 83.0 for the strongest prior designer), emits a topology in 2.4 ms, and uses 21.9--33.2% fewer LLM tokens.

</details>


### [359] [CoMerge: Conflict-Driven Preference Optimization for Multi-Task Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02273)
*Mingjie Zheng,Zihao Chen,Wenqing Chen,Weile Yuan,Zhixuan Chu,Jianxing Yu,Zibin Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CoMerge提出了一种冲突驱动的偏好优化框架，将模型合并重新定义为偏好优化问题，利用朴素合并方法的缺陷作为硬负样本构建偏好对，无需外部标注即可优化轻量级张量级合并系数，在MergeBench上达到0.9968的平均归一化性能，超越所有评估基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型合并是构建多任务大语言模型的高效范式，但面临参数干扰问题。现有方法虽致力于保留专家模型能力并缓解干扰，却未能直接从朴素合并暴露出的退化行为中学习，缺乏对合并缺陷的反馈利用机制。

Method: CoMerge将模型合并重构为偏好优化问题，采用自监督的冲突驱动策略，利用朴素合并方法（如任务算术）的缺陷生成硬负样本以构建偏好对，无需外部标注。通过偏好优化对轻量级的张量级合并系数进行精细调整，缓解参数空间冲突并保留任务特定能力。

Result: 在MergeBench上达到0.9968的平均归一化性能，超越所有数据无关和数据驱动的模型合并基线。在Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct上，对指令跟随和安全等冲突敏感任务有显著提升，且仅优化1,445个标量系数即与全参数微调性能高度竞争。

Conclusion: CoMerge证明了将合并退化行为作为学习信号的有效性，以极小参数开销（仅1,445个标量系数）实现接近全参数微调的性能，为模型合并提供了新的解决范式。

Abstract: Model merging provides an efficient paradigm for constructing multi-task large language models (LLMs) without full model retraining, yet it remains challenged by parameter interference. While existing methods aim to preserve the capabilities of individual expert models and mitigate interference, they generally do not directly learn from the potentially degraded behaviors exposed by naive merging. In this paper, we propose a conflict-driven preference optimization framework for model merging (CoMerge), which reformulates model merging as a preference optimization problem. The approach utilizes a self-supervised, conflict-driven strategy that leverages the defects of naive merging methods (e.g., task arithmetic) as hard negative samples to construct preference pairs without external annotations. By applying preference optimization to refine lightweight, tensor-wise merging coefficients, CoMerge enables the model to mitigate parameter-space conflicts while preserving task-specific capabilities. Extensive experiments show that CoMerge achieves an average normalized performance of 0.9968 on MergeBench, outperforming all evaluated data-free and data-driven model-merging baselines. Furthermore, on Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct, CoMerge yields marked improvements on conflict-sensitive tasks such as instruction following and safety, while remaining highly competitive with full-parameter fine-tuning despite optimizing only 1,445 scalar coefficients.

</details>


### [360] [SCX Router: Streaming Zero-Shot Model Selection with a Decoder-KV Classifier and a Real-World Task Ontology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02292)
*Ihor Stepanov,Aleksandr Smechov,Mykhailo Shtopko,Dmytro Vodianytskyi,Oleksandr Lukashov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出\router{}，一个基于GLiClass的轻量级0.6B参数路由器，通过双向评分器而非自回归生成，为每个推理任务选择合适的LLM模型，在速度、成本和质量间实现最优权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型及其应用的快速普及带来了独特的优化机会：在任务级别选择正确的模型以优化速度、成本和质量的权衡。然而推理端点在质量、价格、延迟、上下文支持、工具使用、领域专长和推理行为等方面差异巨大，这种异质性使得手动启发式规则难以维护，且很难稳定实现理想的性价比权衡。

Method: \router{}采用GLiClass架构，将Qwen3解码器与浅层双向评分器结合。解码器-KV执行路径在会话中保留纯文本键值缓存，仅编码新对话轮次，并评估瞬态候选标签令牌而不将其加入持久缓存。同一检查点还预测任务类型、难度、推理模式和预期输出长度，支持自定义零样本标签。训练数据包括23个家族、115种任务类型、345个可路由子类型和30个领域的任务本体，生成150,000个验证器评分任务和15,000个开放式任务。训练将学习的请求预测与任务属性（资格、成本、缓存复用、安全性、主权）的政策明确分离。

Result: 在六个LiveBench子集上，路由器性能优于平均候选模型；在选定的1,000任务子集上，聚合top-1分数达到0.707，而最强固定模型为0.696，取得了与基准相关的增益。

Conclusion: \router{}验证了轻量级路由方法在多模型推理场景中的有效性，通过非自回归评分和KV缓存优化实现了高效、可扩展的模型选择方案。

Abstract: The rapid proliferation of large language models (LLMs) and the growing diversity of their applications presents a unique optimization opportunity: selecting the right model for the task, while optimizing for speed, cost, and quality at a per-task level. However, inference endpoints can vary widely in quality, price, latency, context support, tool use, domain expertise, and reasoning behavior. This heterogeneity makes manual heuristics difficult to maintain and unlikely to achieve consistently favorable speed--cost--quality trade-offs on their own. We introduce \router{}, a lightweight GLiClass-based router that assigns a suitability score to each inference-time model label without autoregressive generation. The released 0.6B-parameter checkpoint combines a Qwen3 decoder with a shallow bidirectional scorer. Its decoder-KV execution path preserves a text-only key--value cache across a session, encodes only new dialogue turns, and evaluates transient candidate-label tokens without adding them to the persistent cache. The same checkpoint also predicts task type, difficulty, reasoning mode, and expected output length, and supports custom zero-shot labels. For task generation, we construct a task ontology with 23 families, 115 task types, 345 routable subtypes, 1,173 synthetic examples, and an orthogonal axis of 30 domains. Using this structure, we generate 150,000 verifier-scored tasks and 15,000 open-ended tasks. We then train the Qwen3 decoder on these tasks, while explicitly separating learned request prediction from per-task policies for attributes such as eligibility, cost, cache reuse, safety, and sovereignty. Across six LiveBench subsets, the router outperforms the mean candidate; on the selected 1,000-task subset, it achieves an aggregate top-1 score of 0.707 versus 0.696 for the strongest fixed model, with benchmark-dependent gains.

</details>


### [361] [Improving Evaluation Realism with Inference-Time Compute and Deployment Scaffolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02302)
*Axel Ahlqvist,Richard Guan,Juan-Pablo Rivera,Adeline Kassler,Dmitrii Troitskii,Alexandra Souly,Kai Fronsdal,Robert Kirk,John Hughes*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出两种技术（critique refinement和DISH）使模拟对齐评估更难与现实部署区分，从而解决评估意识（evaluation awareness）这一核心障碍。两种技术可组合使用，比延长审计更有效地利用额外计算资源提升评估真实性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对齐评估的核心障碍在于"评估意识"：能力强的模型在被测试时能识别自己处于评估而非实际部署场景，这会削弱安全评估结论的可信度。使得模拟评估难以与现实部署区分是解决问题的关键。

Method: 提出两种技术：（1）critique refinement——在模拟器的每个行为上花费额外的推理时计算，生成多个候选行为，利用目标模型实例的反馈对其精炼以使其更逼真，选择最接近部署的候选；（2）DISH（Deployment-Imitating SWE-Agent Harness）——在编码场景中将目标模型包裹在代理化框架中，缩小模拟与现实部署环境之间的差距。在多个目标模型上进行测试。

Result: 两种技术在多个目标模型上测试有效，且具有组合性：同时应用两者比单独应用任一技术能带来更大的真实性提升。

Conclusion: 自动化方法可以提升对齐评估的真实性，且这些改进在利用额外计算资源方面比简单地延长审计过程更为有效。

Abstract: A core obstacle to alignment evaluation is evaluation awareness: capable models can tell when they are being tested rather than deployed, weakening the conclusions a safety evaluation can support. We present two techniques that make simulated alignment evaluations harder to distinguish from real deployments. Our first technique, critique refinement, spends additional inference-time compute on each simulator action: the simulator generates multiple candidate actions, refines them using feedback from an instance of the target model on how to make them more realistic, and continues the evaluation with the most deployment-like candidate. Our second technique, DISH (Deployment-Imitating SWE-Agent Harness), wraps the target in an agent harness, reducing the gap between simulated and real deployment environments in coding settings. We test the techniques on multiple target models and find that they compose: applying both yields larger realism gains than either alone. Our results show that automated approaches can improve the realism of alignment evaluations, and that these improvements use additional compute more effectively than making the audits longer.

</details>


### [362] [SALA: Semantic-Aware Logical Alignment for Complex Reasoning in In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02336)
*Zhao Ji,Wenqing Chen,Zhixuan Chu,Jianxing Yu,Jingping Liu,Shanhe Zhao,Zibin Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SALA是一个面向复杂推理中上下文学习示例选择的语义感知逻辑对齐框架，通过自动学习任务特定的推理操作并将其嵌入连续语义空间，利用动态时间规整实现推理逻辑的灵活软匹配，在四个推理基准和三个大语言模型上超越了现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于表面相似性的检索方法无法捕捉底层问题解决逻辑，而现有的基于逻辑的方法依赖预定义推理步骤，刚性规则和精确匹配标准难以处理灵活多样的推理过程。

Method: SALA框架包含三个核心环节：(1) 自动归纳任务特定的推理操作，无需依赖固定操作库；(2) 将这些操作嵌入连续语义空间；(3) 使用动态时间规整（DTW）对推理序列进行对齐，实现推理逻辑的软性、灵活匹配，同时保持高可解释性。

Result: 在四个推理基准和三个大语言模型上的实验表明，SALA优于现有的示例选择方法。进一步分析验证了操作归纳和逻辑语义对齐各自发挥的作用。

Conclusion: SALA通过语义感知的逻辑对齐机制，克服了传统检索和刚性逻辑匹配方法的局限，为复杂推理场景下的上下文学习示例选择提供了一种既灵活又可解释的有效方案。

Abstract: Effective in-context learning (ICL) for complex reasoning relies on selecting the right demonstrations. Traditional retrieval methods based on surface similarity fail to capture the underlying problem-solving logic. Recent logic-based methods address this by matching predefined reasoning steps, but the rigid rules and exact-match criteria is improper to handle flexible or diverse reasoning processes. To address the problem, we propose SALA, a Semantic-Aware Logical Alignment framework. Instead of relying on a fixed inventory, SALA automatically learns task-specific reasoning operations. It then embeds these operations into a continuous semantic space and uses dynamic time warping (DTW) to align the reasoning sequences. This approach allows for soft, flexible matching of reasoning logic while remaining highly interpretable. Experiments across four reasoning benchmarks and three LLMs demonstrate that SALA outperforms existing demonstration selection methods. Further analysis confirms the roles of the operation induction and the logical semantic alignment.

</details>


### [363] [Diagnosing with Insights: Structured Analysis of Agent Failures via Behavioral Abstractions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02371)
*Jiayi Bi,Yanjie Gao,Yuanmin Xie,Liqun Li,Tianyin Xu,Fan Yang,Mao Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AGENTSCOPE，一种用于LLM代理（agent）故障诊断的神经符号方法，通过将代理轨迹抽象为结构化表示并引入"神经不变量"概念来规定代理行为属性，结合LLM引导推理精确定位故障步骤及其类型，在两个数据集上显著优于现有最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM代理的普及，理解和诊断代理故障对于实现有效性和可信度至关重要。代理故障通常通过长而复杂的轨迹显现，人工在海量轨迹中寻找问题不可行；传统软件缺陷诊断技术难以应对LLM代理故障，而完全依赖LLM作为评判者也产生不可靠的诊断结果。

Method: AGENTSCOPE采用神经符号方法，核心原则是将代理行为（基于其轨迹）抽象为结构化表示。引入"神经不变量"概念来规定代理行为属性，然后利用LLM引导推理在结构化表示上对照神经不变量进行推理，从而精确定位轨迹中的故障步骤及其类型。

Result: 在公开的代理故障数据集Who&When以及作者自建的更全面的AgentErrata数据集上验证了有效性，AGENTSCOPE在故障定位和归属准确率上显著优于当前最先进方法。

Conclusion: 将结构化抽象与LLM引导推理相结合，能够实现对代理故障的有效、可靠且可解释的诊断。

Abstract: With the proliferation of LLM agents, the ability to understand and diagnose failures in agents is essential to achieving superior effectiveness and trustworthiness. As agent failures often manifest via long and complex trajectories, manually finding the needles in the haystack is untenable. However, traditional diagnosis techniques for software bugs can hardly address LLM agent failures, while completely relying on LLMs as the judge yields unreliable diagnosis results. To overcome these challenges, this paper presents AGENTSCOPE, a new neuro-symbolic approach for agent failure mode diagnosis. The key principle of AGENTSCOPE is to abstract agent behavior, based on its trajectories, into structured representations. Furthermore, AGENTSCOPE introduces the concept of neural invariants to specify agent behavior properties. AGENTSCOPE leverages LLM-guided reasoning atop the structured representation against neural invariants to pinpoint both the failure step and its type in the trajectory. We show the effectiveness of AGENTSCOPE on publicly available agent failure datasets (Who&When) and a more comprehensive dataset created by us (AgentErrata), where AGENTSCOPE significantly outperforms the current state of the art in fault localization and attribution accuracy. Our work shows that integrating structured abstractions with LLM-guided reasoning enables effective, reliable, and interpretable diagnosis for agent failures.

</details>


### [364] [Contrastive Explanations in Quantitative Bipolar Argumentation Frameworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02399)
*Xiang Yin,Nico Potyka,Antonio Rago,Francesca Toni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出用于量化双极论证框架（QBAFs）的对比性解释方法，通过对比归因函数（CAFs）解释两个主题论证之间的推理结果差异，并基于删除、梯度和Shapley值构建了多种CAF，研究了它们的性质，并在医疗保健和偏差识别场景中进行了应用演示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的QBAFs解释大多只针对单个主题论证的推理结果进行解释，缺乏对两个论证之间推理差异的解释能力。对比性解释能够提供更全面的可解释性，尤其在分类任务补充和偏差识别等方面有重要价值。

Method: 本文首先定义了一种通用的对比归因函数（CAF）形式，并建立了一套它们应满足的一般性质。随后引入了基于删除（removal）、梯度（gradients）和Shapley值（Shapley-values）的三种CAF实现，并逐一研究它们的性质。

Result: 提出了三类CAF（基于删除、梯度和Shapley值的对比归因函数），并系统研究了它们满足的性质。在医疗保健和偏差识别两个实际场景中展示了对比性解释的实用性。

Conclusion: 对比性解释为QBAFs提供了一种新的解释范式，能够解释两个主题论证之间的推理差异，弥补了现有单一论证解释的不足，并展示了在敏感领域（如医疗和偏差检测）中的应用潜力。

Abstract: Argumentation frameworks are useful tools for representing and reasoning with information in a variety of settings, e.g. in supplementing AI models as they perform classification tasks, with a notable benefit of providing additional explainability. In this paper, we introduce contrastive explanations for Quantitative Bipolar Argumentation Frameworks (QBAFs), one such formalism. Unlike most existing explanations for QBAFs, which explain the reasoning outcome of a single argument of interest (i.e. a topic argument), contrastive explanations explain the difference between two topic arguments. We introduce a general form of contrastive attribution functions (CAFs) and establish a set of general properties they should satisfy. We introduce CAFs based on removal, gradients and Shapley-values, and study their properties. Finally, to illustrate contrastive explanations, we demonstrate their usefulness in healthcare and bias identification settings.

</details>


### [365] [UTP-Bench: Uncertainty-aware Travel Planning Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02421)
*Etcharla Revanth Rao,Priyanshu Karmakar,Shubhojit Mallick,Manish Gupta,Shreya Ghosh,Abhik Jana*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出UTP-Bench，一个面向不确定性感知旅行规划的大规模基准，覆盖504个印度城市，融合经验延误分布和人群密度数据，并引入三项新评价指标（BAS、CATS、TDAS），揭示现有LLM在应对交通延误和人群波动方面的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有旅行规划基准（如TravelPlanner和TripCraft）假设确定环境，仅评估静态约束满足，忽视了真实世界中交通延误、人群波动等不确定性因素对行程可行性的影响，无法评估生成的计划是否具有鲁棒性。

Method: 构建包含504个印度城市真实旅行数据的基准UTP-Bench，数据集覆盖景点、餐厅、住宿和多模态交通网络；引入真实收集的经验延误分布和人群密度模式；提出三项评估指标（Buffer Adequacy Score, Crowd-Aware Timing Score, Transport Delay Absorption Score）分别量化行程在时间缓冲、人群变化和交通延误方面的鲁棒性。

Result: 对GPT-5、Qwen3、Mistral和Phi-4等SOTA LLM的实验表明，模型生成行程与人工规划的行程之间存在显著差距，尤其在时间缓冲设置、延误感知的交通调度和人群敏感规划方面表现不佳。

Conclusion: 现有LLM在不确定性条件下的旅行规划鲁棒性方面存在明显不足，UTP-Bench可为未来研究提供标准化的评估框架，推动开发更健壮的不确定性感知旅行规划方法。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have recently demonstrated strong capabilities in automated travel itinerary generation. However, real- world travel planning is inherently uncertain: transportation delays, crowd fluctuations, and unexpected stochastic delays frequently inval- idate otherwise feasible schedules. Existing benchmarks like TravelPlanner and TripCraft assume deterministic environments, evaluating only static constraint satisfaction and ignoring whether generated plans remain robust when such uncertainties arise. To address this limitation, we introduce UTP-Bench1 , a large-scale benchmark for uncertainty-aware travel planning. The dataset integrates real-world travel data spanning 504 cities of India, including attractions, restau- rants, accommodations, and multi-modal trans- portation networks. To model realistic disrup- tions, UTP-Bench incorporates empirical delay distributions and crowd-density patterns col- lected from major cities, enabling evaluation of travel plans under stochastic conditions. We further propose three evaluation metrics, namely Buffer Adequacy Score (BAS), Crowd- Aware Timing Score (CATS), and Transport Delay Absorption Score (TDAS), which quan- tify the ability of generated itineraries to main- tain robustness against transit delays and crowd variability. Experiments with state-of-the-art LLMs like GPT-5, Qwen3, Mistral and Phi-4 re- veal substantial gaps between model-generated and human-authored plans, particularly in tem- poral buffering, delay-aware transportation scheduling, and crowd-sensitive planning.

</details>


### [366] [CivBench: A Long-Horizon Benchmark for Tool-Mediated Agents in Civilization VI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02459)
*Austin Tudor David Andrews,Liam Wilkinson,Jamie Heagerty,Harry Coppock,Jakob Nicolaus Foerster,Rui Ponte Costa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CivBench，一个通过Model Context Protocol (MCP)评估语言模型智能体在长时程、工具中介环境中表现的开放基准。单个回合跨越300+轮次，产生数千次工具调用。研究发现智能体存在两个一致性问题：策略性状态监控不足（PMR低）以及规划反思中承诺的近期执行率不高（RAG@10在48.2%-65.8%之间），并将这些模式解释为指令下的偏离而非能力缺失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的语言模型智能体评估多集中在短时程、单一任务上，缺乏对长时程（300+轮次）、工具中介环境中智能体持续规划、状态监控和部分可观测条件下执行能力的系统性评测。作者希望通过CivBench提供一个能够量化这些行为的基准，并设计接口级指标以捕捉智能体在长时间协作中的战略监控和承诺兑现行为。

Method: 构建基于文明6游戏环境的CivBench基准，通过MCP暴露76个工具，并利用叙述层将视觉游戏状态转换为结构化文本。引入两个接口级指标：Proactive Monitoring Rate (PMR)衡量智能体是否主动查询潜在的战略状态；RAG@10衡量结构化的规划反思中声明的承诺是否在随后10轮内被执行。在四个模型家族、23次可运行实验中采用共享的剧本协议（playbook protocol）进行评测分析。

Result: 尽管剧本明确指导每20轮查询胜利进度，智能体实际每30-75轮才查询一次；在20次可检测的失败中有7次未在游戏结束前20轮警告窗口内完成查询。各模型的RAG@10介于48.2%-65.8%，表明智能体经常未能执行自己规划反思中陈述的近期承诺。两个模式在工具可用且有明确指导的情况下仍然出现。该实验是试点而非模型排名，因为此规模下聚合结果无法可靠区分模型。

Conclusion: 智能体在长时程工具环境中即使具备工具访问权和明确指导，仍表现出系统性偏离——策略性监控不足和承诺执行率偏低。这些问题应被理解为指令遵循（instruction following）中的偏差而非能力缺失。作者已开源环境、场景、日志、指标及分析流程。

Abstract: We present CivBench, an open-source benchmark for evaluating language model agents in long-horizon, tool-mediated environments through the Model Context Protocol (MCP). A single episode spans 300+ turns and produces thousands of tool calls over a large action space, requiring sustained planning, state monitoring, and execution under partial observability. The environment exposes 76 MCP tools and a narration layer that converts visual game state into structured text.
  We use CivBench to characterise agent behaviour across four model families in 23 admissible runs. The sample is a pilot, not a model ranking: aggregate outcomes do not reliably discriminate models at this scale. Instead, we introduce two interface-level metrics that the environment makes measurable: Proactive Monitoring Rate (PMR), capturing whether agents actively query latent strategic state, and RAG@10, capturing whether commitments stated in structured planning reflections are executed within ten subsequent turns.
  Across runs we observe two consistent patterns under a shared playbook protocol. Agents under-monitor strategically relevant state that is available but requires explicit querying: despite playbook guidance to query victory progress every 20 turns, agents do so only every 30 to 75 turns, and in 7 of 20 detectable defeats they failed to query within the 20 turn warning window before game end. Agents also frequently fail to execute near-term commitments stated in their own planning reflections (RAG@10 between 48.2% and 65.8% across models). Both patterns arise despite tool access and explicit guidance, and we interpret them as deviations under instruction rather than absences of capability.
  We release the environment, scenarios, logs, metrics, and analysis pipeline at https://github.com/lmwilki/civ6-mcp

</details>


### [367] [Collective creativity in hybrid societies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02620)
*Mason Youngblood,Katie Mudd,Manuel Anglada-Tort,Cameron Jones,Elena Miu,Diana Omigie,Margaret Schedel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文论证生成式AI对创造力的讨论应区分"新颖性"（单个作品的属性）与"多样性"（群体属性），主张应把创造力理解为人与算法组成的混合集体的属性，而非个体属性。关键结论是：AI辅助构思虽能提升个体输出的新颖性，但会收窄群体多样性；这一结果并非必然，而取决于人机群体的组成结构（哪些智能体在场、比例如何、如何连接）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前学界对生成式AI是丰富还是贫化文化存在分歧，作者认为分歧根源在于混淆了新颖性与多样性这两种截然不同的创造力成分，需要更精确的概念框架和更系统的群体视角来重新审视问题。

Method: 通过概念分析将创造力拆分为新颖性与多样性两个维度，提出"混合集体"（人与算法共同组成的种群）的分析框架，并引用AI辅助构思的新颖性提升与多样性收窄的证据，主张混合群体可通过互补性搜索在多样性和个体增益上都优于单一群体。

Result: 指出AI辅助构思可靠提升个体新颖性而收窄总体多样性，但这一趋同并非机器参与的必然结果；人类与模型搜索方式互补，混合群体可以同时超越纯人类或纯AI群体，机器发现的解决方案也能进入人类文化并持续存在。决定结果的是群体组成（智能体种类、比例、连接方式）。

Conclusion: 研究问题的重心应从"AI是否帮助或损害创造力"转向"哪种人机混合组成能让个体增益积累而不侵蚀集体多样性"，这为未来研究与设计提供了新方向。

Abstract: Generative AI is changing how cultural artifacts are created and circulated, and with it our understanding of creativity itself. Researchers disagree about whether these tools enrich or impoverish culture, and we argue that much of that disagreement comes from conflating two distinct components of creativity: novelty, a property of single artifacts, and diversity, a property of populations. We argue further that creativity in the context of generative AI is best understood as a property of hybrid collectives, or populations of interacting people and algorithms, rather than of individuals. AI-assisted ideation reliably raises the novelty of individual output while narrowing diversity in the aggregate, but this is not an inevitable consequence of putting machines in the loop. Because humans and models search in complementary ways, mixed groups can outperform and out-diversify groups of either kind alone, and machine-discovered solutions can enter human culture and persist there. What decides the outcome is composition: which agents are present, in what proportion, and how they are connected. The question is no longer whether AI helps or harms creativity, but which mixtures let individual gains accumulate without eroding collective diversity.

</details>


### [368] [Loom: Weaving Diagnostic Strands into Free-Text Consensus via Embedding-Space Reweighting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02649)
*Ron Begleiter,Katya Egert Berg,Gilad Saban,Gil Shabat*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Loom是一个生成式共识框架，用于真实世界根因分析（RCA），通过将模块化启发式规则产生的开放式假设投影到连续嵌入空间，并用迭代质心重加权算法解决冲突信号，最终以单次轻量级LLM调用生成综合结论。在OpenRCA基准上，Loom在准确率-效率帕累托前沿上占优，每个事件仅需一次LLM调用（约26倍加速，使用8B参数合成器时约33倍）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在真实工业场景中部署NLP系统时，将嘈杂且相互冲突的文本假设聚合为可靠共识是根本挑战。单体LLM代理虽表达力强，但受限于上下文长度、幻觉累积和推理延迟问题；传统弱监督方法虽有统计严谨性，但数学上仅适用于离散类别。亟需一种同时兼顾准确性与效率的桥梁性方案。

Method: Loom框架包含三个核心环节：(1) 模块化启发式规则动态生成带有事件特定实体、时间和度量的诊断模板假设；(2) 将开放形式的假设投影到连续嵌入空间进行表征；(3) 采用迭代质心重加权算法聚合冲突信号并求解共识权重；(4) 共识权重用于锚定一次轻量级LLM合成步骤从而生成最终结论，每个事件仅需单次LLM调用。

Result: 在OpenRCA基准上，Loom处于准确率-效率帕累托前沿：在Bank和Market-2数据集上与最先进的自主代理持平，在Market-1和Telecom上略有落后，但全部四个数据集上每起事件仅需一次LLM调用（约26倍加速，使用8B参数合成器时约33倍加速）。

Conclusion: 确定性共识机制增强了领域专家（SMEs）对系统的信任。论文分享了部署经验与教训：代理深度与推理延迟之间存在权衡；冗余检测出现负面结果；确定性共识在建立专家信任方面具有重要价值。

Abstract: Aggregating noisy, conflicting textual hypotheses into a reliable consensus is a fundamental challenge when deploying NLP systems in real-world industrial settings. While monolithic Large Language Model (LLM) agents offer unbounded expressivity for tasks like Root Cause Analysis (RCA), they suffer from context limits, compounding hallucinations, and prohibitive inference latency. Traditional weak supervision offers statistical rigor but is mathematically restricted to discrete classes. We present Loom, a generative consensus framework deployed for real-world RCA that bridges these paradigms. Loom aggregates open-form hypotheses emitted by modular heuristics (diagnostic templates dynamically populated with episode-specific entities, times, and metrics) by projecting them into a continuous embedding space, and resolves conflicting signals with an iterative centroid-based reweighting algorithm. The resulting consensus weights ground a single lightweight LLM synthesis step. Evaluated on the OpenRCA benchmark, Loom occupies the accuracy--efficiency Pareto frontier: it matches a state-of-the-art autonomous agent on Bank and Market-2 and trails on Market-1 and Telecom, while using a single LLM call per incident on all four datasets ($\sim$26$\times$ faster; $\sim$33$\times$ with an 8B-parameter synthesizer). We discuss our deployment experience, highlighting lessons learned regarding the trade-offs between agentic depth and inference latency, negative results in redundancy detection, and how deterministic consensus fosters trust among Subject Matter Experts~(SMEs).

</details>


### [369] [Door-in-the-Face Requests and Refusal Behaviour in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02707)
*Til Jordan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文测试了"门面效应"（door-in-the-face technique）是否在语言模型上有效。结果因模型而异：在Anthropic的旗舰模型上有效（Opus 5在拒绝大请求后对小请求的遵从率从29.3%升至65.8%），但在OpenAI和Google的旗舰模型及Haiku 4.5上适得其反，使遵从率下降15.5至23.0个百分点。对照实验表明让步本身在所有九个模型上都起作用，但对"刚拒绝过某事"的反应因模型家族而异。该技术不能从公共基准的拒答中迁移，效果取决于请求内容：将265个拒答请求改写为解释性请求后，263个不再被拒绝。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类心理学中"门面效应"（先提大请求被拒后再提小请求更容易被接受）已被广泛验证，但该技术能否移植到语言模型上尚不明确。本研究旨在系统测试这一人类影响技巧对多个商业语言模型的有效性，以理解模型在拒绝与让步情景下的行为差异。

Method: 作者在来自三家供应商的九个生产模型上进行测试：先让每个模型拒绝一个大请求，再向其提出同一请求的缩小版本，并将其遵从率与直接提出小请求的基线进行比较。同时设置对照组（大请求与小请求话题不相关）以定位效应来源，并检验从公共基准数据集提取的拒答样本是否能触发同类效应。

Result: 结果具有明显的家族分化：Anthropic旗舰模型上门面效应有效，Opus 5对小请求的遵从率在拒绝大请求后为65.8%，直接请求时为29.3%；而OpenAI和Google的旗舰模型以及Haiku 4.5上效应反向，遵从率下降15.5至23.0个百分点。对照实验显示让步本身（话题相关）在所有模型上均起作用，但对"刚刚拒绝"的后续反应因模型家族而异。从公共基准中的拒答请求试时，该技术无法复现。

Conclusion: 人类影响技巧向语言模型的迁移是逐模型家族发生的（one model family at a time），不能一概而论。决定"退让"策略是否奏效的核心因素是请求内容本身的性质：将请求从"索取可用指令"改写为"请求同话题解释"能消除263/265的拒绝。这说明语言模型对影响技巧的响应既依赖架构/训练差异，也高度依赖请求语义与意图解读方式。

Abstract: Does the door-in-the-face technique work on language models? In humans, a large request that is refused makes a smaller follow-up request more likely to be granted. We test this on nine production models from three providers: each model refuses a large request, then receives a smaller version of the same request, and we compare its compliance with asking directly. The answer depends on the model. On Anthropic's frontier models the technique works: Opus 5 answers the smaller request 65.8% of the time after refusing the larger one, against 29.3% when asked directly. On the frontier models of OpenAI and Google, and on Haiku 4.5, it backfires, lowering compliance by 15.5 to 23.0 points. A control locates the effect: a refused large request on an unrelated topic does less than the related one on all nine models, so the concession itself matters everywhere, while the reaction to having just refused something differs by model family. The technique does not transfer to refusals drawn from public benchmarks. What decides whether a retreat can work is what the request asks for: rewriting 265 refused requests for usable instructions into requests for explanations of the same topic removed the refusal in 263 cases. Human influence techniques port to language models one model family at a time.

</details>


### [370] [Repo-To-Skill: Distilling GitHub Repositories Into AI4AI Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02749)
*Jianlyu Chen,Yuyang Hu,Hongjin Qian,Jiawei Liu,Wenqing Wei,Xiaolong Chen,Defu Lian,Zhicheng Dou,Chaozhuo Li,Qiwei Ye,Zheng Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DisCo是一个技能驱动的自主研究代理，通过将ML领域中的"操作知识"（operational knowledge）蒸馏为可复用的、经过验证的技能，使研究代理在固定模型骨架和研究框架下显著提升性能。其AIREX-Skill Library包含从1000个广泛使用的ML仓库蒸馏出的5000+验证技能，覆盖20个领域和178个能力族，在MLE-bench、PaperBench、FrontierCS和PassNet等基准上分别提升134.3%、34.4%、9.2%和14.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主ML研究代理虽然结合了模型骨架、规划/执行框架、记忆和验证机制，但领域特定的"操作知识"（如如何让方法真正跑通的know-how）仍游离于代理之外。这些知识存在于仓库和论文中，但形式面向人类读者且体量过大，无法在任务执行中加载。作者希望将这类知识蒸馏为紧凑、可验证的技能，使其跨任务复用而非每次重新发现。

Method: DisCo采用两种互补的蒸馏形式：(1) 任务无关的蒸馏——将领域广泛使用的开源仓库压缩为可复用技能，产生包含5000+验证技能的AREX-Skill Library，覆盖20个领域和178个能力族；(2) 任务导向的蒸馏——针对具体任务按需生成所需技能。在评估中保持GPT-5.5骨干模型、研究框架和执行预算固定，仅改变技能配备来对照。

Result: 在固定GPT-5.5骨干、研究框架和执行预算下，配备技能的代理相比无技能版本在四个基准上分别提升：MLE-bench提升134.3%，PaperBench提升34.4%，FrontierCS提升9.2%，PassNet提升14.0%。性能增益完全来自添加的蒸馏操作上下文。

Conclusion: 将领域操作知识离线蒸馏为紧凑验证技能是一种有效的补充架构思路——它避免了任务运行期间的环境拥挤加载，实现了知识的跨任务重用。技能库的规模效应和多层次（任务无关+任务导向）蒸馏策略为构建更强大的自主ML研究代理提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Autonomous agents are beginning to carry out machine-learning (ML) research end to end. These agents combine a model backbone with a harness for planning, execution, memory, and verification, but this architecture still leaves domain-specific know-how outside the agent. We call this missing layer operational knowledge, the know-how that separates knowing a method from making it work. That knowledge is not absent from the field. It appears in repositories and papers, but in forms written for human readers and too large to load during a task. Once distilled into compact, verified skills, this knowledge can be reused across tasks rather than rediscovered during each run.
  We present DisCo, a skill-powered research agent that creates skills and uses them during research. Its distillation runs in two complementary forms: task-agnostic, condensing the field's widely used repositories into reusable skills, and task-oriented, producing the skills a concrete task calls for. The former, applied across the open ecosystem, yields the AREX-Skill Library, with 5,000+ verified skills distilled from 1,000 widely used ML repositories and organized into 20 areas and 178 capability families. With the GPT-5.5 backbone, research harness, and downstream execution budget held fixed, the skill-equipped research agent scores 134.3% higher on MLE-bench, 34.4% higher on PaperBench, 9.2% higher on FrontierCS, and 14.0% higher on PassNet than the same agent without skills. These gains come from adding distilled operating context under that fixed setup.

</details>


### [371] [Bilevel Coordinated Reflection: A Game-Theoretic Approach to Multi-Agent LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02750)
*Yihang Chen,Yuxiang Chen,Yuxuan Huang,Meng Fang,Weilin Luo,Jun Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文从博弈论和信息论角度统一分析了多智能体LLM系统中的协调、记忆改进与外部验证问题，提出随机反射记忆上升（SRMA）算法，并在SWE-bench上取得72.2%的解决率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM系统（orchestrator-worker结构）虽有强经验结果，但缺乏统一的数学框架来解释协调机制、记忆改进机制，以及外部验证在其中的作用。作者希望回答：协调何时有效？反射（reflection）为何能持续改进？什么条件下门控（gating）能提高性能？

Method: 1) 将orchestrator-worker交互建模为双层协调博弈，证明在有界耦台下工作者的局部更新博弈是近似势博弈，其均衡松弛由分解质量控制。2) 将反射建模为语义记忆状态上的随机运动，导出自由形式反射的有限时间上界、最坏情形紧致性及持久危害条件下的正下界。3) 证明信息论不可行性结果：仅观察生成文集的gate无法在文本不可区分环境中一致改进，而环境接地gate可以。4) 提出SRMA算法，仅在接地评估风险严格下降时接受候选记忆，给出几何/多项式收敛率及最优匹配构造。5) 提供随机评估的置信门控和分段平稳环境的重锚定保证。

Result: 在500个SWE-bench实例上，基于Kimi的完整系统解决72.2%的问题，而公开mini-SWE-agent参考为70.8%。同时实验验证了预测的协调律与漂移律。

Conclusion: 作者建立了一个统一的理论框架解释多智能体LLM系统中的协调、记忆与验证问题，证明环境接地验证相较于仅文本门控的本质优势，并给出收敛性与紧致性保证的SRMA算法。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems commonly use an orchestrator to decompose a task for a team of workers and then improve through textual reflection. Despite strong empirical results, these systems lack a unified account of coordination, memory improvement, and the role of external verification. We model orchestrator-worker interaction as a bilevel coordination game: under bounded coupling, the workers' local-update game is an approximate potential game whose equilibrium slack is controlled by decomposition quality. We then analyse reflection as stochastic movement over semantic memory states. For free-form reflection, we derive a finite-time upper bound, prove worst-case tightness, and give a positive lower bound under a falsifiable persistent-harm condition. We further prove an information-theoretic impossibility result: no gate that observes only the generated transcript can improve uniformly over text-indistinguishable environments, whereas an environment-grounded gate can. Motivated by this separation, we introduce Stochastic Reflective Memory Ascent (SRMA), which accepts a candidate memory only after a grounded evaluation risk strictly decreases. Under calibration and non-degenerate corrective mass, SRMA converges exactly, geometrically or polynomially; matching constructions show that both rate regimes are order-tight. We also provide confidence gating for stochastic evaluation and re-anchoring guarantees for piecewise-stationary environments. Experiments instantiate these objects with environment-grounded metrics and test the predicted coordination and drift laws. On 500 SWE-bench instances, the complete Kimi-based system resolves 72.2% versus a 70.8% public mini-SWE-agent reference. Code: https://github.com/YihangChen9/Bilevel-Coordinated-Reflection

</details>


### [372] [Measurement-Driven Sub-Network Selection for On-Premise Retrieval-Augmented Factory Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02760)
*Vasileios Rizeakos,Georgios Paisios,Alexandros Machairas,Michael Birbas,Athanasios Bachoumis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对工厂车间边缘设备的检索增强问答助手部署方案：通过结构压缩和检索增强适配后，模型大小不再是评估答案质量的可靠指标，因此将部署视为一个适配后的子网络选择问题，依据评判质量和设备吞吐量在可配置的能力下限和内存预算下选择最佳子网络，并借助权重共享超级网络和夹层风格原地址蒸馏降低选择成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工厂车间需要本地化的会话式机器文档助手，但具备该能力的模型往往难以适配车间硬件（资源受限的边缘设备）。现有方案通常仅优化模型大小、速度或质量中的单一维度，会牺牲其他性能；同时缺乏系统性的部署选择框架来处理异质边缘设备。

Method: 1) 对模型进行结构压缩并获得多个子网络；2) 引入检索接地适配（retrieval-grounded adaptation）以恢复质量；3) 证明压缩及适配后模型大小与答案质量不再单调相关；4) 将部署建模为后适配的子网络选择问题，在可配置的通用能力下限和内存预算约束下，以评判的答案质量和实测设备吞吐量为目标进行优化；5) 使用权重共享超级网络配合夹层风格（sandwich-style）原地址蒸馏（in-place distillation）使选择过程经济高效。

Result: 在制造手册案例研究中：压缩后模型的评判质量损失为未剪枝模型的13.7%，检索接地蒸馏将其恢复至仅损失4.6%（恢复约三分之二的损失）；同一助手可在三个异构边缘层级上运行，待机功耗为1.3至5瓦。

Conclusion: 模型大小在经过结构压缩与检索适配后不再可靠预测答案质量，这表明应将边缘端部署视为适配后的选择问题而非仅压缩问题。以通用能力和检索质量为双目标、在实际吞吐约束下选择子网络的框架能在能力、质量和吞吐之间取得更优平衡，且权重共享超级网络使该选择代价可接受。

Abstract: On-premise assistants can give factory workers conversational access to machine documentation, but models capable of the task rarely fit shop-floor hardware. We show that after structural compression and retrieval-grounded adaptation, model size is no longer a reliable predictor of adapted answer quality: general capability falls almost linearly with parameter count, while judged retrieval-augmented answer quality does not. We therefore treat deployment as a post-adaptation selection problem, committing one sub-network per device on judged answer quality and measured on-device throughput under a configurable general-capability floor and memory budget; rules that optimize size, speed, or quality alone each give up capability or throughput. A weight-shared supernetwork trained with sandwich-style in-place distillation keeps this selection inexpensive. In a manufacturing-manual case study, extraction costs 13.7 percent of the unpruned model's judged quality and retrieval-grounded distillation returns it to within 4.6 percent, recovering two thirds of the loss, and the same assistant runs across three heterogeneous edge tiers at 1.3 to 5 watts standby.

</details>


### [373] [Large Language Models (LLMs) for Telecom Root Cause Analysis (RCA): A Structured Reasoning Framework for Evidence-Grounded Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02805)
*Hao Zhou,Mandar Kulkarni,Hao Chen,Yan Xin,Charlie,Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综述了电信网络根因分析（RCA）从规则/机器学习到LLM驱动技术的演进，并提出一个结构化推理框架，通过规范上下文组织、决策路径推理和证据支撑解释三个步骤，在5G RCA数据集上显著提升诊断准确率和决策一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 5G/6G网络中的跨层复杂依赖关系使性能劣化的根因诊断极为困难；虽然LLM具备推理和知识整合能力，但直接应用朴素LLM进行电信RCA会产生幻觉、推理不稳定以及与结构化网络证据对齐差等问题，亟需专门的框架来弥合这一鸿沟。

Method: 首先系统综述电信RCA从规则/ML到LLM方法的演进并梳理现有范式（结构化推理、检索增强知识接地、智能体编排、可验证推理）；在此基础上提出一个三阶段结构化推理框架：1) 将异构网络遥测数据组织为规范化上下文；2) 在诊断过程中施加决策路径推理以约束推理轨迹；3) 生成基于证据的、可解释的故障识别结论。

Result: 在两个5G RCA基准数据集（TeleLogs和TelecomTS）上的实验表明，所提框架在诊断准确率和决策一致性方面均优于基线方法，并在跨数据集场景中验证了结构化推理设计对实用LLM基RCA系统的重要性。

Conclusion: 结构化推理设计对于构建下一代电信网络中可靠、实用的LLM基RCA系统至关重要，该框架通过将诊断推理与电信域证据及知识对齐，有效缓解了朴素LLM的幻觉和不稳定推理问题。

Abstract: Root cause analysis (RCA) is a critical task in telecom network operations, but diagnosing performance degradations in modern 5G and emerging 6G networks remains challenging due to complex cross-layer dependencies. While large language models (LLMs) offer promising capabilities for reasoning and knowledge integration, directly applying vanilla LLMs to telecom RCA often leads to hallucination, unstable reasoning, and poor alignment with structured network evidence. This work first reviews the evolution of telecom RCA from rule-based and machine learning (ML) approaches to emerging LLM-enabled techniques, and provides an overview of recent paradigms, including structured reasoning, retrieval-augmented knowledge grounding, agentic orchestration, and verifiable reasoning. Building upon these insights, we propose a structured reasoning framework for LLM-enabled telecom RCA that aligns diagnostic reasoning with telecom-specific evidence and domain knowledge. The proposed approach first organizes heterogeneous network telemetry into canonical contexts, and then enforces decision-path reasoning during diagnosis, and finally generates evidence-grounded explanations for reliable fault identification. Experimental results on two 5G RCA datasets, TeleLogs and TelecomTS, demonstrate that the proposed framework consistently improves diagnostic accuracy and decision consistency compared with baseline techniques. These cross-dataset results highlight the importance of structured reasoning design for practical LLM-based RCA systems in next-generation telecom networks.

</details>


### [374] [AI Contextual Measurement for Recovering Individual and Group-Level Effects: Validation Against Survey Measures and an Occupational Application](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02821)
*Wenxin Jiang,Xuyang Wang,Yuxiao Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AICOME框架，用于评估AI生成的受访者级测量能否在情境模型（contextual models）中恢复个体和群体层面的效应。通过在2022年中国家庭追踪调查（CFPS）数据上，以职业为群体结构、多个工作相关变量为基准进行验证，结果显示AI情境测量在丰富受访者与职业特征可用时能恢复大部分情境模型信息，尤其在工作时长变量上表现最佳，但也存在明确的边界条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究者越来越多地使用人工智能从常规调查数据中构建社会、组织和职业特征的测量，但这些AI衍生度量通常被视为响应预测，缺少对其能否在情境模型中恢复个体层和群体层效应的系统性评估框架。

Method: 提出AICOME框架，核心思路是将AI在受访者层构建的量度用于推导群体层聚合值及个体偏离值，从而同时估计组间（between-group）与组内（within-group）关联。使用2022年CFPS数据进行验证，以职业为分组结构，对计算机使用、外语使用、每周工作时长和管理职责四个基准变量进行响应层、模型层、情境层和边界条件四类验证。

Result: AI情境测量在具备丰富受访者和职业特征时能恢复观察调查变量中大部分情境模型信息。每周工作时长是最强验证案例，AI测量复现了CFPS中工作时长与满意度之间显著的组间和组内负关联。边界条件明确：当信息仅限职业和基本人口统计特征时表现下降，多个相关概念同时不可观测时恢复能力更弱。

Conclusion: AICOME对于从丰富数据集恢复少量理论上重要的构念最有价值，而非将AI测量视为响应预测的替代品。

Abstract: Researchers increasingly use artificial intelligence to construct measures of social, organizational, and occupational characteristics that are absent from conventional surveys. We propose AICOME, AI COntextual MEasurement, a framework for evaluating whether AI-derived respondent-level measures can recover individual and group-level effects in contextual models. The key idea is that an AI measure constructed at the respondent level can be used to derive its group-level aggregate and its individual deviation, allowing researchers to estimate both between-group and within-group associations rather than treating AI measurement as response prediction alone.
  We validate the framework using the 2022 China Family Panel Studies (CFPS), where occupations provide the empirical grouping structure and several job-related survey variables provide validation benchmarks. For computer use, foreign-language use, weekly hours, and management responsibilities, we compare survey measures with AI-derived measures in response-level, model-level, contextual, and boundary-condition validations. The results show that AI contextual measurement can recover much of the contextual-model information contained in observed survey variables when rich respondent and job characteristics are available. Weekly hours provides the strongest validation case, with AI-derived measures reproducing the large negative between- and within-occupation associations with satisfaction observed in CFPS. The framework also identifies clear boundary conditions: performance deteriorates when information is restricted to occupation and basic demographics, and recovery is weaker when several related concepts are treated as simultaneously unobserved. The findings suggest that AICOME is most useful for recovering a limited number of theoretically important constructs from rich existing datasets.

</details>


### [375] [Discriminative World Models for Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02885)
*Kelvin Li,Dhruv Pendharkar,Anish Pahilajani,Chuyi Shang,Leon Oks,Leonid Karlinsky,Rogerio Feris,Trevor Darrell,Roei Herzig*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为"预测状态匹配"（predicted-state matching）的训练目标，用于训练网络智能体的世界模型。该方法要求预测表示能够区分真实结果状态与替代动作到达的状态，弥补了传统监督式下一状态预测与下游排序器之间不对齐的问题。实验表明该方法在预测状态匹配基准、PRM式动作排序及端到端任务成功率上均优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的网络智能体世界模型通常通过监督式下一状态预测来训练，生成固定的HTML或AXTree快照表示。然而该目标与下游排序器存在不对齐问题——排序器依赖于预测状态能够在候选动作之间具有判别性，而单纯预测固定表示不保证这一点，导致基于世界模型的测试时动作选择效果受限。

Method: 引入预测状态匹配（predicted-state matching）训练目标：预测的表示必须能区分真实结果状态与替代动作达到的状态。使用从WebArena Go-Browse轨迹导出的分支型网络智能体数据集进行训练，该数据集的每个决策点包含多个替代动作及其结果状态。在测试时利用该世界模型采样候选动作、预测结果状态，并由排序器或过程奖励模型（PRM）进行评分。

Result: 在保留的预测状态匹配基准上，该方法优于采用监督式下一状态预测训练的世界模型；在WebPRMBench上相比仅基于动作的PRM及使用监督式下一状态预测世界模型增强的PRM，提高了PRM风格的动作排序性能；在WebArena-Lite上，测试时动作选择端到端任务成功率得到提升。

Conclusion: 预测状态匹配训练目标能更好地对齐世界模型表示与下游排序需求，使世界模型生成的预测状态更具判别性，从而提升动作排序与端到端任务表现，验证了以判别性为导向的训练方式对网络智能体世界模型的价值。

Abstract: Recent web agents use world models for test-time action selection by sampling candidate actions, predicting the resulting web states, and ranking them with a ranker model or a Process Reward Model (PRM). These world models are typically trained via supervised next-state prediction to generate fixed representations like HTML or AXTree snapshots. However, this objective is misaligned with the downstream ranker, which relies on predicted states being discriminative across candidates to accurately score them. To address this, we introduce predicted-state matching, a training objective where the predicted representation must distinguish the true resulting state from those reached by alternative actions. We train these models using a branching web-agent dataset derived from WebArena Go-Browse trajectories, where every decision point contains multiple alternative actions and their resulting states. Experiments on our held-out predicted-state matching benchmark show that our approach outperforms world models trained with supervised next-state prediction. We further show that our approach improves PRM-style action ranking on WebPRMBench compared with action-only PRMs and PRMs augmented with supervised-next-state world models. Finally, on WebArena-Lite, using our world model for test-time action selection improves end-to-end task success. Our project page is available at: https://dhruvpendharkar.github.io/dwm/.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [376] [Not All Agreement Counts as Corroboration: Provenance-Conserving Multi-View Fusion for Typed Action Admission in Human-Robot Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01662)
*Zekai Jin,Hanrong Zhang,Yihong Tang,Fei Hu,Zhen Dong,Yi Shao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PACT 提出一种将证据可数性作为关系变量的多源融合框架，通过供应的来源分区定义可数单元，仅在单元间累积证据，以区分计算多重性与证据多重性。实验表明其相较单一来源聚合显著降低风险覆盖面积，并在离线人机协作中保持输出不变的同时实现类型化动作准入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对于具身系统，预测一致性本身不足以决定证据是否足以支撑行动，因为重复推理同一观测可以放大一致性但不增加证据。来源局部数值无法揭示输出是否具有可独立计数的来源，因此需要一种能保留来源起源的证据融合机制。

Method: PACT 将证据可数性建模为关系变量，依赖供应的来源分区界定可数单元：在每个单元内保留坐标级共享支持，仅跨单元累积证据；对未满足的释放条件映射为 hold、confirm 或 fallback 三种动作。使用坐标级 meet 作为满足单一保真性与插入非放大性的最大预算，并具备 coarsening 单调性与固定分区稳定性。

Result: 在 48 个场景簇上的 31,200 次评估中，PACT 达到 0.0861 的归一化风险覆盖面积(ncsAURC)。排除构造的对抗一致实验臂后，来源分区聚合相比单一来源聚合将 ncsAURC 降低 0.0557，且在完整来源记录上原生分数更优。在离线人机协作中，八倍相机内重复使 720 个类型化响应/检查点保持不变，相机分组的 PACT 在 60 个回合中准入 57 个参考一致候选中 47 个，无可观察到的参考不一致准入。

Conclusion: PACT 有效分离了计算多重性与证据多重性：一致性仅在来源允许独立累积时才构成佐证。该框架为具身系统的证据计数与动作准入提供了一种来源感知的理论与实用方案。

Abstract: For embodied systems, predictive agreement alone does not determine whether evidence warrants action; evidential origin matters. Repeated inference over one observation can multiply agreement without adding evidence, while source-local values do not reveal whether outputs have separately countable origins. PACT treats evidence countability as a relational variable for provenance-conserving fusion and typed action admission. A supplied provenance partition defines countable units. PACT retains coordinatewise support shared within each unit, accumulates only across units, and maps unmet release conditions to hold, confirm, or fallback. Under the stated assumptions, source-local values cannot identify countability; the coordinatewise meet is the greatest budget satisfying singleton fidelity and insertion non-amplification, with coarsening monotonicity and fixed-partition stability. Across 31,200 evaluations in 48 scene clusters, PACT attains a common-support normalized risk-coverage area (ncsAURC) of 0.0861. Excluding the constructed adversarial-consensus arm, provenance-partition aggregation reduces ncsAURC by 0.0557 relative to singleton aggregation, while the corroboration contrast vanishes. On complete-source records, native scores favor PACT, but a common posterior-peak score narrows its difference from nested Dirichlet and favors product fusion. Reassigning provenance over unchanged predictions moves evidence budgets as predicted. In offline human-robot collaboration, eightfold within-camera duplication leaves 720 typed responses per checkpoint unchanged; camera-grouped PACT admits 47 of 57 Qwen3-VL-32B reference-consistent candidates with no observed reference-inconsistent admission in 60 episodes. PACT separates computational from evidential multiplicity: agreement constitutes corroboration only when provenance permits separate accumulation.

</details>


### [377] [TriSAR: Task Coordination and Collision Avoidance for Aerial Robot Teams in Disaster Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01731)
*Aditya Anil Kapile,Pedro Machado,Isibor Kennedy Ihianle*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过2x2因子设计实验，在基于物理的Gazebo仿真地震灾区场景中评估了五架无人机协调系统TriSAR。结果表明：反应式斥力碰撞避免能显著消除碰撞阈值违规（贪心分配下效果尤为显著），而遗传算法(GA)任务分配仅在斥力禁用时才显示出比贪心分配更优的任务效率优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多无人机灾害响应系统需要协调的任务分配与局部轨迹控制，但这些协调层分别及组合对任务效率和运行安全性的贡献，在受控实验条件下尚未得到充分表征。

Method: 在模拟地震损坏城市环境的基于物理的Gazebo仿真中，以五架无人机协调系统TriSAR为对象，采用2x2因子设计，比较两种任务分配策略（遗传算法与贪心适应度分配），并分别启用或禁用反应式碰撞避免。每种配置在包含五架无人机和八个目标的共同场景下进行30次随机试验评估。

Result: 贪心分配下启用斥力消除了所有碰撞阈值违规（Mann-Whitney U=885, p=4.03x10^-12, r=0.97）；GA分配下同样确认了保护效果（U=675, p=1.26x10^-5, r=0.50）。GA分配在启用斥力时未显示对贪心分配的统计显著优势，但在禁用斥力时在步数、路径长度和能量指标上显著更优（Welch's t检验，|g|介于0.92-1.76）。

Conclusion: 反应式斥力提供了显著且依分配策略而定的安全效益；GA任务分配的额外计算复杂度仅在斥力禁用时才能带来可检测的任务效率优势。

Abstract: Multi-Unmanned Aerial Vehicle (UAV) disaster-response systems require coordinated task assignment and local trajectory control, yet the individual and combined contributions of these coordination layers to mission efficiency and operational safety remain insufficiently characterised under controlled experimental conditions. TriSAR is evaluated as a five-UAV coordination system operating in a physics-based Gazebo simulation of an earthquake-damaged urban environment. A 2 x 2 factorial design compares two task-allocation strategies (Genetic Algorithm and greedy fitness-based allocation) with reactive collision avoidance enabled or disabled. Each of the four configurations was evaluated over 30 stochastic episodes in a common scenario of five UAVs and eight targets. Under greedy allocation, enabling repulsion eliminated recorded collision-threshold violations, confirmed by a Mann-Whitney test (U = 885, p = 4.03 x 10^-12, rank-biserial r = 0.97). Under GA allocation, the same protective effect was confirmed (U = 675, p = 1.26 x 10^-5, rank-biserial r = 0.50). For mission-efficiency metrics, GA-based allocation showed no statistically detectable advantage over greedy allocation when repulsion was enabled, but a significant advantage in steps, path length, and energy when repulsion was disabled (Welch's t-tests, |g| between 0.92 and 1.76). These results show that reactive repulsion provides a substantial, allocation-dependent safety benefit, while the additional computational complexity of GA-based task allocation yields a detectable mission-efficiency benefit only when repulsion is disabled.

</details>


### [378] [Designing Versatile Samples for Learned Trajectory Scoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01799)
*Yaguang Li,Jiaru Zhang,Chuheng Wei,Can Cui,Ziran Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对端到端驾驶策略中轨迹评分器训练监督不足的问题，设计了一种通过沿横向（朝向可驾驶边界）和纵向（朝向引导车辆）扰动日志人类轨迹来生成更丰富正负样本的训练数据集，从而为评分器提供更具信息量的监督信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端驾驶策略通常生成候选轨迹池并由评分器选择，但强规划器（planner）生成的轨迹往往集中在安全模式附近，导致评分器在决策边界附近缺乏有效监督，限制其判别能力。

Method: 构建两个扰动生成器：横向扰动使人类轨迹朝向可驾驶边界偏移，纵向扰动使轨迹朝向引导车辆方向偏移，由此生成比基础规划器候选池更具信息量的正负样本。随后将基于Transformer的评分器附加到两个冻结的生成式规划器（DiffusionDrive和MeanFuser）上，并使用NAVSIM navtrain数据集进行训练。

Result: 在ResNet-34特征提取下，DiffusionDrive达到90.1 EPDMS，MeanFuser达到90.4 EPDMS，分别比基线提升0.4和0.3 EPDMS。

Conclusion: 通过针对决策边界设计的扰动数据生成方式，可以有效提升轨迹评分器的性能，验证了该训练数据设计方法的有效性。

Abstract: Many current end-to-end driving policies emit a pool of candidate trajectories and select one, which makes selection a separable component: a scorer can be retrained while the planner, its backbone, and its trajectory generator all stay frozen. However, many strong planners concentrate their proposals around safe mode, providing limited supervision near decision boundaries. In this work, we design a training dataset that provides more informative supervision for the scorer. In particular, we construct two generators that perturb the logged human trajectory along the two axes a vehicle can be displaced: laterally toward the drivable boundary and longitudinally toward a leading vehicle. The designed dataset produces more informative positive and negative samples than the base planner's proposal pool. We attach a transformer-based scorer to two frozen generative planners, DiffusionDrive and MeanFuser, and train it on the NAVSIM navtrain dataset. The results of the experiments show that we achieve 90.1 EPDMS on DiffusionDrive and 90.4 EPDMS on MeanFuser when using ResNet-34, with 0.4 and 0.3 EPDMS respectively, from the designed training dataset.

</details>


### [379] [One Demonstration, Many Objects: Generalizing Manipulation via Local Contact Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01938)
*Satvik Sharma,Samrat Sahoo,Huang Huang,Fei-Fei Li Jiajun Wu,Dorsa Sadigh,Jeannette Bohg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DemoMimic提出一种基于接触点局部几何的灵巧操控策略，通过接触中心奖励提升sim-to-real一致性，在16个物体、4个任务和2种机器人手上实现平均71%的真实世界成功率，且表现出了最强的迁移能力和最小的sim-to-real性能衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多指机器人手的灵巧操控虽然前景广阔，但大规模机器人手部数据难以采集；虽然从人类演示学习中可扩展地学习物体交互和接触策略先验，但现有的sim-to-real RL方法要么缺乏显式鼓励精确接触的奖励导致真实世界表现弱，要么对未见物体实例泛化能力差。

Method: 提出DemoMimic策略，通过聚焦于接触点局部的物体几何来进行操控。其接触中心奖励显式激励精确接触并改善sim-to-real一致性，使单一真实世界策略能够在保持局部接触结构的情况下跨不同形状、尺度、质量、摩擦力的物体进行迁移。

Result: 在真实世界消融实验中，DemoMimic在16个物体、4个任务和2种机器人手实现上取得了71%的平均成功率，相比基线方法具有最小的sim-to-real性能下降。

Conclusion: 聚焦接触点局部几何并设计接触中心奖励能显著提升灵巧操控策略的sim-to-real迁移能力与跨物体泛化性，验证了该方法的有效性和通用性。

Abstract: Dexterous manipulation with multi-fingered robot hands promises human-level dexterity, but collecting large-scale dexterous robot hand data remains difficult. Learning from human demonstrations has emerged as a scalable alternative to robot teleoperation, providing strong priors on object interaction and contact strategies. Recent sim-to-real RL methods incorporate such priors, but often (i) omit rewards that explicitly incentivize precise contact, yielding weak real-world performance, and/or (ii) generalize poorly to unseen object instances. We propose DemoMimic (Dexterous Motion Mimic), a policy that manipulates objects by focusing on their geometry local to the contact points. Its contact-centric rewards encourage precise contact and improve sim-to-real consistency, yielding a single real-world policy that transfers across objects of varying shape, scale, mass, and friction wherever local contact structure is preserved. Real-world ablations show that DemoMimic achieves 71% success across 16 objects, four tasks, and two robot-hand embodiments, with the smallest sim-to-real drop compared to baselines.

</details>


### [380] [MACAW: Reliable And Efficient Surgical Debridement Using Monocular Adaptive Compact Attention Windows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01961)
*Ziyang Chen,Shutong Jin,Preethi Satish,Sareena Mann,Cael Magner,Danyal Fer,Omid Mohareri,Gary Guthart,Ken Goldberg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种增强外科手术灵巧性的系统，用于清创术（切除病变或坏死组织碎片）。系统利用视觉伺服将线缆驱动夹爪在图像平面内与目标位置对齐，并创新性地提出MACAW（单目自适应紧凑注意窗口）方法来实现深度控制。在达芬奇研究套件（dVRK）上进行的100次物理试验中，相机帧伺服将平均夹爪位置偏移从37像素降至5像素以内（仅需4个优化步骤，平均耗时0.39秒）。MACAW显著优于程序和学习的视觉语言动作（VLA）基线，在每碎片11秒的情况下达到93%的成功率，吞吐量为每小时304个碎片。扩展至双臂清创设置后仍保持92%成功率、平均每碎片7秒，吞吐量提升至每小时473个碎片。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 增强外科医生的灵巧性可以将其从繁琐的子任务中解放出来。清创术因空间感知不精确和线缆驱动的不确定性而具有挑战性，亟需自动化系统来提升手术操作精度与效率。

Method: 开发增强灵巧系统，包含两个核心环节：(1) 利用视觉伺服在图像平面内将线缆驱动夹爪与目标位置对齐；(2) 提出MACAW（单目自适应紧凑注意窗口）用于深度控制，通过单目图像中的自适应紧凑注意窗口机制实现精准的深度估计与抓取。

Result: 在dVRK上100次物理试验中，视觉伺服将位置偏移从37像素降至5像素以内，仅需4个优化步骤、平均0.39秒。MACAW成功率93%（每碎片11秒，304碎片/小时），显著超过程序和VLA基线。双臂设置下维持92%成功率、每碎片7秒，吞吐量达473碎片/小时。

Conclusion: 所提出的增强灵巧系统结合视觉伺服与MACAW深度控制，能够高效、精准地完成手术清创，并在双臂扩展中进一步提升吞吐量，展示了其在实际手术场景中解放外科医生双手的潜力。

Abstract: Augmenting the dexterity of human surgeons has the potential to free them from tedious subtasks. We consider debridement (removal of diseased or dead tissue fragments), which is challenging due to imprecision in spatial perception and cable actuation. We develop an augmented dexterity system for surgical debridement that uses visual servoing to align the cable-driven gripper with the target position in the image plane, and then introduces a novel approach to depth control, MACAW: Monocular Adaptive Compact Attention Windows. Across 100 physical trials using the da Vinci Research Kit (dVRK) robot, camera-frame servoing reduced average gripper position offset from 37 to fewer than 5 pixels within 4 optimization steps, taking an average of only 0.39s. MACAW significantly outperforms procedural and learned VLA baselines, achieving a 93% success rate at 11 seconds per fragment, yielding a throughput of 304 fragments per hour. Extending MACAW to a bimanual debridement setup maintains a 92% success rate at an average of 7 seconds per fragment, increasing the throughput to 473 fragments per hour.

</details>


### [381] [Design and Validation of a Lightweight, Low-Profile Powered Knee Prosthesis with Quasi-Direct Drive Actuation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02003)
*Ross J. Cortino,Ryan Posh,Emily G. Keller,Robert D. Gregg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出并验证了一款新型轻量化（2.6 kg）、低外形（24.5 cm）的准直驱（QDD）膝关节假肢。通过优化18:1两级传动机构并进行热与结构有限元分析，该设备实现了145 Nm的峰值扭矩、1 Nm的低反向驱动扭矩及精确的位置/力矩控制。三名不同K级的膝上截肢参与者在平地行走和坐站转换中均表现出仿生运动学与接近健全人水平的峰值膝伸扭矩，验证了QDD假肢在商业转化中的临床可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的膝上假肢为被动或高度齿轮传动类型，难以提供良好的扭矩控制与反向驱动性；而已有QDD膝关节假肢原型过于沉重笨重，阻碍了临床推广。因此需要在保证性能的同时大幅减小设备质量与体积，推动QDD技术的临床转化。

Method: 作者设计了一款2.6 kg、24.5 cm高的QDD膝关节假肢，采用优化后的18:1两级传动系统，并结合热力学与结构有限元分析以在减重的同时保障输出性能。随后通过台架实验验证其高输出扭矩、低反向驱动扭矩（1 Nm）及精确的位置/力矩控制；并招募三名不同K级的膝上截肢受试者进行平地行走与坐站转换的临床验证。

Result: 设备峰值扭矩达145 Nm，反向驱动扭矩低至1 Nm，在质量、构建高度、峰值扭矩及声学噪声方面达到或优于领先商业动力膝关节。三名受试者在平地行走和坐站转换中展现出仿生运动学，峰值膝伸扭矩处于健全人参考范围的一个标准差以内。

Conclusion: 本研究通过轻量化设计解决了QDD膝关节假肢此前体积与重量过大的瓶颈，在性能上达到或超越商业基准，确立了新兴QDD动力膝关节假肢的临床可行性，有望为使用者带来更优的动态活动表现。

Abstract: Fully-powered knee prostheses, unlike traditional passive knees, can perform controlled positive work, reducing the need for compensatory behaviors by users during energy-intensive activities. While quasi-direct drive (QDD) actuators provide superior torque control, backdrivability, and acoustic noise properties compared to traditional highly-geared actuators, prior QDD prototypes have been too heavy and bulky for commercial translation. In this work, we present the design and validation of a new lightweight (2.6 kg) and low-profile (24.5 cm tip-to-tip build height) QDD knee prosthesis. By optimizing an 18 to 1 two-stage transmission alongside thermal and structural finite-element analyses, we significantly reduce device mass while enabling a peak torque of 145 Nm. Through benchtop tests, we validate the device's high output torque, low backdrive torque (1 Nm), and its precision position and torque control capabilities. We also demonstrate biomimetic kinematics and peak knee extension torques (within one standard deviation of able-bodied references) during both level-ground walking and sit-stand transitions performed by three participants with transfemoral amputation and varying K-levels. By meeting or improving upon the mass, build height, peak torque, and acoustic noise of a leading commercial powered knee, this work establishes the clinical viability of emerging QDD prostheses that promise improved dynamic performance for their users.

</details>


### [382] [Real-Time Dynamics-Based Torque-Sampling MPPI for Compliant and Force Aware Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02020)
*Euncheol Im,Taehyun Kim,Yonghwan Oh,Myotaeg Lim,Yisoo Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于MPPI的任务空间控制框架，通过显式求解刚体动力学并在实时MPC中强制执行安全约束，实现7自由度机械臂在非结构化环境中的精确运动和力控制。利用扭矩采样架构结合GPU并行化，达到超过166 Hz的求解更新率和0.18秒的预测时域，并通过真实世界实验验证其柔顺与力感知行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MPC方法难以在实时性约束下有效处理机械臂的非线性刚体动力学，尤其是在非结构化环境中需要同时实现精确运动控制、力控制和安全约束以保证物理交互的柔顺性与安全性。

Method: 所提框架采用Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI)方法解决实时MPC中的非线性动力学问题，并在任务空间中显式求解刚体动力学且强制执行安全约束；开发了基于扭矩采样的控制架构，以利用GPU并行化高效处理采样计算。

Result: 框架实现了超过166 Hz的求解更新率，预测时域为0.18秒；在7自由度机械臂上的真实世界实验中验证了所提方法的性能。

Conclusion: 通过MPPI与GPU并行扭矩采样的结合，所提框架能够实时处理非线性动力学并实现安全、柔顺的力感知交互行为，为机械臂在非结构化环境中的物理交互提供了一种有效而实用的控制方案。

Abstract: This study proposes a novel Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI)-based task-space control framework. The proposed framework explicitly solves rigid-body dynamics within a real-time MPC formulation and enforces safety constraints, enabling accurate motion and force control that yields compliant behaviors for safe and effective physical interaction of robotic manipulators in unstructured environments. By leveraging MPPI, the proposed framework efficiently handles nonlinear dynamics that are difficult to solve with conventional MPC approaches in real-time. Furthermore, we develop a torque-sampling-based control architecture that enables efficient exploitation of GPU-based parallelization, resulting in effective compliant and force-aware behaviors. As a result, the proposed framework achieves a solver update rate of over 166 Hz with a 0.18 s prediction horizon, and its performance is validated through real-world experiments on a 7-DoF manipulator.

</details>


### [383] [Modeling What Changes: Sparse, Residual World Models for Object-Centric Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02046)
*Param Thakkar,Parsika Paresh Shah,Manisha Sushant Gote*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种稀疏/残差世界模型：通过逐对象变化门控（change gate）+残差增量头（residual delta head）仅预测场景中发生变化的物体，而非整体重建下一状态。在MuJoCo桌面推物基准（3-8个物体）上，相比稠密MLP基线，该模型在参数量减少8.6-11.1倍的同时预测精度提升2.5-4.6倍，变化检测F1达0.80-0.87（稠密基线退化为0），跨物体数量零重训迁移（F1保留99.4%），仅用四分之一数据即可达到约90%的全量数据精度；在自回归推理中误差累积显著更小，且在采样规划器中经特征化后能开始规划，而稠密模型在所有随机种子下均为零成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有整体式（monolithic）世界模型在每一步都预测完整下一状态，将建模能力浪费在重复预测场景中静态的大多数物体上，且会向静态部分注入预测误差。作者质疑：显式建模变化（稀疏/残差式的逐物体变化门控+残差增量）是否是物理预测与控制中更有效且更具可解释性的归纳偏置。

Method: 提出稀疏/残差世界模型架构：每个物体配备一个变化门（change gate），决定该物体是否发生变化；对被门控标记为变化的物体，仅用残差增量头（residual delta head）预测其状态偏移，未变化物体保持上一状态，从而避免对整个场景进行稠密重建。在MuJoCo桌面推物从3到8个物体的扩展场景中与稠密MLP基线对比测试，并进一步在采样规划器（sampling-based planner）内验证规划可行性（含预测式模型、特征化训练及真实仿真器oracle作为对照）。

Result: （1）精度：稀疏/残差模型在预测下一状态姿态上比稠密MLP精确2.5-4.6倍，而参数量仅为后者的8.6-11.1倍分之一；（2）变化检测F1达0.80-0.87，稠密基线该指标退化不可用；（3）跨物体数量零重训迁移，F1保留99.4%；（4）仅用四分之一数据可达到全量数据约90%的精度；（5）自回归推演中误差累积远小于稠密模型，后者漂移而前者贴近无运动底线；（6）规划实验中：纯预测式模型在采样规划器中失败（但真实模拟器oracle用同一规划器可解任务，证明规划器本身健全）；对稀疏模型做特征化并按规划器访问的状态训练后，规划成功率达0.23±0.06（三个种子），而稠密模型在所有种子上均为零。

Conclusion: 建模"什么在变化"而非"重新预测整个世界"，是一种简单有效的面向物体中心（object-centric）物理AI的归纳偏置。稀疏残差世界模型在精度、参数效率、数据效率、可迁移性、误差累积与下游规划能力上全面优于稠密整体式基线，为学习形的物理预测与控制提供了一条更具前景的路径。

Abstract: Monolithic world models predict the entire next state at every step, spending capacity re-predicting the static majority of a scene and injecting error into it. We ask whether explicitly modeling change (a per-object change gate plus a residual delta head that perturbs only the objects the gate flags) is a more effective and interpretable bias for physical prediction and control. On a MuJoCo tabletop pushing benchmark scaling from 3 to 8 objects, the sparse/residual model predicts next-state poses 2.5 to 4.6 times more accurately than a dense multilayer perceptron at 8.6 to 11.1 times fewer parameters, sustains change-detection F1 of 0.80 to 0.87 where the dense baseline is degenerate, transfers across object counts with zero retraining (99.4 percent F1 retention), and reaches about 90 percent of its full-data accuracy with a quarter of the data. In autoregressive rollout it compounds far less error, hugging the no-motion floor while the dense model drifts. Finally, inside a sampling-based planner, prediction-only models fail (though a true-simulator oracle solves the task with the identical planner, confirming the planner is sound), but once featurized and trained for the states a planner visits, the sparse model begins to plan (0.23 plus or minus 0.06 success over three seeds) while the dense monolith stays at zero at every seed. Modeling what changes, rather than re-predicting the whole world, is a simple, effective bias for object-centric physical AI; code, data generators, and all checkpoints will be released upon publication.

</details>


### [384] [Koopman-Based Robust Model Predictive Control for Nonlinear Systems with Stochastic Intermittent Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02079)
*Guanhua Liu,Tong Wu,Lixian Zhang,Weifeng Du,Minghao Han*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Koopman的随机模型预测控制（MPC）框架，用于处理间歇性状态测量下的约束非线性系统控制问题，通过马尔可夫跳跃误差建模、概率截断软约束和精确惩罚机制，在可验证条件下证明了闭环系统的均方最终有界性与递归可行性，并在视觉伺服跟踪任务上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 间歇性状态测量对约束非线性系统的MPC构成根本性挑战：反馈中断期间预测不确定性不断增长，而测量触发的重置会破坏名义状态传播，可能危及闭环稳定性与递归可行性。现有方法难以同时高效处理预测不确定性增长和状态重置引起的约束违例问题。

Method: 1) 使用Lipschitz约束的深度Koopman模型作为线性潜在预测器，实现计算高效的在线优化；2) 将间歇测量建模为两模式离散时间马尔可夫链，构建统一的马尔可夫跳跃误差模型统一描述开环传播和测量触发重置；3) 在数值可验证的充分条件下推导预测误差的均方最终有界性和显式均匀二阶矩界；4) 构造无分布假设的概率误差半径用于按置信度截断依赖丢包的约束收紧；5) 采用精确惩罚软约束机制处理重置引起的跳变和长时间丢包。

Result: 在终端兼容性和有界扰动条件下，建立了递归可行性和闭环调节误差的均方最终有界性。数值仿真在视觉伺服跟踪任务上验证了理论结果，展示了在随机测量不可用条件下的有效跟踪性能。

Conclusion: 该框架将Koopman算子的线性化优势与随机MPC的鲁棒性设计相结合，为间歇测量条件下约束非线性系统的稳定控制提供了理论保障和可行方案；通过概率截断软约束有效权衡了标称性能与约束鲁棒性。

Abstract: Intermittent state measurements pose fundamental challenges to model predictive control of constrained nonlinear systems because prediction uncertainty grows during feedback outages and measurement-triggered resets disrupt nominal state propagation, potentially compromising closed-loop stability and recursive feasibility. This paper develops a Koopman-based stochastic MPC framework with probabilistically truncated soft constraints. Specifically, a Lipschitz-constrained deep Koopman model provides a linear latent predictor, enabling computationally efficient online optimization. The intermittent measurement process is modeled as a two-mode discrete-time Markov chain, yielding a unified Markov jump error model for open-loop propagation and measurement-triggered resets. Under numerically verifiable sufficient conditions, the prediction error is shown to be mean-square ultimately bounded, and an explicit uniform second-moment bound is obtained. A distribution-free probabilistic error radius is then constructed for a prescribed confidence level and used to truncate dropout-dependent constraint tightening. An exact-penalty soft-constraint mechanism accommodates reset-induced jumps and prolonged dropouts. Under the stated terminal compatibility and bounded-disturbance conditions, recursive feasibility and mean-square ultimate boundedness of the closed-loop regulation error are established. Numerical simulations on a visual-servoing tracking task corroborate these theoretical results and demonstrate effective tracking under stochastic measurement unavailability.

</details>


### [385] [Unified Motion Retargeting for Humanoids with Learned Point Cloud Correspondence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02134)
*Hanyang Cao,Yuetong Fang,Taesoo Kwon,Runyi Yu,Ji Ma,Jing Tan,Yangchen Zhou,Baoze Du,Yi Gu,Yukang Gao,Ruoli Dai,Lei Han,Renjing Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出统一运动重定向框架UMR，利用外表面点云作为人与机器人之间的统一接口，学习密集点云对应关系，无需手工设计关键点或肢体配对，实现从异构动作源到多种机器人形态的高保真运动重定向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人形机器人运动重定向方法依赖手工设计的稀疏关键点或肢体配对来建立人-机对应关系，导致重定向质量受限于人工语义设计，难以扩展到不同动作来源和机器人形态，且仅能提供稀疏指导，无法精确再现细节姿态和交互。

Method: UMR将人体外部点云作为人与机器人之间的统一接口，学习密集点云对应关系，将重定向与源骨骼语义及机器人特定拓扑解耦；利用学到的密集对应关系作为几何锚点，执行约束点云匹配优化，实现表面级姿态对齐以及交互接触信息的直接迁移。

Result: 实验表明，UMR在异构动作来源、不同机器人形态以及从运动到交互的下游场景中均能统一重定向，相比现有最先进方法实现了更高的运动保真度和物理合理性。

Conclusion: UMR为大规模人体运动数据转化为人形机器人训练数据提供了可扩展的基础框架，缓解了人工设计映射的瓶颈。

Abstract: Humanoid learning increasingly relies on transforming vast and diverse human motion data into high-quality robot reference trajectories. However, retargeting human motion to humanoid robots is challenging due to substantial differences in morphology, degrees of freedom, joint ranges, and kinematic constraints between humans and robots. Existing retargeting methods typically address these differences by defining human-robot correspondence through hand-crafted sparse keypoints or body-part pairs. As a result, retargeting quality depends heavily on manual semantic design, limiting scalability across motion sources and robot morphologies and providing only sparse guidance for reproducing detailed poses and interactions. In this paper, we present Unified Motion Retargeting (UMR), a framework that learns dense point cloud correspondence without requiring manually designed human-robot mappings. By treating exterior point clouds as a unified interface between human motion and humanoid robots, UMR decouples retargeting from source-specific skeletal semantics and robot-specific topology. The learned dense correspondence provides fine-grained geometric anchors for constrained point cloud matching optimization, enabling surface-level pose alignment and direct transfer of interaction contacts. Experiments demonstrate that UMR unifies retargeting across heterogeneous motion sources, robot embodiments, and downstream scenarios ranging from locomotion to interaction, while achieving higher motion fidelity and plausibility than state-of-the-art methods. UMR therefore provides a scalable foundation for transforming large-scale human motion references into robot-ready training data.

</details>


### [386] [Hardware-Accelerated Instance Segmentation for Resource-Constrained Space Robotics with Criticality Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02219)
*Siddhant Shete,Hilmi Dogu Kücüker,Udo Frese,Frank Kirchner*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向资源受限月球机器人的实时实例分割框架，通过AVIS（基于激活方差的无标签量化校准）和YOLO模型在DPU上的部署优化，联合解决了极端低光、有限算力和辐射容错三大约束。实验表明在月球微巡视器上实现了309ms推理延迟、5.7W功耗，恢复了69.8%的量化精度损失，并通过定向缓解将全局关键性降低31.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主月球任务面临三重耦合约束：极端低光条件下的感知能力、机载计算资源极为有限、以及辐射引发的硬件故障可能静默破坏推理结果。现有方案未能同时考虑量化校准与系统级故障暴露问题，缺乏面向真实部署约束的端到端解决方案。

Method: (1) 提出Activation Variance Informative Sampling (AVIS)，一种无标签校准策略，基于激活方差统计确定性选择校准样本；(2) 在DPU上部署基于YOLO的分割模型，通过架构修改减少CPU回退路径并实现静态编译执行，在低光条件下获得有界延迟；(3) 引入软件级关键性分析，估计故障暴露程度并指导辐射约束场景下的缓解策略。

Result: 在月球微巡视器平台上，AVIS结合偏置校正恢复了69.8%的量化诱导精度损失，推理延迟为309ms，功耗为5.7W；定向缓解措施将全局关键性降低31.7%。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了在空间部署约束下实现可靠、安全的AI感知框架的集成方法，为自治月球任务中的感知系统设计提供了可参考的蓝图。

Abstract: Autonomous lunar missions require real-time per- ception under three coupled constraints: extreme low-light conditions, limited onboard compute, and radiation-induced hardware faults that can silently corrupt inference. We present a deployment-oriented instance segmentation framework for resource-constrained lunar robotics that jointly addresses quan- tization calibration and system-level fault exposure under strict compute constraints. First, we introduce Activation Variance Informative Sampling (AVIS), a label-free calibration strategy that deterministically selects calibration samples based on activation variance statistics. Second, we deploy a YOLO-based segmentation model on a Deep Learning Processor Unit (DPU) with architectural modifications that reduce CPU fallback paths and enable statically compiled execution with bounded latency in low-lighting conditions. We further introduce a software-level criticality analysis to estimate fault exposure and guide mitigation under radiation-constrained operation. On a lunar micro-rover platform, AVIS with bias correction recovers 69.8% of quantization-induced accuracy loss while achieving 309 ms inference latency and 5.7 W power consumption. Targeted mitigation reduces global criticality by 31.7%. The results demonstrate an integrated approach and a blueprint for a reliable and safe AI perception framework under space deployment constraints.

</details>


### [387] [Towards Effective Physical Reservoir Computing with a Pneumatic Soft Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02157)
*Jeevan Hebbal Manjunath,Jun Wang,Suyi Li,Wenlong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过气动软臂五囊传感柱实验，系统研究了囊泡互连拓扑、刚度和传感器数量对弯曲角度估计性能的影响，提出三条PRC设计准则：独立密封囊比共享歧管保留更丰富的可观测状态；提高基线压力会增加响应冗余并加剧估计误差（耦合拓扑中尤甚）；密封拓扑中两个策略性传感器即可恢复大部分收益，三个可捕获几乎所有收益，更多传感器几乎无额外价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理储层计算（PRC）利用物理动力学系统作为计算资源，用于状态估计与控制等任务，但缺乏关于如何更有效设计物理储层的正式设计规则研究。

Method: 使用带五囊传感柱的气动软臂，在36组匹配试验中系统改变囊泡互连拓扑、软臂刚度和传感器数量，以固定脊回归估计器和0.2s压力历史为基准，评估各设计在同时刻弯曲角度估计任务上的性能。

Result: 得出三条设计准则：(1)独立密封囊泡比共享歧管保留更丰富的可观测状态；(2)提高传感柱基线压力使囊泡响应更冗余，在耦合拓扑中显著增加估计误差；(3)密封拓扑中两个策略性传感器已获得大部分性能，三个几乎饱和，更多传感器无实质提升。

Conclusion: 拓扑、刚度和传感器数量应协同设计以实现软体机器人状态的精确PRC；更强的激励无法弥补不良设计选择所移除的状态多样性。

Abstract: Physical reservoir computing (PRC) refers to the use of a physical dynamical system as a computational resource for tasks such as state estimation and control, but there has been a lack of formal study of design rules towards more effective design of such physical reservoirs. Using a pneumatic soft arm with a five-pouch sensing column, this work studies how the pouch interconnection topology, robot stiffness, and the number of instrumented sensors affect bending-angle estimation performance. Across 36 matched trials spanning waveform, baseline pressure of the sensing column, and actuation range, all designs are evaluated under the same-time bending-angle estimation benchmark using 0.2 s of pressure history and a fixed ridge estimator. Our analysis of the experimental results leads to three design guidelines. First, independently sealed pouches preserve a much richer observable state than a shared manifold. Second, increasing the baseline pressure of the sensing column makes the pouch responses more redundant and increases estimation error most strongly in the coupled topology. Third, in the sealed topology, two strategically placed sensors already recover most of the attainable benefit, three capture essentially all of it, and additional sensors provide little or no additional value. In summary, the results suggest that topology, stiffness, and number of instrumented sensors should be co-designed for accurate PRC of soft robot states; stronger excitation alone cannot recover the diversity that poor design choices have already removed.

</details>


### [388] [FOCUS: Foot Observation Confidence for Robust Humanoid Proprioceptive Odometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02222)
*Kaixin Feng,Angsong Li,Shaopeng Zhang,Enyu Li,Peiwen Lin,Chuang Wang,You Li,Haiyu Lan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出FOCUS框架，通过学习连续的前向运动学（FK）可靠性权重替代传统的二值接触判定，用于改进腿式机器人本体感知里程计。该网络仅依赖IMU和关节运动学测量，通过非标注的仿真信号自动训练，并用可靠性权重平滑地融合FK速度观测与IMU传播的机体速度。实验表明FOCUS在仿真和真实平台上均大幅降低了绝对轨迹误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有接触辅助的腿式里程计依赖二值接触判定来决定是否信任整只脚的FK测量，但动态运动（如部分支撑、脚趾拖行和脚滑）中接触并不等同于FK可靠，二值接触决策会在长轨迹中累积显著漂移。因此需要一种更细粒度、连续的可靠性度量来替代粗糙的硬切换。

Method: FOCUS网络预测每只脚的连续FK可靠性权重而非二值接触。权重用于将FK速度观测与IMU传播的机体速度进行混合，并自适应调整扩展卡尔曼滤波（EKF）的观测协方差，实现平滑的可靠性感知融合。网络通过FK加权的速度一致性损失和轻量级仿真接触正则化，从自动生成的仿真信号中训练，无需人工标注连续FK可靠性标签。部署模型仅依赖IMU和关节运动学测量。

Result: 在仿真行走情景中ATE降低83.7%；保持仿真动态运动的运动尺度与频谱能量保真度；在19段真实行走数据中ATE降低70.8%；在四种真实动态运动例程中平均ATE降低42.7%。

Conclusion: FOCUS证明连续的FK可靠性估计比二值接触判定更能有效地提升腿式里程计精度，尤其在动态运动和长轨迹场景下优势明显，且其仅依赖IMU和关节测量，适用于扭矩传感不可靠的硬件平台。

Abstract: Foot forward kinematics (FK) is widely used to improve proprioceptive legged odometry by providing reliable velocity constraints during foot support. Existing contact-aided estimators generally rely on binary contact decisions to determine whether the FK measurements of an entire foot should be trusted.
